### [1] [Modern analog computing for solving differential and matrix equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13179)
*Zhong Sun,Piergiulio Mannocci,Manuel Le Gallo,Abu Sebastian*

Main category: cs.ET

TL;DR: 本文综述了现代模拟计算的发展，聚焦三大核心计算原语（微分方程求解、矩阵方程求解、矩阵向量乘法），分析了基于分立元件、集成电路和阻变存储器等硬件实现，重点讨论了阻变存储器阵列的优势，并探讨了精度、可扩展性、存内计算关系及计算复杂度等挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据密集型应用（如AI和科学计算）对计算能力的需求日益增长，使模拟计算重新引起关注。本文旨在对现代模拟计算的硬件实现、核心计算原语及应用进展进行统一综述，厘清其优势、挑战与发展方向。

Method: 首先识别三大计算原语（微分方程、矩阵方程、矩阵向量乘法）及其内在联系；然后分类调研CMOS模拟电路和阻变存储器等硬件实现；最后分析精度、可扩展性、存内计算及计算复杂度等关键问题。

Result: 阻变存储器阵列因实现效率高而成为最具前景的模拟计算硬件平台；现代模拟计算已能有效求解微分方程和矩阵方程；但精度和可扩展性仍是主要瓶颈。

Conclusion: 现代模拟计算是下一代计算前沿的重要推动力，尽管面临挑战，但在特定计算任务中具有独特优势，值得深入研究。

Abstract: In recent years, driven by the computational demands of data-intensive applications such as artificial intelligence and scientific computing, analog computing has gained renewed interest. Given the diversity of computational tasks and recent advancements in analog CMOS circuits and resistive memory technologies, we refer to the evolving landscape as modern analog computing. In this context, we identify three core computational primitives: solving differential equations, solving matrix equations, and performing matrix-vector multiplications, and we explore the connections among them. We also examine various hardware implementations of these analog computing operators, including those built with discrete components, integrated circuits, and resistive memory devices. Among these, resistive memory arrays emerge as particularly promising due to their implementation efficiency. The paper then surveys recent progress in leveraging modern analog computing to solve differential and matrix equations using both advanced analog CMOS circuits and resistive memory arrays. Finally, we discuss the applications of these circuits, the precision and scalability issues and their potential solutions, the relationship with in-memory computing, and the unique computational complexity of analog computing. This paper provides a unified perspective on analog computing, highlighting its strengths, current developments, and challenges, and positioning it as a pivotal enabler of next-generation computational frontiers.

</details>

### [2] [Learning to Assist: Collaborative VLAs for Implicit Human-Robot Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12475)
*Leo Xu,Letian Li,Alex Cuellar,Michael Hagenow*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究了端到端训练的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型在人类-机器人协作（HRC）中的应用，发现了动作分块策略在隐式协作中的"演示动作泄漏"故障模式（即动作块跨越潜在任务转换导致过早辅助行为），并提出了一种推理时引导方法缓解该问题。通过16人参与的长周期协作组装任务用户研究证明，该方法在保持策略性能的同时允许更长执行周期，实现了更快协作和更少失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有HRC系统多依赖手工设计的流水线，可扩展性和灵活性有限，需要探索端到端学习范式的协作能力及其实际部署的关键因素。

Method: 1) 评估两种最先进的VLA模型在隐式HRC中的表现；2) 识别出动作分块策略中"演示动作泄漏"故障模式及其与执行周期的关系；3) 提出推理时引导方法缓解过早辅助行为。

Result: 长执行周期会加剧动作泄漏问题；提出的引导方法能有效缓解过早辅助行为；16人用户研究表明，引导方法支持更长执行周期，同时减少过早辅助，带来更快协作和更少失败。

Conclusion: 端到端学习的VLA模型可以支持协作操作，但需要针对动作分块策略在隐式协作中的固有问题进行改进；推理时引导是一种有效的轻量级解决方案。

Abstract: Human-robot collaboration (HRC) combines the complementary strengths of humans and robots to improve task efficiency. However, many existing collaborative systems rely on hand-engineered pipelines, limiting their scalability and flexibility for new tasks. In this work, we show that models trained end-to-end with imitation learning, specifically vision-language-action (VLA) models, can support collaborative manipulation, and characterize the key factors affecting their real-world performance. We evaluate two state-of-the-art models and identify a failure mode of action-chunking policies in implicit HRC, where demonstration action leakage (i.e., action chunks crossing latent task transitions) can cause premature assistive behavior. We find that this issue increases with longer execution horizons and occurs in real-world collaborative VLA systems, such as when a robot attempts to hand over a tool before the person is ready. We propose an inference-time steering method to mitigate these erroneous assistive actions while preserving policy performance. Finally, through a 16-participant user study on a long-horizon collaborative assembly task, we show that steering enables a longer execution horizon while mitigating premature assistance, leading to faster collaboration and fewer failures compared to a shorter-horizon policy.

</details>


### [3] [Action-Effect Memory Pretraining for Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12499)
*Yijing Zhou,Qiwei Liang,Sitong Zhuang,Jiaxi Li,Xianpeng Wang,Boyang Cai,Yunyang Mo,Renjing Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AEM是一个面向机器人操作的Action-Effect Memory预训练框架，通过交织视觉与动作特征并应用掩码建模，从视动历史中学习紧凑的时间表征。采用Mamba编码的最终视觉token作为单向量时间瓶颈，用于解码和下游控制。在仿真和真实场景中，AEM结合Diffusion Policy和Flow Policy均持续提升了操作性能，超越了单帧预训练和直接帧堆叠方法，同时降低了推理延迟和计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人表征预训练方法主要关注单帧视觉编码，忽视了操作任务的时间本质——在部分可观测条件下，当前观测单独往往不足以做出正确决策。因此需要一种能够从视觉-动作历史中学习紧凑时间表征的方法。

Method: AEM将操作建模为动作驱动的交互过程，通过交织视觉和动作特征并应用掩码建模，从缺失的历史信息中恢复内容，学习动作条件下的状态演化。使用Mamba编码的最终视觉token输出作为紧凑的历史表征，形成单向量时间瓶颈，兼顾全局上下文与高效推理。

Result: AEM在仿真和真实场景中均持续提升了操作性能，在干净场景、杂乱随机场景和非马尔可夫任务中均优于基线方法。消融实验表明，历史感知预训练优于单帧预训练和直接帧堆叠，同时降低了推理延迟和计算成本。

Conclusion: AEM验证了面向机器人操作的时间表征预训练的有效性，展示了历史感知建模在部分可观测条件下的优势，为机器人学习提供了一种兼顾性能与效率的紧凑时间表征方案。

Abstract: We present AEM, an Action-Effect Memory pretraining framework for robot manipulation that learns compact temporal representations from vision-action history. Unlike prior robot representation pretraining methods that mainly focus on single-frame visual encoding, AEM targets the temporal nature of manipulation, where the current observation alone is often insufficient under partial observability. AEM models manipulation as an action-driven interaction process by interleaving visual and action features and applying masked modeling to recover missing content from incomplete histories, thereby learning action-conditioned state evolution. The Mamba-encoded output of the final vision token is used as a compact history representation, serving as the global context for decoding and downstream control. This design preserves a single-vector temporal bottleneck while keeping inference efficient. We evaluate AEM with Diffusion Policy and Flow Policy. AEM consistently improves manipulation performance in both simulation and real-world settings, outperforming baselines across clean scenes, cluttered and random scenes, and non-Markovian tasks. Ablation studies further show that history-aware pretraining surpasses single-frame pretraining and direct frame stacking, while reducing inference latency and computational cost.

</details>


### [4] [Bounding Boxes as Goals: Language-Conditioned Grasping via Neuro-Symbolic Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12910)
*Allison Andreyev,Landon Eum,Nestor Tiglao,Romel Gomez*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GRASP是一个基于预训练VLM的轻量级框架，通过神经符号目标状态和边界框检测管线，使机器人无需额外训练即可理解自然语言指令并执行桌面操作任务，在90次真实机器人实验中达到73.3%的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM驱动的机器人任务与运动规划方法计算开销大或需要大量示范训练，缺乏轻量级、零样本的开放词汇操作方案。

Method: 利用预训练VLM将自然语言查询转化为神经符号目标状态，通过边界框检测管线实现物理世界中的语义锚定，无需固定颜色列表或硬编码坐标即可理解抽象空间概念。

Result: 在90次真实机器人实验中，三个难度级别下整体成功率为73.3%，无需任何任务特定训练。

Conclusion: GRASP为开放词汇桌面操作提供了一个计算高效、无需微调的可行框架，展示了将VLM与符号规划结合实现零样本泛化的潜力。

Abstract: For robotics to be effectively integrated into household or industrial environments, machines must adapt to natural-language prompts in real time. Although Vision-Language Models (VLMs) have enabled zero-shot generalization in robot task and motion planning (TAMP), current state-of-the-art approaches often remain computationally "heavyweight" or require extensive training on thousands of demonstrations. We present GRASP (Grounded Reasoning and Symbolic Planning), a framework designed as a step toward open-vocabulary tabletop manipulation. Our approach leverages a pretrained VLM to translate natural-language queries into neuro-symbolic goal states, grounded in the physical world via a bounding-box detection pipeline. Unlike methods that rely on fixed color lists or hard-coded coordinates, GRASP enables robots to interpret abstract spatial concepts such as "top shelf" and execute tasks without additional fine-tuning. We achieve 73.3% overall success across 90 real-robot trials at three difficulty levels, requiring no task-specific training.

</details>


### [5] [Foresight: Iterative Reasoning About Clues that Matter for Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12550)
*Arthur Zhang,Carl Qi,Donne Su,Xiangyun Meng,Amy Zhang,Joydeep Biswas*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Foresight框架，利用微调后的视觉语言模型在测试时交替进行运动规划提案与批评，通过人类反馈强化学习对齐行为偏好，在无地图开放世界导航中相比基线方法任务成功率提升37%，人工干预减少52%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有开放世界无地图导航方法受限于依赖已知导航因子和封闭类别集合，或在运动规划前识别线索而遗漏与规划相关的线索，无法处理稀疏语言指令中的未充分指定目标和环境线索推断问题。

Method: 提出Foresight测试时框架，使用微调后的VLM交替生成图像空间运动计划并对计划进行批评，后续计划基于先前批评进行迭代优化；通过人类反馈学习奖励模型，利用强化学习对VLM进行后训练，使其在计划-批评循环中与开放集行为偏好对齐。

Result: 在离线评估和6个真实世界环境中，相比最先进的测试时推理和基础模型基线，Foresight将平均任务成功率提升37%，每次任务的人工干预减少52%，并在Jetson AGX Orin上实时运行。

Conclusion: 预训练VLM经过适应后能够发现与指令相关的新颖环境线索，通过测试时迭代计划-批评机制能有效改进运动规划，为机器人运动优化的测试时推理提供了新方向。

Abstract: Open-world mapless navigation from sparse language instructions requires resolving underspecified goals and inferring which environmental cues are relevant for reaching the goal. For instance, reaching an out-of-view destination may require interpreting ramps, signs, or detours that reveal where to go or which route to take. Prior works are limited by their reliance on known navigation factors and closed-set factor categories, or identify cues before motion planning and miss plan-dependent cues. We argue that pretrained Vision-Language Models (VLMs) can discover novel instruction-relevant cues, but require adaptation to focus on which cues matter and how they should influence motion planning. We realize these ideas in Foresight, a test-time framework in which a finetuned VLM alternates between proposing image-space motion plans and critiquing them using the language goal and visual context. Subsequent plans are conditioned on prior critiques, enabling iterative motion refinement before execution. To align plan critiques and refinements with open-set behavior preferences, we learn a reward model from human feedback and use it to post-train the VLM with reinforcement learning in the plan-critique loop. In offline evaluations and 6 real-world environments, Foresight improves average task success by 37% and reduces interventions per mission by 52% relative to state-of-the-art test-time reasoning and foundation-model baselines, while running in real-time on a Jetson AGX Orin. We will release code, data, and training details to support future work on test-time reasoning for robot motion refinement. Additional videos at: https://amrl.cs.utexas.edu/foresight

</details>


### [6] [Trajectory-Level Redirection Attacks on Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12978)
*Gokul Puthumanaillam,Vardhan Dongre,Pranay Thangeda,Hooshang Nayyeri,Dilek Hakkani-Tür,Melkior Ornik*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文发现并形式化了VLA策略中一种新的轨迹级安全漏洞：攻击者可以构造看似无害的提示，在保持指令表面含义不变的情况下，将机器人执行的最终物理结果重定向到攻击者指定的目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA攻击主要研究对抗性提示如何诱发特定低级动作或使动作在不同图像中持续存在，但尚未探索提示在闭环控制中反复使用时可导致轨迹级失败的模式。

Method: 提出命令保持的轨迹重定向（command-preserving trajectory redirection）威胁模型，并设计了一种on-policy提示搜索方法，通过 rollout 来发现满足约束的对抗性扰动。

Result: 仿真和硬件实验表明，接近良性的提示扰动能够将VLA rollout重定向到攻击者指定的目标。

Conclusion: 揭示了VLA指令接地中存在轨迹级漏洞：看似保持原始命令的文本仍可让攻击者控制机器人的最终物理结果。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) policies bring natural language into closed-loop robot control, enabling robots to execute manipulation tasks directly from text instructions. The same interface gives text a recurring role in control because the prompt is reused at every replanning step, and each prompt-conditioned action changes the future observations on which the policy acts. Existing VLA attacks study adversarial prompts that elicit targeted low-level actions or make such actions persist across changing images. We identify a stronger trajectory-level failure mode: a prompt that still $\textit{appears}$ to specify the intended task but redirects the final physical outcome. We mathematically formalize this setting as $\textit{command-preserving trajectory redirection}$, a prompt-only threat model in which the attacker chooses one prompt before the episode, all policy and environment components remain fixed, and the prompt must stay close to the benign instruction while omitting target words and correction language. To find such prompts, we introduce an on-policy prompt search method that uses rollouts to discover perturbations whose closed-loop behavior tracks a target task while satisfying the command-preserving constraints. Experiments in simulation and on hardware show that near-benign prompt perturbations can redirect VLA rollouts to attacker-specified targets. These results expose a trajectory-level vulnerability in VLA instruction grounding: text that appears to preserve the intended command can still give an adversary control over the robot's final physical outcome. Project website: https://vla-redirection-attack.github.io/

</details>


### [7] [G-MAPP: GPU-accelerated Multi-Agent Planning and Perception for Reactive Motion Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12579)
*Tanmay Bishnoi,Riddhiman Laha,Tobias Löw,Jose Alex Chandy,Luis F. C. Figueredo,Sami Haddadin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于GPU加速的机器人反应式运动生成框架，通过加速世界建模和矢量场规划，实现了在动态非结构化环境中的实时碰撞规避，相比CPU版本最高提速5倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非结构化环境中的反应式运动生成仍是机器人学中的开放挑战。现有方法要么为静态场景生成全局轨迹，要么采用对环境做出保守假设的模型。主要瓶颈在于高保真环境下的规划运行时性能，以及感知与规划模块之间的时间集成问题。

Method: 提出一个利用GPU加速世界建模和基于矢量场规划的框架，实现更快的并行状态探索以进行准全局轨迹规划，并使用现成深度传感器实现感知-动作环路的紧凑耦合。

Result: 在7自由度Franka Emika机器人上的定量和定性实验表明，所提GPU框架相比CPU版本实现了最高5倍的加速，在简单和具有挑战性的物理世界场景中均成功避碰。

Conclusion: 所提框架在不牺牲运行时性能和世界表征的前提下，通过GPU加速实现了动态杂乱环境中感知与规划的紧密耦合与实时反应式运动生成。

Abstract: Reactive motion generation in unstructured environments remains an open challenge in robotics. Due to the computational complexity of collision-free motion generation, existing methods either generate global trajectories for static scenarios, or employ models that make conservative assumptions about the environment. This paper identifies the primary bottleneck as the runtime performance demand of planning on high-fidelity environments, and the temporal integration between the perception and planning modules. Therefore, we propose a framework that does not compromise on runtime performance and world representations for perception and planning by accelerating world modeling and vector-field based planning using the GPU. This allows us to achieve faster parallel state exploration for quasi-global trajectory planning, and tighter coupling of the perception-action loop in real-time for dynamic cluttered environments with off-the-shelf depth sensors. We quantitatively evaluate the computation-time and success rate differences for the CPU and GPU versions of our planner, and perform qualitative evaluations of our coupled framework using real-world experiments on a 7-DoF Franka Emika robot. Experimental results demonstrate that our GPU-based framework achieves up to a 5x speedup over the CPU version and successfully avoids collisions across both trivial and challenging physical world scenarios.

</details>


### [8] [MCR-Bionic Hand: Anatomical Structural Priors for Dexterous Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13601)
*Haosen Yang,Guowu Wei*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出结构性智能双框架（结构先验生成+肌肉介导调制），并基于此开发了1:1肌肉骨骼仿生手MCR-Bionic Hand，通过解剖结构替代部分控制算法，实现了低维状态生成与精细接触后调制的结合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灵巧手通常被视为高维主动控制系统，但人手灵巧性部分编码在骨骼、韧带、肌腱等物理结构中，现有机器人手忽略了这一结构性智能的贡献。

Method: 提出结构性智能双框架：(1) 结构先验生成——腕指联动、FDS/FDP走线、背侧伸肌帽将低维姿态输入转化为默认抓取构型及PIP-DIP协调；(2) 肌肉介导调制——外在肌、蚓状肌、骨间肌调节MCP姿态、远端稳定性、指尖力路径及接触状态。基于该框架开发MCR-Bionic Hand，集成八骨双排腕部、交叉腕腱、解剖屈肌腱走线、掌板/侧副韧带约束、背侧伸肌帽及内在肌通路。

Result: 功能演示和几何力学模型表明：腕部姿态诱导多关节预塑形，伸肌帽将PIP姿态映射为耦合DIP响应，内在肌通路调节远端稳定性和指尖作用方向。硬币旋转、笔转移、硬币翻转、魔方操作等接触丰富任务验证了低维状态生成与接触后调制的结合。

Conclusion: 解剖仿生学的价值不在于视觉相似性，而在于识别人手结构中承担部分控制功能的结构，为降低灵巧手控制复杂度提供新思路。

Abstract: Dexterous robotic hands are usually formulated as high dimensional active control systems governed by degrees of freedom, actuation, and algorithms. Human hand dexterity, however, is partly encoded in the physical architecture of bones, ligaments, tendons, aponeuroses, and intrinsic muscles. This work describes that contribution as two linked forms of structural intelligence: structural prior generation, in which wrist to finger tenodesis, FDS/FDP routing, and the dorsal extensor hood transform low dimensional posture inputs into default grasp configurations and PIP to DIP coordination; and muscle mediated modulation, in which extrinsic muscles, lumbricals, and interossei regulate MCP posture, distal stability, fingertip force paths, and contact states around that default state.
  Based on this framework, MCR-Bionic Hand is developed as a 1:1 musculoskeletal biomimetic hand integrating a two row eight bone wrist, cross wrist tendons, anatomical flexor routing, volar plate and collateral ligament constraints, the dorsal extensor hood, and intrinsic muscle pathways within one body. Functional demonstrations and geometric mechanical models show that wrist posture induces multi joint pre shaping, the extensor hood maps PIP posture to a coupled DIP response, and intrinsic plus pathways modulate distal stability and fingertip action direction after grasp formation. Contact rich tasks, including coin rotation, pen transfer, dorsal coin flipping, and cube manipulation, show that MCR-Bionic links low dimensional state generation with fine post contact modulation. These results suggest that anatomical biomimetics is valuable not for visual similarity, but for identifying human hand structures that perform part of control.

</details>


### [9] [From Imitation to Alignment: Human-Preference Flow Policies for Long-Horizon Sidewalk Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12603)
*Honglin He,Zhizheng Liu,Yukai Ma,Bolei Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FlowPilot提出了一种基于锚定流匹配（anchored flow matching）和人类偏好学习的单目视觉无地图导航策略，用于长期人行道自主导航，在仿真中实现42%成功率和66%路线完成率，并将真实世界的干预率降低40.0%以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期人行道导航需要轻型感知（仅单目RGB相机），但模仿学习策略存在复合误差、缺乏社交合规性和反事实推理能力，难以应对复杂人行道场景。

Method: 1) 使用锚定流匹配（anchored flow matching）作为动作表示，在大规模机器人车队数据上预训练策略，捕获人行道导航行为的复杂多模态分布；2) 设计人类在环的偏好学习机制，利用少量人类干预数据对策略进行调优，增强反事实推理和社交合规性。

Result: 仿真中实现42%成功率和66%路线完成率；FlowPilot-HP将干预率（IR）降低40.0%，近干预率（NIR）降低52.1%。

Conclusion: FlowPilot通过流匹配预训练加人类偏好微调的范式，在仅使用单目RGB相机的情况下，显著提升了人行道导航的鲁棒性、社交合规性和反事实推理能力，是迈向轻量级长期自主导航的有效方案。

Abstract: Autonomous long-horizon sidewalk navigation is essential for micro-mobility applications such as robotic food delivery and assistive electronic wheelchairs. Unlike autonomous driving on the road, long-horizon sidewalk navigation requires precise maneuvering through unpredictable sidewalk terrains and pedestrians, with a lightweight perception stack as minimal as a single monocular RGB camera. While imitation learning (IL) from demonstrations offers a practical solution, the resulting autopilot policy often suffers from compounding errors, a lack of social compliance on sidewalks, and deficiencies in counterfactual reasoning to handle complex situations. To address these challenges, we introduce FlowPilot, a mapless navigation policy that achieves robust and efficient long-horizon navigation performance using only a monocular RGB camera. We first propose to use anchored flow matching as an action representation for policy pre-training on large-scale robot fleet data and to capture the diverse, complex, multimodal distribution of sidewalk navigation behaviors. To bridge the gap between imitation and alignment, we further design a human-in-the-loop preference learning scheme to tune the policy on a small amount of human intervention data. It strengthens the model's counterfactual reasoning and social compliance on sidewalks. We evaluate FlowPilot through extensive simulation and real-world experiments in diverse sidewalk environments. FlowPilot achieves 42% success rate and 66% route completion in simulation, while FlowPilot-HP further improves real-world robustness and social compliance, reducing IR by 40.0% and NIR by 52.1% relative to the base model.

</details>


### [10] [EgoEngine: From Egocentric Human Videos to High-Fidelity Dexterous Robot Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12604)
*Yangcen Liu,Shuo Cheng,Xinchen Yin,Woo Chul Shin,Alfred Cueva,Yiran Yang,Zhenyang Chen,Chuye Zhang,Danfei Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: EgoEngine是一个将自我中心人类操作视频转换为高保真机器人数据的框架，通过弥合视觉和动作两个鸿沟，首次实现了无需真实机器人演示的零样本视觉运动灵巧策略学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灵巧操作受限于大规模机器人演示数据的收集成本，而自我中心人类视频虽提供可扩展的操作行为来源，但存在人类与机器人之间的视觉差异和动作差异两大鸿沟。

Method: 给定自我中心RGB视频，EgoEngine同时生成：(i) 高保真机器人观测视频（用机器人替换人类，保留场景上下文和时间对齐）；(ii) 任务对齐且可执行的机器人动作轨迹（满足可行性约束）。

Result: 在仿真和真实机器人上的实验表明，EgoEngine实现了从自我中心人类视频到机器人数据的可扩展转换，并首次展示了无需真实机器人演示的零样本视觉运动灵巧策略学习。

Conclusion: EgoEngine提供了一种可扩展的框架，将自我中心人类操作视频高效转换为机器人可用的高保真数据，为降低灵巧操作的演示数据收集成本提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Dexterous manipulation is limited by the cost of collecting large-scale robot demonstrations. Egocentric human videos offer a scalable source of diverse manipulation behaviors, but directly using them for robot learning requires bridging two gaps: the visual gap between human and robot observations, and the action gap between human motion and robot-executable action. We propose EgoEngine, a scalable framework for transforming egocentric human manipulation videos into high-fidelity robot data. Given an egocentric RGB video, EgoEngine produces: (i) a high-fidelity robot observation video replacing human with robot while preserving scene context and temporal alignment, and (ii) a task-aligned, executable robot action trajectory under feasibility constraints. Experiments in simulation and on real robots show that EgoEngine enables scalable conversion of human videos into robot data and, to our knowledge, demonstrates the first zero-shot visuomotor dexterous policy learning from egocentric human videos without real-robot demonstrations. Project website: https://egoengine.github.io.

</details>


### [11] [Multi-Modal Multi-Agent Robotic Cognitive Alignment enabled by Non-Invasive Consumer Brain Computer Interfaces: A Proof of Concept Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13190)
*Nataliya Kosmyna,Liz Jenkins,Anoop K. Sinha*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于脑机接口和实时EEG信号处理的“认知对齐”多智能体交互框架，通过检测用户高认知负荷状态并暂缓主智能体的通信，待认知负荷降低后再释放消息，从而避免在不当时机打断用户，减少认知过载并提升任务表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的人机交互方法忽略了人类内部认知状态，导致多智能体系统常在不适当时机打断用户，造成认知过载和任务表现下降。

Method: 采用消费级脑机接口实时监测EEG频谱波段功率，构建基于参与度的检测管道；通过HTTP信令机制在主智能体检测到高参与度时将其输入输出置于保持状态，由次级智能体在后台处理复杂任务；待认知负荷恢复基线后释放队列信息。

Result: 初步结果验证了结合实时信号处理、大语言模型和物理机器人实体构建认知感知、非侵入式多智能体系统的可行性。

Conclusion: 该框架实现了认知对齐的多智能体交互，能够根据人类实时认知状态智能延迟通信，为构建更自然、非侵入式的人机交互系统提供了可行路径。

Abstract: While non-verbal behaviors and expressive movements are essential for natural human-robot interaction, existing methods often overlook a crucial element: the human's internal cognitive state. Frequently, proactive multi-agent systems can interrupt humans at inopportune moments, leading to cognitive overload and decreased task performance. This paper introduces a framework for generating "cognitively aligned" multi-agent interactions, enhancing the ability of robotic systems to contextually defer communications to the user of an agent system during moments of high human mental workload and engagement. We present the design and implementation of a closed-loop architecture that explores the interplay between autonomous task execution and real-time neurophysiological focus. Using a consumer-grade Brain-Computer Interface (BCI), our approach continuously monitors Electroencephalography (EEG) spectral band powers while a human performs an engagement-inducing task. We propose an engagement-driven pipeline where an HTTP-based signaling mechanism places a primary agent's sensory inputs and audio outputs into a holding state upon detecting high engagement. This allows secondary agents to seamlessly process complex, delegated tasks in the background. Once the human's cognitive state returns to a lower cognitive load baseline, the primary agent releases the queued agent message. Our preliminary results demonstrate the feasibility of leveraging real-time signal processing, Large Language Models (LLMs), and physical robotic embodiments to create cognitively-aware, non-intrusive multi-agent systems.

</details>


### [12] [DARRMS -- An Efficient Algorithm for Dynamic Attention Radius in Resource-Constrained Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12614)
*Benjamin Alcorn,Eman Hammad*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种通过限制智能体观测半径（注意力半径）来降低计算资源消耗的多智能体决策算法，在资源受限环境下保持系统性能与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统多智能体决策框架假设完全可观测和无限计算能力，这与现实世界中计算资源受限的挑战不符，需要一种轻量级高效算法。

Method: 引入注意力半径机制，让智能体主动忽略环境中对行动规划不必要的部分，并通过联合优化注意力半径与决策过程来实现协调与可扩展性。

Result: 通过理论分析和经验验证，自适应观测方法在降低计算资源需求的同时，有效提升了系统性能并维持了稳健的决策策略。

Conclusion: 所提算法能在资源约束下实现高效的协调与可扩展性，证明限制观测范围是一种实用的轻量化解决方案。

Abstract: Multi-agent systems are integral tools for various domains such as robotics, cybersecurity, and autonomous vehicle planning. These types of systems often have constraints on the computational resources, leading to a need for efficient lightweight algorithms. Traditional decision making frameworks often assume ideal conditions, such as full observability and unlimited computational capacity, which do not align with real-world challenges. In this paper, we introduce a new algorithm that allows for reduced demand on computational resources without a large cost of other performance metrics. Agents will limit their observability to some attention radius, which intentionally allows them to ignore parts of the environment that might be unnecessary for action planning. By optimizing both the attention radius and decision-making, our approach enhances coordination and scalability in uncertain environments. Through both theoretical analysis and empirical validation, we demonstrate the effectiveness of adaptive observation in improving system performance and maintaining robust decision-making strategies in resource-constrained systems.

</details>


### [13] [EWAM: An Enhanced World Action Model for Closed-Loop Online Adaptation in Embodied Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12690)
*Xin Zhou,Cong Miao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出增强世界行动模型(EWAM)，一种基于冻结Cosmos3骨干网络的闭环在线自适应架构，通过四个轻量神经层实现零样本任务适应，无需额外微调或演示数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 减少预训练模型适应新任务布局所需的额外部署数据量，在零样本协议下实现高效的任务泛化。

Method: 在冻结的Cosmos3骨干网络上插入四个可微分的轻量神经层：神经经验记忆层（提供任务上下文）、神经异常检测层（实时监控状态偏差）、神经策略路由层（基于异常严重度选择执行策略）、神经动作校正层（优化动作生成）。仅路由决策为离散监督，其余模块可微分集成。

Result: 零样本任务协议下，无需额外演示集和骨干网络微调，通过推理时协同推理机制取得性能提升。

Conclusion: EWAM通过轻量级推理时自适应机制，在不修改预训练模型的前提下有效适应新任务布局，验证了零样本任务适应的可行性。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose the Enhanced World Action Model (EWAM), a closed-loop online adaptation architecture built upon a pretrained and fully frozen Cosmos3 backbone network. Evaluated entirely under a zero-shot task protocol, EWAM is centrally focused on reducing the amount of additional deployment data required to adapt to new task layouts. Notably, no extra task-specific demonstration sets were introduced in any of the evaluations, and no fine-tuning was performed on the backbone network. Its performance gains stem entirely from an inference-time co-reasoning mechanism composed of four inserted lightweight neural layers: the Neural Experience Memory Layer located in the intermediate layers of the Diffusion Transformer (DiT) provides task-relevant execution context; the Neural Anomaly Detection Layer after the state prediction head monitors the divergence between predicted and actual states in real time; the Neural Policy Routing Layer dynamically selects direct execution, conservative replanning, or rollback recovery based on the anomaly severity; and the Neural Action Correction Layer refines the generated action chunks using execution diagnostics. Unlike naive feature fusion, the memory, anomaly detection, and correction modules are deeply integrated into the Cosmos3 forward path in a differentiable manner, with only the final routing decision being a discrete supervised one.

</details>


### [14] [EquiDexFlow: Contact-Grounded SE(3)-Equivariant Dexterous Grasp Generative Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12728)
*Clinton Enwerem,John S. Baras,Calin Belta*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: EquiDexFlow是一个SE(3)-等变流匹配模型，能够联合预测抓取姿态、关节角度、 fingertip接触点、表面法向和接触力，并通过将接触投影到物体表面、将力约束在库伦摩擦锥内来保证物理可行性，在仿真和真实机器人上均取得优异效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有灵巧抓取生成器多将接触力作为后处理验证步骤，导致运动学可行的抓取姿态仍可能违反稳定物理抓取的条件。本文旨在解决这一预测与物理约束脱节的问题。

Method: 提出EquiDexFlow，一种SE(3)-等变流匹配模型，联合预测手腕姿态、关节角、指尖接触点、表面法向和接触力。通过将接触投影到物体表面、将力约束在库伦摩擦锥内来保证物理可行性。证明了端到端的SE(3)等变性，并在8,100个力闭合抓取样本上训练。

Result: 在200次旋转上验证SE(3)等变性，手腕残差低于0.04°，关节偏差严格为零。在Allegro Hand上零摩擦违规，综合得分最高，扳手残差最低。重定位到LEAP Hand后，所有关节至少位于执行器包络内5%。真实机器人上所有六个测试物体均成功完成开环抓取-保持实验。

Conclusion: 将接触力和摩擦约束直接集成到生成模型中，而非作为后处理步骤，能够显著提升灵巧抓取的物理可行性，且SE(3)等变性保证了模型对任意旋转的泛化能力。

Abstract: Most learned dexterous grasp generators relegate contact forces to a downstream verification step, so a kinematically-plausible pose can still violate the conditions for a stable physical grasp. We address this with EquiDexFlow, an SE(3)-equivariant flow-matching model that jointly predicts wrist pose, joint angles, fingertip contacts, surface normals, and contact forces from an object point cloud. Our architecture projects contacts onto the object surface and forces into the Coulomb friction cone by construction, so placement and friction compliance hold without loss penalties. We prove end-to-end SE(3) equivariance and verify it empirically over 200 rotations, with wrist residuals below $0.04^\circ$ and exactly zero joint deviation. Trained on 8,100 force-closure grasps across 81 objects for the 16-DoF Allegro Hand, our model achieves zero friction violations, the best composite score, and the lowest wrench residual among all ablation variants. We retarget decoded fingertip contacts to a 16-DoF LEAP Hand via per-finger inverse kinematics, and our hardware-feasible refinement places every joint at least 5% inside its actuator envelope while preserving wrench balance. On the physical robot, retargeted EquiDexFlow-decoded grasps complete open-loop pick-and-hold trials on all six test objects, with every asymmetric object succeeding at both the canonical pose and a $120^\circ$ co-rotation. Videos, code, and checkpoints are available at https://equidexflow.github.io.

</details>


### [15] [Sparse2Act: Learning Action-Aligned Sparse 3D Representations for Cross-Domain Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12759)
*Yu Guo,Chang Yu,Siyu Ma,Yunuo Chen,Yin Yang,Ying Nian Wu,Chenfanfu Jiang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Sparse2Act 提出利用末端执行器动作作为几何监督信号，通过掩码稀疏3D点云编码器的预训练框架实现观察-动作对齐。预训练后的编码器可被下游策略复用，在LIBERO-10上达到86.9%的成功率，并支持跨域迁移和sim-to-real迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的稀疏3D编码器通常通过下游任务目标学习，导致表示与特定数据分布、策略架构和动作参数化绑定，缺乏可迁移的通用3D表示。

Method: 提出Sparse2Act框架，利用任务空间中的末端执行器动作作为几何监督信号，对掩码稀疏3D点云token进行预训练，使其围绕观测伴随的工作空间运动组织场景特征。预训练后仅复用编码器初始化，下游策略保留自身架构和动作空间。

Result: LIBERO-10上500次微调后平均成功率86.9%；LIBERO到Meta-World跨域迁移在Meta-World-5上达73.4%；真实世界实验sim-to-real迁移在四项任务上平均成功率72.5%。

Conclusion: 机器人动作可以作为紧凑的几何监督信号，用于训练可复用的稀疏3D表示，验证了动作对齐预训练的有效性。

Abstract: Explicit 3D representations are attractive for manipulation because they expose object shape, workspace geometry, and robot-object relations in metric coordinates. However, sparse 3D encoders are often learned through downstream task objectives, tying the representation to a particular data distribution, policy architecture, and action parameterization. We introduce Sparse2Act, an observation-action alignment framework for pretraining sparse point-cloud encoders. The key idea is to use task-space end-effector actions as geometric supervision: masked sparse 3D tokens are trained to organize scene features around the workspace motion paired with the observation. After pretraining, only the encoder initialization is reused by downstream policies, allowing them to retain their own architectures and action spaces, including joint-space commands. On the LIBERO-10 benchmark, our method achieves 86.9% average success after 500 fine-tuning steps. The same pretrained encoder supports LIBERO-to-Meta-World cross-domain transfer, achieving 73.4% average success on the Meta-World-5 benchmark. Ablations on the objective and decoder capacity show that the gains come from the masked action-alignment signal and remain useful across downstream action decoders. In real-world experiments, simulation pretraining followed by limited real-data fine-tuning achieves an average success rate of 72.5% across four tasks, demonstrating effective sim-to-real transfer. These results suggest that robot actions can provide compact geometric supervision for reusable sparse 3D representations.

</details>


### [16] [Stubborn: A Streamlined and Unified Reinforcement Learning Framework for Robust Motion Tracking and Fall Recovery for Humanoids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12814)
*Xiao Ren,Yuhui Yang,Zongbiao Weng,Zhijie Liu,He Kong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Stubborn框架，一种统一的人形机器人运动跟踪与摔倒恢复强化学习框架，通过非对称Actor-Critic架构、偏航对齐表示、基于伯努利分布的概率终止机制和自适应采样策略，实现无需多阶段训练的鲁棒运动跟踪与摔倒恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将运动跟踪和摔倒恢复视为不同任务，需要多阶段训练和专门的恢复奖励/策略，且训练中立即终止失败轨迹限制了恢复行为的探索。

Method: 1) 偏航对齐的运动表示降低对全局漂移和航向干扰的敏感度；2) 基于伯努利分布的概率终止机制鼓励摔倒恢复行为探索；3) 基于概率终止和跟踪误差驱动的自适应采样策略，动态重塑采样分布以提升困难片段和不稳定状态的训练效率。

Result: 与SOTA方法对比和消融实验表明，Stubborn取得了具有竞争力的性能，概率终止机制和自适应采样策略显著提升了性能和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: Stubborn通过统一框架有效解决了人形机器人运动跟踪与摔倒恢复问题，避免了多阶段训练的复杂性，并提升了训练效率和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Recent reinforcement learning approaches have shown great promise in improving humanoid motion tracking performance and achieving fall recovery under disturbances. However, most existing works treat motion tracking and fall recovery as different tasks and require multi-stage training with specialized recovery rewards and/or separate recovery policies. Moreover, existing reinforcement learning-based methods often terminate training episodes immediately after severe tracking failures, limiting recovery-oriented exploration in unstable or fallen states. To address the above issues, we propose Stubborn, a streamlined and unified reinforcement learning framework to achieve robust humanoid motion tracking and fall recovery. Specifically, Stubborn uses an asymmetric Actor-Critic architecture and consists of three major components. First, a yaw-aligned tracking representation is adopted to reduce sensitivity to global drift and heading disturbances while preserving gravity-related balance information. Second, we introduce a Bernoulli-based probabilistic termination mechanism that enables the policy to encourage exploration of fall-recovery behaviors under varying failure modes. Third, we propose a probabilistic termination and tracking-error-driven strategy that dynamically reshapes the sampling distribution based on tracking performance, increasing the training efficiency for difficult motion segments and unstable states. Extensive comparisons with SOTA methods and ablation studies show that Stubborn achieved competitive performance, and the proposed probabilistic termination mechanism and adaptive sampling strategy contributed to the performance and robustness gains. For real-world demonstrations, please refer to https://aislab-sustech.github.io/Stubborn/.

</details>


### [17] [AIR-VLA+: Decoupling Movement and Manipulation via Cascaded Dual-Action Decoders with Asymmetric MoE for Aerial Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12859)
*Jianli Sun,Bin Tian,Qiyao Zhang,Zijian Liu,Yutong Wang,Zhiyong Cui,Bai Li,Yisheng Lv,Yonglin Tian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AIR-VLA+提出了一种面向空中操纵系统的级联双动作解码器架构，通过非对称特征级MoE和隐式视觉抓取投影器，解耦无人机移动与机械臂操作的表征耦合，在标准化基准上以48.0平均分全面超越所有基线，任务完成度相比单头策略提升80.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空中操纵系统中的端到端控制存在严重的表征耦合问题：无人机平台移动与末端执行器操作在动作尺度、动力学特性及控制目标上差异显著，传统单头策略无法有效协调这种异构控制冲突。

Method: 1) 级联双动作解码器：操纵解码器与移动解码器级联，无人机单向观测机械臂意图以实现工作流协调，同时隔离移动信息反向传播对操作稳定性的影响。2) 移动解码器输入增强模块：引入隐式视觉抓取投影器感知夹爪-物体交互状态，注入压缩全局语义特征。3) 非对称特征级MoE：在移动解码器中部署隐式MoE架构，不同移动专家在训练中自发形成对不同任务阶段的能力倾向，通过特征流形上的密集软混合计算增强任务阶段适应性。

Result: 在标准化AIR-VLA基准上平均分达48.0，全面超越所有基线；任务完成度相比单头π0.5策略提升80.2%，有效缓解了复合机器人的异构协调控制冲突。

Conclusion: 级联双解码器与非对称MoE架构能够有效解耦空中操纵系统中移动与操作的表征耦合，显著提升异构协调控制性能，为复合机器人端到端控制提供了新的设计范式。

Abstract: Aerial manipulation systems have long suffered from representation coupling in end-to-end control, as platform-level Unmanned Aerial Vehicle (UAV) movement and end-effector-level arm manipulation differ substantially in action scale, dynamics, and control objectives. In this paper, we propose AIR-VLA+, a flow matching action generation architecture specifically designed for aerial manipulation, featuring cascaded dual-action decoders and an asymmetric feature-level Mixture of Experts (MoE). We construct cascaded manipulation and movement decoders, allowing the UAV to unidirectionally observe the manipulator's intent during movement to achieve workflow coordination, while isolating the impact of UAV movement information backpropagation on arm manipulation stability. Addressing the characteristic that UAV movement is highly dependent on high-level semantics and responsible for task state transitions in aerial manipulation, we design an input feature enhancement module for the UAV movement decoder. This module introduces an implicit visual grasp projector to perceive the interaction state between the gripper and the object, and injects compressed global semantic features. Within the UAV movement decoder, we deploy an implicit MoE architecture, enabling different movement experts to spontaneously exhibit capacity inclinations for various task stages during training. Through dense soft blending computation on the feature manifold, the UAV movement is endowed with stronger task-stage adaptability. Experiments on the standardized AIR-VLA benchmark demonstrate that our method comprehensively surpasses all baselines with an overall average score of 48.0. The overall task completion score improves by 80.2\% compared to the single-head $π_{0.5}$ policy, effectively mitigating the heterogeneous coordinated control conflicts of composite robots.

</details>


### [18] [Learning to Adapt: Representation-Based Reinforcement Learning for Multi-Task Skill Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12890)
*Aryan Naveen,Haitong Ma,Haldun Balim,Na Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了RepMT-SAC框架，通过谱MDP分解实现多任务强化学习中的知识共享和迁移，在四旋翼轨迹跟踪任务上取得高达30%的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习在样本效率和跨任务泛化方面存在局限性，限制了其实际应用。

Method: 提出RepMT-SAC框架，使用谱MDP分解捕获可迁移的动力学特征，将价值函数结构化为任务无关的核心部分加上最小任务特定调整。

Result: 在四旋翼轨迹跟踪任务上，RepMT-SAC在分布内任务上实现强零样本性能，在分布外任务上实现快速少样本适应，相比基线方法提升高达30%。

Conclusion: RepMT-SAC通过高效的知识共享和鲁棒的迁移能力，有效解决了多任务RL中的样本效率和泛化问题。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning has achieved remarkable success in learning complex control policies, yet its applicability remains limited due to sample inefficiency and poor generalization across tasks. In this work, we propose RepMT-SAC, a framework for multi-task RL that enables efficient knowledge sharing and robust transfer to new tasks. RepMT-SAC uses spectral MDP decomposition to capture transferable dynamics, structuring the value function into a task-agnostic core with a minimal task-specific adjustment. This design allows for strong zero-shot performance on in-distribution tasks and rapid few-shot adaptation to out-of-distribution tasks. We evaluate RepMT-SAC on quadcopter trajectory-following tasks across in-distribution and out-of-distribution contexts, demonstrating that it outperforms baselines by up to 30%.

</details>


### [19] [An Embodied Simulation Platform, Benchmark, and Data-Efficient Augmentation Framework for Wet-Lab Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12936)
*Zhe Liu,Huanbo Jin,Zhaohui Du,Zhe Wang,He Xu,Peijia Li,Jiaming Gu,Quan Lu,Qi Wang,Bin Ji,Ting Xiao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Pipette是一个面向湿实验室机器人学习的仿真平台、基准测试和数据增强框架，包含43个开源可编辑资产、基于仿真的数据增强流水线、11个任务基准测试，以及自然语言驱动的场景构建功能。仅需30次演示即可实现有效训练，仿真增强将SmolVLA成功率从44.1%提升至74.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 湿实验室机器人可提升生物医学实验的可重复性、通量和安全性，但其学习规模化面临三大挑战：需要可定制的仿真器以安全生成可复现任务、需要开源可编辑实验室资产、需要高效流水线将有限演示转化为可用训练数据。

Method: Pipette包含三大核心组件：(1) 43个开源可编辑湿实验室资产及可扩展资产构建流水线；(2) 基于仿真的数据增强流水线，回放人类演示并施加光照、相机、速度和动作扰动，通过自动任务成功检测过滤生成片段；(3) 11个任务的湿实验室具身基准测试，涵盖样品处理、培养器操作、设备操作和精密放置等场景。同时支持自然语言驱动的场景构建和任务注册。

Result: 仅用30次演示/任务，ACT达到65.5%平均成功率；仿真增强将SmolVLA从44.1%提升至74.7%，π0从40.4%提升至46.5%。

Conclusion: Pipette验证了面向数据高效的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）训练和评估的有效性，通过仿真数据增强显著降低了湿实验室机器人学习对人工演示数量的依赖。

Abstract: Wet-lab robots can improve the reproducibility, throughput, and safety of biomedical experiments, but scaling their learning requires customizable simulators for safe and reproducible task generation, open editable laboratory assets, and efficient pipelines that turn limited demonstrations into usable training data. We present Pipette, an embodied simulation platform, benchmark, and data-efficient augmentation framework for wet-lab robot learning. Pipette releases over 43 open-source and re-editable wet-lab assets, together with an extensible asset-building pipeline. A key component of Pipette is its simulation-based data augmentation pipeline, replaying human demonstrations in simulation, applies lighting, camera, speed, and action perturbations, and filters generated episodes with automatic task success checks, rapidly expanding usable training data from limited manual demonstrations. We further introduce an 11-task wet-lab embodied benchmark covering sample handling, culture-ware manipulation, device operation, and precision placement. With only 30 demonstrations per task, ACT achieves 65.5% average success rate, while simulation augmentation improves SmolVLA from 44.1% to 74.7% and π0 from 40.4% to 46.5%, validating the effectiveness of Pipette for data-efficient VLA training and evaluation. Pipette also supports natural-language-driven scene construction and task registration, lowering the barrier for non-expert users to define new wet-lab robotic tasks.

</details>


### [20] [Towards Reliable Sequential Object Picking in Clutter: The Runner-up Solution to RGMC 2025](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12954)
*Wei Yu,Xidan Zhang,Ziyi Zheng,Weijie Kong,Huixu Dong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种集成硬件-软件流水线，用于解决杂乱环境中的顺序物体抓取问题，通过多功能夹爪设计、物体分布与遮挡关系的新型表示，结合物体识别、清理和抓取策略，在RGMC 2025的Pick-in-Clutter赛道中获得第二名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管现有研究在杂乱环境抓取中取得了较高成功率，但在顺序物体搜索与分拣等更具挑战性的任务中仍缺乏成熟解决方案，因此本文旨在解决这一实际工业需求。

Method: 设计了一个集成硬件-软件流水线，包含：1) 多功能夹爪的硬件设计；2) 物体分布与遮挡关系的新型空间表示；3) 结合物体识别、清理和多模态抓取策略的完整流程。

Result: 在实验室测试和比赛场景中均展现出强劲性能，最终在RGMC 2025的Pick-in-Clutter赛道中荣获第二名。

Conclusion: 该流水线能够高效地在杂乱环境中实现物体的识别、搜索和顺序抓取，为工业环境中的复杂抓取任务提供了可行方案。

Abstract: As a long-standing challenge in robotic manipulation, stable and efficient grasping in cluttered environments is of great importance in industrial settings. While recent studies have achieved relatively high success rates in grasping from clutter, there remain few mature solutions for more demanding tasks such as sequential object search and sorting. This work addresses sequential object picking in cluttered environments based on the Cluttered Environment Picking Benchmark (CEPB) and presents our solution to the Pick-in-Clutter track of the 10th Robotic Grasping and Manipulation Competition (RGMC) at ICRA 2025. The task poses several key challenges. First, it requires robust and collision-aware grasping with high success rates across a diverse set of objects, including both rigid and deformable ones. Second, it demands efficient search for target objects, which places stringent requirements on the decluttering and searching strategies of the solution. To address the above challenges, we design an integrated hardware-software pipeline that combines object recognition, decluttering, and multi-modal grasping. The main contributions include the hardware design of a multifunctional gripper and novel representations for object distribution and occlusion relationships in cluttered space. This pipeline enables efficient recognition, search, and sequential grasping of objects in clutter, demonstrating strong performance in both laboratory tests and competition scenarios, and ultimately achieving second place in the Pick-in-Clutter track of the RGMC 2025.

</details>


### [21] [SERF: Spatiotemporal Environment and Robot Feature Map for Long-Horizon Mobile Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12956)
*Sunghwan Kim,Byeonghyun Pak,Kehan Long,Yulun Tian,Nikolay Atanasov*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出SERF（时空环境与机器人特征图），通过将环境与机器人本体表示为共享潜在空间中的神经点，并利用在线更新的时空特征图作为视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型的状态输入，提升了移动操作策略在长时域任务中的推理能力。实验表明，该方法在BEHAVIOR-1K基准上优于纯图像基线，轨迹更直接，对场景变化更鲁棒，并能从物体掉落等失败中恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时域移动操作任务要求机器人持续推理定位、环境变化和任务进度，而仅依赖图像观测难以有效处理这些信息，因此需要一种更丰富的时空状态表示。

Method: 构建SERF图：将环境和机器人关节表示为共享潜在空间中的神经点，通过在线方式更新——环境点使用目标级刚性跟踪更新，机器人点使用正向运动学更新。提取多参考帧和多空间尺度的地图令牌作为VLA模型的状态输入，提供局部和全局上下文。

Result: 在BEHAVIOR-1K基准上，SERF VLA策略优于图像基线，子目标到达更快、轨迹更直接，对场景配置变化更鲁棒，并能从物体掉落失败中恢复。

Conclusion: 时空特征图作为状态表示能够显著提升移动操作策略在长时域任务中的性能，为机器人长期自主操作提供了有效的表示学习范式。

Abstract: Long-horizon robot mobile manipulation requires continual reasoning about localization, environment changes, and task progress, all of which are challenging to infer from image observations alone. In this paper, we show that conditioning a mobile manipulation policy on a spatiotemporal feature map improves reasoning over long horizons. The map represents the environment and the articulated robot body as neural points in a shared latent space and is updated online from egocentric observations and proprioceptive state. We update the environment neural points using object-level rigid tracking and the robot neural points using forward kinematics. We use our spatiotemporal environment and robot feature (SERF) map as a state input to a vision-language-action (VLA) model by extracting map tokens from multiple reference frames and spatial scales, providing the policy with both local and global context. We demonstrate SERF on BEHAVIOR-1K, a benchmark for long-horizon mobile manipulation in household environments. Experiments show that the SERF VLA policy outperforms image-only baselines, reaches subgoals faster by following more direct trajectories, improves robustness to scene-configuration shifts, and recovers from object-drop failures.

</details>


### [22] [EmbodiSteer: Steering Embodiment-Agnostic Visuomotor Policies with Joint-Space Guidance for Zero-Shot Cross-Embodiment Deployment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12965)
*Shihefeng Wang,Kangchen Lv,Mingrui Yu,Xiang Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: EmbodiSteer是一种无需训练的框架，通过将推理时的扩散采样提升到关节空间并加入全身碰撞感知引导，使与具体机械臂无关的视觉运动策略实现零样本的具身感知部署，在仿真和实物机器人上显著降低了碰撞率并提高了任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于笛卡尔末端执行器动作的模仿学习方法虽然支持与具体机械臂无关的策略学习，但由于缺乏对部署机器人本体的感知，在全身碰撞规避等机器人特定约束下表现脆弱，限制了策略在实际物理机器人上的鲁棒部署。

Method: EmbodiSteer保持策略在笛卡尔空间学习，但在推理时通过正向运动学和雅可比更新将扩散采样提升到目标机器人的关节空间，并在每次去噪步骤后施加全身碰撞感知引导，使机械臂在保持所学末端执行器行为的同时避开碰撞。

Result: 在9个仿真机器人上，碰撞率降低46.1%，任务成功率提升28.5%；在高度受限场景下的两台实物机器人上，碰撞率降低90.0%，成功率提升36.7%。

Conclusion: EmbodiSteer证明了无需重新训练即可将与具体机械臂无关的策略零样本适配到具身感知部署的可行性，实现了在保持末端执行器行为的同时有效避免全身碰撞。

Abstract: Scalable robot imitation learning relies on large-scale heterogeneous data from diverse robots or body-free data, making Cartesian end-effector actions a key interface for embodiment-agnostic policy learning. However, end-effector-only abstraction leaves Cartesian policies unaware of the deployed robot body, making them brittle under robot-specific constraints such as whole-body collision avoidance. To overcome this limitation, we present EmbodiSteer, a training-free framework that steers embodiment-agnostic visuomotor policies toward zero-shot, embodiment-aware deployment. EmbodiSteer keeps policy learning in Cartesian space while efficiently lifting inference-time diffusion sampling into the target robot's joint space via forward kinematics and Jacobian-based updates. With whole-body collision-aware guidance over joint trajectories after each denoising step, the arm can be steered away from collisions while preserving learned end-effector behavior. Compared with Cartesian-only execution, EmbodiSteer reduces collision rate by 46.1% and improves task success rate by 28.5% across 9 simulated robots, and further achieves 90.0% collision rate reduction and 36.7% success rate increase on two physical robots in highly constrained scenarios. Our project page is at https://frankwang67.github.io/EmbodiSteer-Page.

</details>


### [23] [GenHOI: Contact-Aware Humanoid-Object Interaction by Imitating Generated Videos without Task-Specific Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12995)
*Zhihai Bi,Qiang Zhang,Guoyang Zhao,Jiahang Cao,Xueyin Luo,Yushan Zhang,Jinglan Xu,Ruoyu Geng,Yulin Li,Andrew F. Luo,Jun Ma*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GenHOI是一个无需任务特定训练或物理演示数据，通过直接模仿单个生成视频实现人形机器人零样本物体交互的框架。它从仿真场景重建和首帧图像出发，结合语言指令生成交互视频，提取接触约束优化运动轨迹，最后由闭环控制器执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人需要具备与多样物体交互的能力，但现有方法要么需要耗时的任务特定策略训练，要么依赖刚性的轨迹回放，难以适应新交互场景。作者希望实现一种无需任务特定训练和物理演示数据的零样本交互能力。

Method: GenHOI框架包括：(1) 在仿真中重建机器人-物体场景并渲染首帧图像；(2) 结合语言指令生成任务导向的交互视频；(3) 分析视频提取交互相关的接触事件并估计手-物接触区域；(4) 将接触线索编码为以物体为中心的几何约束，作为物理优化先验；(5) 从视频恢复参考运动并优化平滑，消除尺度模糊并适应未见过的相对位姿；(6) 闭环跟踪控制器执行优化后的轨迹。

Result: 在多种物体交互任务（箱子抓取、非对称双臂搬椅子、从下方抬桌子、圆柱物体环抱）的仿真和真实实验中验证了框架的有效性，实现了零样本的物体交互。

Conclusion: GenHOI通过视频生成引导的方式，实现了人形机器人对多样物体交互任务的零样本泛化，避免了任务特定训练的需求，为通用人形机器人交互提供了新范式。

Abstract: Humanoid-Object Interaction (HOI) is a fundamental capability for humanoid robots, yet it remains challenging due to the tight coupling between dynamic balance and stable interaction with diverse objects. Existing methods often require time-consuming task-specific policy training or rely on rigid trajectory replay, which limits their ability to accommodate novel interaction scenarios. In this work, we present \textit{GenHOI}, a simple yet effective framework that enables humanoid robots to perform diverse object-interaction tasks in a zero-shot manner by directly imitating a single generated video, without task-specific training or physical demonstration data. GenHOI first reconstructs the robot-object scene in simulation and renders a first-frame image, which, together with the language command, conditions the synthesis of a task-oriented interaction video. The generated video is then analyzed to identify interaction-relevant contact events and estimate hand-object contact regions, which are encoded as object-centric geometric constraints that convert visual interaction cues into physically grounded optimization priors. Guided by these priors, the reference motion recovered from the video is refined and smoothed to resolve the scale ambiguity inherent in 2D video generation, while adapting a single reference trajectory to unseen robot-object relative poses. The optimized trajectory is finally executed by a closed-loop tracking controller. We validate the proposed framework in extensive simulation and real-world experiments across diverse object-interaction tasks, including box grasping, asymmetric bimanual chair carrying, table lifting from below, and cylindrical-object enveloping.

</details>


### [24] [Comparing Commercial Depth Sensor Accuracy for Medical Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13028)
*Pit Henrich,Maximilian Weiherer,Franziska Hansen,Bernhard Egger,Franziska Mathis-Ullrich*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文在猪骨标本、猪肚标本和硅胶肾脏模型上，使用触笔采样参考，对四种深度传感器（Intel RealSense D405、PMD Flexx2、Stereolabs ZED 2i、Zivid 2M+ 60）在约50cm距离下进行了基准测试，比较了立体视觉、结构光和飞行时间三种技术。结果表明Zivid 2M+ 60在所有对象和指标中表现最佳，ZED在真实组织上排名第二但在模型上表现最差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度估计在医学和外科领域有广泛应用，但真实组织（如均匀表面、镜面表面、次表面散射）对深度传感器构成挑战，现有传感器在这些场景下的性能缺乏系统比较。

Method: 在猪骨标本、猪肚标本和硅胶肾脏模型上，使用触笔采样点作为地面真值参考，在约50cm距离下对四种深度传感器（Intel RealSense D405、PMD Flexx2、Stereolabs ZED 2i、Zivid 2M+ 60）进行基准测试，涵盖立体视觉、结构光和飞行时间三种技术。

Result: Zivid 2M+ 60在所有对象和指标中表现最佳；Stereolabs ZED 2i在真实组织上排名第二，但在硅胶肾脏模型上表现最差。

Conclusion: 不同深度传感器在医学应用中的性能差异显著，Zivid 2M+ 60在挑战性表面条件下具有优势，而ZED在真实组织上表现良好但在模型上不佳，提示传感器选择需根据具体应用场景权衡。

Abstract: Depth estimation has numerous medical and surgical applications. We benchmark four depth sensors on a porcine bone specimen, a porcine belly specimen, and a silicone kidney phantom using stylus-sampled references. These objects contain several real-world challenges, including homogeneous surfaces, specular surfaces, and subsurface scattering. The comparison includes stereo, structured-light, and time-of-flight sensors at a distance of approximately 50 cm. Specifically, the Intel RealSense D405 (Intel RealSense, United States), PMD Flexx2 (pmdtechnologies, Germany), Stereolabs ZED 2i (Stereolabs, France), and Zivid 2M+ 60 (Zivid, Norway) are compared. The Zivid 2M+ 60 performed best across all objects and metrics considered in this work. The ZED ranked second for real tissue, but last on the phantom.

</details>


### [25] [RoboProcessBench: Benchmarking Process-Aware Understanding in Vision-Language Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13040)
*Dayu Xia,Yue Shi,Yao Mu,Huiting Ji,Chaofan Ma,Yingjie Zhou,Hua Chen,Yang Liu,Jiezhang Cao,Guangtao Zhai*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RoboProcessBench是一个用于评估视觉语言模型在机器人操作任务中过程感知能力的基准，包含约5.8万问答对和12个诊断任务族，实验表明现有模型存在明显局限，但通过微调可提升局部状态、运动、进度和基元感知能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的VLM评估未能测试模型在机器人操作执行过程中是否具备细粒度的过程理解能力（包括静态监控和动态推理），因此需要构建专门的基准来填补这一空白。

Method: 将过程感知能力分解为静态监控和动态推理两个维度，实例化为12个诊断问题族（涵盖阶段、接触、运动、协调、基元级进度、时序顺序、结果和基元级转换），基于物理执行轨迹构建包含约5.8万问答对的ProcessData语料库，并划分为SFT和Eval两个子集。

Result: 多种VLM在ProcessData-Eval的12个诊断任务族上均表现出广泛的局限性；但使用ProcessData-SFT微调后，Qwen2.5-VL-7B和InternVL-3-8B在局部状态、运动、进度和基元感知指标上取得了一致的提升。

Conclusion: RoboProcessBench既可作为评估基准，也可作为可学习的监督来源，用于开发能够监控和评估机器人操作过程的VLM。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) are increasingly explored as visual critics, reward generators, and failure detectors in robotic manipulation. These roles implicitly require models to judge not only final task success, but also how a manipulation execution is physically and temporally progressing. However, existing evaluations fail to test whether VLMs possess fine-grained process understanding. To address this gap, we present RoboProcessBench, a benchmark for process-aware understanding in vision-language robotic manipulation. RoboProcessBench decomposes such capability into two complementary dimensions, \emph{static monitoring} and \emph{dynamic reasoning}, instantiated as 12 diagnostic question families covering phase, contact, motion, coordination, primitive-local progress, temporal order, outcome, and primitive-level transitions. Built from physically grounded execution traces, the curated benchmark corpus ProcessData contains \textasciitilde 58k question-answer pairs across 260 manipulation tasks, which is further split into ProcessData-SFT and ProcessData-Eval for post-training and evaluation purposes. Extensive evaluation of various VLMs on ProcessData-Eval reveals broad limitations across 12 diagnostic task families, suggesting current models still lack robust process-aware understanding of manipulation executions. But with ProcessData-SFT, the post-trained \textit{Qwen2.5-VL-7B} and \textit{InternVL-3-8B} exhibit consistent gains on local state, motion, progress, and primitive-aware cues. These results demonstrate that RoboProcessBench serves as both an evaluation benchmark and a learnable supervision source for developing VLMs capable of monitoring and evaluating robotic manipulation processes. Project webpage: \href{https://processbench-2026.github.io/RoboProcessBench-Web/}{https://processbench-2026.github.io}.

</details>


### [26] [Y-BotFrame: An Extensible Embodied Agent Framework for Quadruped Robot Assistants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13049)
*Luyao Zhang,Ke Li,Yuan Ding,Xulong Zhao,Guo Yu,Chengwei Yan,Fuyu Dong,Jiawei Hu,Di Wang,Nan Luo,Gang Liu,Quan Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Y-BotFrame 是一个可扩展的具身智能平台，将四足机器人转变为智能地面助手，集成多模态感知（语音、视觉、激光雷达）并以大语言模型为认知核心，支持自然语言指令驱动的任务规划与执行，实现无需遥控器的人机协作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 四足机器人具备高灵活性和复杂地形适应能力，但现有平台在自然语言交互、多模态感知融合和模块可扩展性方面存在不足，缺乏通用化、指令驱动的具身智能部署方案。

Method: 将大语言模型作为认知核心，集成语音、视觉和激光雷达等多模态感知能力，把用户自然语言指令映射为可执行的具身任务单元，支持即插即用的模块化扩展和迭代升级。

Result: 实现了无需遥控器的自然语音指令交互与视觉反馈，构建了高效的人机协作系统，提供了通用指令驱动具身智能体的参考实现。

Conclusion: Y-BotFrame 为四足机器人在真实场景中的通用化部署提供了可行方案，其可扩展架构支持未来功能模块的持续集成与迭代。

Abstract: Quadruped robots are capable of traversing a wide range of complex terrains with high flexibility. As highly mobile ground-based intelligent platforms, they can be equipped with modules for navigation control, environmental perception, and intelligent interaction, thereby serving as real-world mobile deployment platforms for various algorithms. In this paper, we introduce Y-BotFrame, an extensible embodied platform that turns a robot into an intelligent ground assistant. Y-BotFrame integrates multimodal perception capabilities, including speech, vision, and LiDAR, and employs a large language model as the cognitive core for environmental understanding, contextual reasoning, and task planning. The system maps user natural-language instructions into executable embodied task units that can be carried out by the robot. Y-BotFrame supports natural interaction through voice commands and visual feedback, removing the need for a remote controller and enabling efficient human-robot collaboration. With a highly extensible framework, Y-BotFrame supports plug-and-play integration of new functional modules as well as modular upgrades and iterative development, offering a reference implementation for the real-world deployment of general-purpose, instruction-driven embodied agents.The supplementary video is available at https://xdei-group.github.io/Y-BotFrame/.

</details>


### [27] [EA-WM: Event-Aware World Models with Task-Specification Grounding for Long-Horizon Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13053)
*Kailin Wang,Haoxiang Jie,Yaoyuan Yan,Jiacheng Zhou,Zhiyou Heng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出EA-WM框架，通过在预训练视觉特征空间中增强事件预测与验证，使世界模型能够感知任务相关的事件状态（如物体移动、接触状态变化等），从而提升机器人长时程操作中规划的可解释性和任务对齐性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有预训练特征世界模型仅进行视觉或潜在空间预测，无法判断想象出的未来状态是否满足任务相关的事件条件（如物体是否移动、抽屉状态是否改变、放置谓词是否满足），缺乏关系级、谓词级且物理可解释的进展信号。

Method: EA-WM框架包含三个核心组件：1）在冻结的预训练视觉特征空间中滚动生成候选未来轨迹；2）将候选未来解码为结构化事件状态；3）使用包含任务进展、语义一致性、物理可行性和不确定性四个评分项的验证器进行筛选。该验证器引导基于采样的规划、门控候选动作，并在接触敏感的LIBERO酒架场景中从PPO生成的提议中选择最优动作。

Result: 在导航、可变形物体操作、墙壁约束操作和语言描述操作等多个研究中，EA-WM表明事件感知验证能够使特征空间世界模型更可解释且与任务进展更好地对齐。

Conclusion: 事件感知验证是提升特征空间世界模型在长时程机器人操作中实用性的有效途径，使模型不仅能预测未来状态，还能判断该状态是否符合任务目标。

Abstract: Pretrained-feature world models provide a useful substrate for robot imagination, but visual or latent prediction alone does not determine whether an imagined future satisfies task-relevant events. Long-horizon manipulation requires progress signals that are relational, predicate-level, and physically grounded: whether an object has moved, whether a drawer or contact state has changed, whether a placement predicate is satisfied, and whether a candidate future is reliable enough for execution. We introduce EA-WM, an event-aware world-model framework that augments frozen visual-feature dynamics with task-specification-grounded event prediction and verification. EA-WM rolls out candidate futures in pretrained visual-feature space, decodes them into structured event states, and scores them using task-progress, semantic-consistency, physical-feasibility, and uncertainty terms. The verifier guides sampling-based planning, gates candidate actions, and, in the contact-sensitive LIBERO wine-rack setting, selects among PPOgenerated proposals. Across navigation, deformable-object, wall-constrained, and languagedescribed manipulation studies, EA-WM shows that event-aware verification can make featurespace world models more interpretable and better aligned with task progress.

</details>


### [28] [FTP-1: A Generalist Foundation Tactile Policy Across Tactile Sensors for Contact-Rich Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13102)
*Chengbo Yuan,Zicheng Zhang,Mingjie Zhou,Wendi Chen,Yi Wang,Zhuoyang Liu,Dantong Niu,Shuo Wang,Hui Zhang,Wenkang Zhang,Yingdong Hu,Yuanqing Gong,Wanli Xing,Chuan Wen,Cewu Lu,Kaifeng Zhang,Yang Gao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FTP-1是首个跨传感器、跨本体的通用触觉基础策略，通过异构编码器将图像、阵列、状态等不同触觉信号统一为形态感知潜在标记，经共享触觉Transformer专家联合建模，在26个数据源、21种传感器、约3000小时数据上预训练，实现对新传感器的零样本迁移，在5种硬件配置上微调后成功率提升17.2%-31%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于视觉的通用机器人策略已取得显著成功，但触觉策略仍受限于固定的具体硬件和传感器配置，原因是触觉信号在不同硬件间高度异构，难以实现跨传感器泛化。

Method: 采用异构编码器将图像型、阵列型和状态型等不同触觉输入投影为统一的形态感知潜在标记，再由共享的触觉Transformer专家联合建模，在汇聚的约3000小时多源触觉操作数据上进行预训练。

Result: 在5种硬件配置的下游微调实验中，FTP-1在已见传感器上成功率提升17.2%，在两种未见触觉传感器上零样本迁移成功率达31%的提升。

Conclusion: FTP-1建立了首个统一的触觉操作基础策略基线，为后续触觉策略提供了模型层面的共享起点，证明跨传感器、跨本体的触觉预训练是可行的。

Abstract: Despite the success of vision-based generalist robotic policies, existing tactile-based policies remain tied to fixed embodiments and sensor setups. This is because tactile signals are highly heterogeneous across hardware, making cross-sensor generalization difficult. We present FTP-1,the first generalist foundation tactile policy pretrained to acquire transferable tactile manipulation abilities across diverse sensors and embodiments. FTP-1 supports varied tactile inputs, including image-, array-, and state-based signals, by using heterogeneous encoders to project them into unified morphology-aware latent tokens that are jointly modeled by a shared tactile Transformer expert. Pretrained on around 3,000 hours of tactile manipulation data aggregated from 26 data sources, spanning human and robot demonstrations across 21 sensors, FTP-1 learns tactile skills that transfer beyond the sensors seen during pretraining. Across downstream finetuning experiments spanning 5 hardware configurations, FTP-1 improves contact-rich manipulation on seen sensor setups by +17.2% and, surprisingly, transfers to two previously unseen tactile-sensor setups, achieving a +31% gain in success rate. FTP-1 establishes the first unified foundation baseline for tactile manipulation, providing future tactile policies with a shared model-level starting point. Pretrained models, datasets, training code and more visualization at https://ftp1-policy.github.io.

</details>


### [29] [Redesigning Regularization for Effective Policy Smoothing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13169)
*Taisuke Kobayashi,Naoto Yamanaka*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种改进的正则化方法，用于强化学习中策略函数的平滑。通过识别原始Lipschitz连续性正则化实现的三个缺陷并提供修复方案，该方法在多个任务和算法中实现了平滑动作与控制性能的提升，并在四足机器人sim-to-real迁移中证明其对目标速度突变具有鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原有Lipschitz连续性正则化存在理论与实现之间的差距，导致实现繁琐且平滑效果不足，而简单实现又被广泛采用。需要识别具体问题并提供适当的实现方案。

Method: 识别原始Lipschitz正则化实现的三个缺陷，逐一提供修复方案，改进正则化设计以更好地平滑策略函数。

Result: 改进后的正则化在多个任务和算法上表现良好，成功实现平滑动作的同时提升了控制性能；在四足机器人的sim-to-real强化学习中，平滑运动增强了应对目标速度突变的能力。

Conclusion: 所提出的修正正则化有效弥合了理论与实现之间的差距，提供了更充分的策略平滑效果，并验证了平滑性对sim-to-real迁移鲁棒性的积极作用。

Abstract: This paper proposes a novel regularization design to effectively smooth policy functions in reinforcement learning. While regularization that enhances ``global'' Lipschitz continuity was initially considered, it has been limited to ``local'' Lipschitz continuity due to a tradeoff between smoothness and expressiveness. However, it has become apparent that the original implementation is cumbersome and does not provide sufficient smoothing, leading to a preference for simpler implementations. This stems from a discrepancy between theory and implementation, and a more appropriate implementation can expect to facilitate smoothing. Therefore, this paper identifies three reasons why the original implementation does not function adequately and provide remedies for them. This modified regularization performs well across multiple tasks and algorithms, successfully achieving smooth motion while improving control performance. Furthermore, by applying it to sim-to-real reinforcement learning for a quadruped robot, it is demonstrated that smooth motion provides robustness against sudden changes in target velocity commands.

</details>


### [30] [Embedding ISO 10218 Safety Compliance in Robots via Control Barrier Functions for Human-Robot Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13203)
*Federico Parma,Cesare Tonola,Nicola Pedrocchi,Manuel Beschi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出两种基于控制障碍函数（CBF）的预测性安全滤波器方法（CBF约束PD安全滤波器和任务缩放SQP控制器），通过显式结合人体加速度数据预测最小人机安全距离，在满足ISO 10218安全标准的同时，相比传统SSM方法减少了63%的轨迹误差并避免了过度规避操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准SSM滤波器基于恒定人体速度等保守假设，导致最小安全距离预测不准确，引发不必要停机；需要更精确的预测性安全方法以提高人机协作效率。

Method: 提出两种方法：方法I为CBF约束的PD安全滤波器；方法II为任务缩放SQP控制器（施加空间管状约束）。两种方法均将含人体加速度数据的预测性CBF作为不等式约束集成到SQP框架中，在控制层面保证安全。

Result: 在UR10e机器人上的仿真和实机实验表明：方法II相比方法I，平均轨迹误差减少63%，避免了过度规避动作，在符合ISO 10218 SSM准则的前提下保持高任务吞吐量。

Conclusion: 所提方法通过引入人体加速度信息的前瞻性CBF约束，有效解决了传统SSM的保守性缺陷，实现了安全与效率的更好平衡。

Abstract: Human-Robot Collaboration (HRC) requires strict adherence to safety standards, such as ISO 10218, to prevent harmful interactions. Standard Speed and Separation Monitoring (SSM) filters calculate safe robotic speeds based on conservative assumptions, such as constant human velocity, which prevents accurate predictions of minimum separation distances and causes unnecessary operational halts. This paper proposes a Control Barrier Function (CBF) that explicitly incorporates human acceleration data to analytically forward-predict the minimum human-robot separation distance during a worst-case robotic stopping trajectory. To guarantee safety at the control level, this predictive CBF is integrated as an inequality constraint within a Sequential Quadratic Programming (SQP) framework. Specifically, two methods are proposed: Method I, a CBF-constrained PD safety filter; and Method II, a task-scaling SQP controller that enforces a spatial tube constraint. Simulated and real-world experiments on a UR10e robot evaluate the two proposed methods against a standard industrial SSM module baseline. Results demonstrate that Method II dynamically modulates execution speed and confines spatial deviations. Compared to Method I, Method II achieves a 63\% reduction in mean trajectory error and avoids excessive evasive manoeuvres, ensuring high task throughput while complying with ISO 10218 SSM guidelines.

</details>


### [31] [Proprioceptive-visual correspondence enables self-other distinction in humanoid robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13222)
*Yurun Chen,Tianyuan Gao,Yizhong Ge,Shikun Ban,Yizhou Wang,Hongkai Xiong,Wenjun Zeng,Wentao Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种让类人机器人无需身份标签或运动学模型，仅通过本体感觉-视觉对应学习自我-他者区分，并自举构建三维身体自我模型的方法，支持目标到达、碰撞感知规划及人机运动重定向等下游任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 类人机器人在与人类共享工作空间时缺乏区分自我与他者的能力，而这是社会智能的前提。现有方法通常需要身份标签或精确的运动学模型，限制了机器人的自主社交能力。

Method: 利用本体感觉（关节角度）与视觉（图像）之间的对应关系，无监督地学习自我-他者区分；在此基础上自举（bootstrapping）构建一个预测性自我模型，将关节配置映射到三维身体占有空间。

Result: 在多智能体场景中，系统能够可靠地识别自身，学习三维自我模型，并成功应用于目标到达、碰撞感知运动规划和人类到机器人的运动重定向任务。

Conclusion: 该方法为机器人在共享物理环境中与他人协调行动时建立身体自我表征提供了一条可行路径。

Abstract: Distinguishing self from others is a prerequisite for social intelligence, yet humanoid robots that increasingly share workspaces with humans still lack this ability. Here we show that a humanoid robot can learn self-other distinction from proprioceptive-visual correspondence, without any identity labels or kinematic models. Once established, this distinction bootstraps a predictive self-model that maps joint configurations to three-dimensional body occupancy, capturing how the robot's body changes with action. In multi-agent scenes involving humans or morphologically identical robots, the system reliably identifies itself, learns a 3D self-model, and supports downstream tasks including target reaching, collision-aware motion planning, and human-to-robot motion retargeting. Together, these results outline a route toward bodily self-representation in robots that act and coordinate alongside others in shared physical environments. Project page: https://euron-zc.github.io/humanoid-self-model/.

</details>


### [32] [WT-UMI: Tactile-based Whole-Body Manipulation via Force-Supervised Contact-Aware Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13232)
*Jaehwi Jang,Zhaoyuan Gu,Alfred Cueva,Zimeng Chai,Junjie Sheng,Thong Nguyen,Himank Galundia,Yifan Wu,Huishu Xue,Isaac Legene,Ojas Mediratta,Davin Doan,Andrew Collins,Sarah Sadegh,KyoungMok Kim,Rishita Dhalbisoi,Zun Chen,Ye Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出WT-UMI，一种可穿戴全身触觉接口，支持人体演示和人形遥操作双模式，通过力条件目标位姿校正、力监督规划器和触觉导纳控制器，在五种接触丰富的操作任务中优于四种基线策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模仿策略在处理体大变形容器和共享负载物体的全身操作时，大多仅隐式处理接触力，且不同演示源存在互补模态与固有权衡：人类演示能捕捉自然接触力但非机器人可执行动作，遥操作记录机器人动作但力调节不自然。

Method: 1）WT-UMI可穿戴触觉接口，提供触觉图像、接触力和末端位姿的精确观测；2）力条件目标位姿校正模块，从遥操作数据学习校正量；3）力监督规划器，预测末端位姿块和接触力轨迹；4）基于触觉的导纳控制器，以预测接触力为参考。

Result: 在五种接触丰富任务（可变形物体、体大刚体、人-人形协作）上，WT-UMI相比四种基线策略提升了成功率并降低了接触位置跟踪误差。

Conclusion: WT-UMI通过统一人体演示和遥操作的力感知信息，实现了更优的全身操作性能，验证了显式力建模和触觉反馈在接触丰富操作中的有效性。

Abstract: Whole-body humanoid manipulation of bulky, deformable, and shared-load objects requires distributed contact sensing and explicit force regulation, yet most imitation policies treat contact force only implicitly. On the other hand, different demonstration sources provide complementary modalities with inherent trade-offs: human demonstrations capture natural contact forces but not robot-executable actions, while teleoperation directly records robot actions but with less natural force regulation. This paper presents \textbf{WT-UMI}, a wearable whole-body tactile interface worn by human operators or mounted on humanoids, providing accurate observations of tactile images, contact forces, and end-effector poses across both human demonstration and humanoid teleoperation modes. We introduce a force-conditioned target-pose correction module that converts measured human poses into contact-aware robot targets by learning corrections from teleoperation data. To leverage the natural force interaction in human data, we propose a force-supervised planner that predicts end-effector pose chunks and contact-force trajectories. The predicted contact force serves as the reference for a tactile-based admittance controller. Across five contact-rich tasks spanning deformable objects, bulky rigid objects, and human--humanoid collaboration, WT-UMI improves success rate and reduces contact-position tracking error over four policy baselines. Our project page is available at https://wt-umi.github.io/WTUMI/.

</details>


### [33] [Humor Style Drives Laughter, Topic Shapes Acceptability: Evaluating Bilingual Personal and Political Robot-Delivered AI Jokes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13256)
*Anna-Maria Velentza,Anne-Gwenn Bosser*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究探索了机器人在大学课堂中传递AI生成笑话的效果，发现幽默类型显著影响趣味性（攻击性和亲和性幽默评分更高），笑话内容主要影响适当性（人物相关笑话优于政治笑话），语言偏好受笑话内容和参与者语言流利度影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 幽默在人类社交中至关重要，计算幽默的最新进展为将幽默融入人机交互创造了新机会，但尚不清楚幽默风格、笑话内容和语言偏好如何影响群体场景中机器人传递幽默的感知效果。

Method: 采用混合因子设计，让参与者评估机器人在大学教室中传递的AI生成笑话，考察幽默类型（亲和性、自我增强、攻击性、自我贬低）和笑话内容（人物相关 vs. 政治）对趣味性和适当性的影响，以及语言偏好。

Result: 幽默类型显著影响趣味性，攻击性和亲和性幽默评分更高；笑话内容主要影响适当性，人物相关笑话优于政治笑话；语言偏好受笑话内容和参与者自报流利度及幽默实践的共同影响。

Conclusion: 在HRI中，幽默类型和内容的选择需要根据社交场景谨慎设计，攻击性和亲和性幽默在趣味性上更有效，但人物相关笑话在适当性上更安全，同时需考虑用户的语言背景。

Abstract: Humor plays a central role in human social relationships, and recent advances in computational humor create new opportunities for integrating humor into human-robot interaction (HRI). While large language models (LLMs) can generate diverse forms of humor, it remains unclear how humor style, joke content, and language preference shape perceptions of robot-delivered humor in group settings. In this exploratory study, we employed a mixed factorial design in which participants evaluated AI-generated jokes delivered by a robot in a university classroom. We examined the effects of humor type (Affiliative, Self-Enhancing, Aggressive, Self-Defeating) and joke content (person-related vs. political) on perceived funniness and appropriateness, as well as preferred language. Results show that humor type significantly influences funniness, with Aggressive and Affiliative humor rated higher, while joke content primarily affects appropriateness, with person-related jokes preferred over political ones. Language preference was shaped by both joke content and participants' self-reported fluency and humor practices.

</details>


### [34] [See Selectively, Act Adaptively: Dual-Level Structural Decomposition for Bimanual Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13279)
*Yoon-Ji Choi,Young-Chae Son,Soo-Chul Lim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于双层结构分解的双臂操作VLA框架，通过视图选择性视觉路由器和交互感知动作混合专家模块，分别处理视觉相关性和双臂交互模式，在仿真和真实任务上显著优于单体基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单体VLA策略通过单一共享表征和动作生成路径处理多样化视觉输入和交互模式，无法分别考虑视觉相关性和双臂交互结构，导致策略学习困难。

Method: 提出双层结构分解框架：1) 视图选择性视觉路由器动态调整腕部视角贡献以强调相关视觉线索；2) 交互感知动作混合专家(MoE)将动作生成分解为协调模式和单臂模式两条通路。

Result: 在RoboTwin 2.0的6个仿真任务上平均成功率提升27.7%，在3个真实长时域任务上提升43.3%，并持续优于消融变体。

Conclusion: 联合考虑选择性视觉处理和双臂交互结构的显式分解，为鲁棒的双臂操作提供了有效的归纳偏置。

Abstract: In bimanual robotic manipulation, task-relevant visual information varies with the task stage and context, while the interaction of the two arms shifts between independent and coordinated modes, making policy learning challenging. However, existing monolithic Vision-Language-Action (VLA) policies process diverse visual inputs and interaction patterns through a single shared representation and action generation pathway, often failing to separately account for visual relevance and bimanual interaction structure. To address this issue, we propose a bimanual manipulation VLA framework based on Dual-Level Structural Decomposition. The View-Selective Visual Router dynamically adjusts wrist-view contributions to emphasize relevant visual cues, while the Interaction-Aware Action Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) decomposes action generation into coordinated and arm-wise pathways to adapt to varying bimanual interaction modes. We evaluate the proposed method on six simulated bimanual manipulation tasks in RoboTwin 2.0 and three long-horizon real-world tasks. Our model improves the overall average success rate over a monolithic baseline by 27.7% in simulation and 43.3% in real-world evaluation, while consistently outperforming single-module variants across both settings. These results demonstrate that jointly considering selective visual processing and explicit decomposition of bimanual interaction structures provides an effective inductive bias for robust bimanual manipulation.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 提出自适应各向异性虚拟夹具框架，并引入基于EMG信号的直觉控制接口，通过前臂肌肉收缩实现实时调整夹具几何形状，在精准引导与自由 repositioning 之间无缝切换。

Conclusion: 基于EMG意图推断的自适应各向异性虚拟夹具能够有效提升机器人辅助腹腔镜手术的操作性能和人机协作体验。

Abstract: In this paper, we address the development of an adaptive assistance system for robot-assisted laparoscopic surgery, specifically for delicate tasks such as Resection and Dissection. Even if Virtual Fixtures offer significant advantages for guiding a surgeon's movements, conventional Virtual Fixtures are often defined by fixed geometries, lacking the flexibility to adapt to the surgical workflow or the surgeon's immediate intent. To address these limitations, we propose a novel framework for an adaptive and anisotropic virtual fixture. In addition, we introduce an intuitive control interface that modulates the fixture's geometry in real-time based on the surgeon's intent, inferred from EMG signals. This approach allows the surgeon to dynamically expand or disengage the constraint by contracting their forearm muscles, enabling seamless transitions between precise guided motion and free repositioning of the tool. Experimental results from a pilot user study, based on a standardized surgical training task, demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed method. The system showed significant improvements in task accuracy and movement consistency, alongside a reduction in perceived cognitive load, effort, and frustration.

</details>

Conclusion: 该导电纤维制造成本极低、速度快，兼具应变和电容感知能力，具有良好柔韧性和可修复性，为低成本机器人传感提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Existing stretch and touch sensors for robots are generally expensive with respect to at least one of material costs, required manufacturing equipment, or manufacturing time. We present and experimentally characterize a conductive fiber made using only inexpensive commercial off-the-shelf parts (conductive thread at $0.07/ft, silicone tubing at $0.94/ft) and tools (loop-style needle threader at $2), which can be manufactured quickly (20 cm length in 2 minutes.) We demonstrate its use as a resistive strain sensor with three applications: Triggering a grasp in a pneumatically actuated assistive finger, sensing the pose of a pneumatically actuated robotic strap, and estimating the pose of a flexible solid. We also demonstrate that it can be used as a capacitive sensor with two applications: First, as a touch sensor which triggers a commercial robot arm to move, and second, as a near-field sensor enabling the robot arm to follow a moving hand. The capacitive sensors are knitted, showcasing the high flexibility of the fiber. We discuss methods for improving manufacturing scalability and their cost trade-offs. Finally, we demonstrate a method for repairing a cut fiber.
