### [37] [Real-Time Execution with Autoregressive Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13355)
*Sangkyu Lee,Seohyeon Park,Tackgeun You,Avi Caciularu,Idan Szpektor,Hwasup Lim,Youngjae Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文证明自回归策略通过调整分词步长和约束解码可以实现实时执行，并在性能上一致优于同级别的流匹配策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模视觉-语言-动作模型的实际部署需要实时执行，而现有实时执行工作主要聚焦于扩散策略变体，忽视了自回归策略在同步推理中较慢的推出速度问题。

Method: 通过调整分词步长（tokenization horizon）和应用约束解码来保证严格的延迟边界，从而实现多轨迹解码以最大化性能。

Result: 在仿真和真实环境中，自回归策略一致优于同级别的流匹配策略，同时显著提升了同步推理下的任务完成速度。

Conclusion: 自回归策略凭借更快的收敛性、更好的指令跟随泛化能力以及实时执行能力，仍然是具有竞争力的策略类型。

Abstract: Real-time execution, enabled by asynchronous inference that ensures both smooth action trajectories and fast reactivity, is critical for realistic deployments of large-scale Vision-Language-Action models. However, recent work on real-time execution primarily focuses on variants of diffusion policies, even though it is more critical for autoregressive policies given their slower rollout speed in synchronous inference. In contrast, we demonstrate that autoregressive policies can achieve real-time execution by adjusting the tokenization horizon and applying constrained decoding, thereby guaranteeing strict latency bounds that enable multi-trajectory decoding to maximize performance. Across simulated and real-world environments, we find that the autoregressive policy consistently outperforms its equivalent-level flow-matching policy counterpart while achieving significantly improved task completion speeds from synchronous inference. Coupled with the inherent advantages of autoregressive policies, such as faster convergence and better generalizability in instruction-following, these results confirm that autoregressive policies can remain a competitive policy type supporting real-time execution.

</details>


### [38] [GeoHAT: Geometry-Adaptive Hybrid Action Transformer for Mobile Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13394)
*Xiangyu Zhu,Renjun Wu,Luzhou Ge,Jinyan Liu,Xuesong Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GeoHAT是一个基于扩散的端到端框架，通过轻量级傅里叶空间编码器将密集3D坐标映射为几何token，并采用门控融合选择性注入视觉基础模型特征，同时使用混合全身动作解码器将手臂和基座分解为不同子空间进行动作生成，在ManiSkill-HAB基准测试中达到79.3%的平均成功率，比最强基线提升23.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有全身移动操作策略存在三个问题：1）依赖2D特征或稀疏3D表示，缺乏密集空间结构；2）将手臂和基座编码在同一动作向量中，忽略其不同的控制需求；3）现有的密集融合策略在噪声深度下会破坏预训练表示且计算开销大。因此需要一种能有效注入几何信息且兼顾不同部位控制需求的框架。

Method: GeoHAT包含三大模块：1）轻量级傅里叶空间编码器，将密集逐像素3D坐标映射为几何token，无需额外3D视觉骨干；2）基于深度有效性的逐token门控融合机制，选择性将几何token注入视觉基础模型特征；3）混合全身动作解码器，将手臂和基座分解为不同子空间，通过稀疏交叉注意力让每种动作模态关注其任务相关的视觉上下文，并使用时序因果建模捕捉帧内协调和帧间依赖。

Result: 在ManiSkill-HAB仿真基准测试中达到79.3%的平均成功率，比最强基线提升23.7%；在真实世界的多样化任务中也一致优于所有基线方法。

Conclusion: 该研究表明：几何信息应仅在可靠处注入、仅在需要处关注的原则是有效的；将手臂和基座分解为不同子空间的动作解码器能更好地处理全身移动操作的异构控制需求；轻量级傅里叶编码器可在不引入额外3D骨干的情况下有效编码密集几何信息。

Abstract: Whole-body mobile manipulation requires coordinating mobile base and manipulator under shifting viewpoints, posing challenges in geometric perception and action generation. Current policies either rely on 2D features or sparse 3D representations that lack dense spatial structure, and typically encode arm and base within one action vector that ignores their distinct control demands. Moreover, existing dense fusion strategies risk corrupting pretrained representations under noisy depth while incurring heavy computational overhead. We present GeoHAT, an end-to-end diffusion-based framework built on a simple principle: geometry should be injected only where reliable and attended to only where needed. GeoHAT employs a lightweight Fourier spatial encoder that maps dense per-pixel 3D coordinates into geometric tokens without an additional 3D vision backbone. These tokens are then selectively injected into vision foundation model features through per-token gated fusion modulated by depth validity, preserving the semantic prior while enriching spatial understanding. For action generation, a Hybrid Whole-Body Action Decoder decomposes arm and base into distinct subspaces and lets each action modality attend to its task-relevant visual context through sparse cross-attention, while causal temporal modeling captures intra-timestep coordination and inter-timestep dependencies. Experiments on the ManiSkill-HAB simulation benchmark demonstrate that GeoHAT achieves a 79.3% mean success rate, surpassing the strongest baseline by 23.7%. Furthermore, real-world experiments on diverse tasks also confirm consistent improvements over all baselines.

</details>


### [39] [GIVE: Grounding Human Gestures in Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13435)
*Pengfei Liu,Gen Li,Junqiao Fan,Boyu Ma,Jindou Jia,Yang Xiao,Jianfei Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GIVE是一种通过视觉和语义双路径增强预训练VLA模型的手势理解能力的方法，在真实HRI实验中目标识别准确率提升40%、任务成功率提升80%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前VLA模型将机器人操作视为纯文本驱动任务，忽略了手势在人机交互中的重要作用，导致语言指令模糊时意图定位不准确、操作不可靠。

Method: 通过两条互补路径融入手势信息：视觉路径在机器人观测上叠加手部骨架和指尖射线以实现显式物体定位；语义路径生成手势和任务指令的高级描述以实现鲁棒的意图定位。无需修改模型架构。

Result: 在真实HRI实验中，目标识别准确率提升40%，任务成功率提升80%，并对未见过的空间布局和不同参与者表现出强鲁棒性和泛化能力。

Conclusion: GIVE通过视觉-语义双路径增强有效将手势理解融入VLA模型，显著提升了人机交互中意图定位的准确性和操作可靠性。

Abstract: Human communication is inherently multimodal, where language is often accompanied by non-verbal cues such as gestures to convey intentions. However, current Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models treat robotic manipulation as a pure text-driven task, overlooking the important role of gestures in Human-Robot Interaction (HRI). This often leads to inaccurate intent grounding and unreliable manipulation when language instructions are ambiguous or underspecified. To address this challenge, we propose GIVE (Gesture Intent via Visual-Semantic Enhancement), an effective approach that enhances pre-trained VLA models with human gesture understanding without architectural modifications. Specifically, GIVE incorporates gesture information through two complementary pathways: a visual pathway that overlays hand skeletons and fingertip rays onto robot observations for explicit object grounding, and a semantic pathway that generates high-level descriptions of human gestures and task instructions for robust intent grounding. By jointly leveraging visual and semantic guidance, GIVE enables VLA policies to better associate gestures with manipulation behaviors and adapt to dynamic interaction intents. In real-world HRI experiments, GIVE substantially outperforms the baseline, improving target object recognition accuracy by 40% and overall task success rate by 80%, while demonstrating strong robustness and generalization to unseen spatial layouts and diverse participants.

</details>


### [40] [NavWAM: A Navigation World Action Model for Goal-Conditioned Visual Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13494)
*Daichi Azuma,Taiki Miyanishi,Koya Sakamoto,Shuhei Kurita,Yaonan Zhu,Petr Khrapchenkov,Motoaki Kawanabe,Yusuke Iwasawa,Yutaka Matsuo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出NavWAM，一种将世界模型预测与动作执行端到端结合的扩散Transformer策略，通过联合学习未来观测、目标进度值和动作块，实现视觉前瞻直接驱动闭环控制，在图像目标导航任务中优于基于规划的世界模型基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有导航世界模型虽然能预测未来视觉，但仅作为独立预测模块，需要外部规划器将预测转化为闭环控制，导致预测与动作决策之间存在鸿沟。

Method: 采用扩散Transformer架构，将未来观测、目标进度值和动作块编码为共享潜在序列，联合学习预测与动作/价值目标；通过仿真预训练+真机适配两阶段训练。

Result: 在离线基准测试和闭环真机部署中，NavWAM在默认策略模式下（无需CEM动作搜索）优于基于规划的世界模型基线和代表性直接导航策略。

Conclusion: 将世界模型预测与动作执行端到端联合学习，能够使视觉前瞻直接服务于机器人控制，提升导航性能并简化系统复杂性。

Abstract: Goal-conditioned visual navigation requires a robot to act under partial observability by anticipating how its motion will change the future egocentric view and whether that change brings it closer to the goal. Navigation world models provide such visual foresight, but they remain prediction modules that require an external planner to convert predicted futures into closed-loop control. We propose Navigation World Action Model (NavWAM), a diffusion-transformer policy that turns navigation world-model prediction into executable action by representing future observations, goal-progress values, and action chunks in a shared latent sequence. By learning future prediction jointly with the action and value targets that determine closed-loop behavior, NavWAM makes visual foresight directly usable for robot control. We build NavWAM through simulation pretraining and real-robot adaptation, and evaluate it on image-goal navigation against planning-based world models and a representative direct navigation policy. Across offline benchmarks and closed-loop real-robot deployment, NavWAM improves over planning-based world-model baselines in our evaluations while using the default policy mode without CEM-style action search. Project page: https://dachii-azm.github.io/navwam/

</details>


### [41] [SPARC: Reliable Spatial Annotations from Robot Demonstrations at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13497)
*Nils Blank,Paul Mattes,Maximilian Xiling Li,Jakub Suliga,Thomas Roth,Moritz Reuss,Pankhuri Vanjani,Rudolf Lioutikov*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SPARC 是一个风险感知框架，能自动为机器人演示生成带有可靠性校准的结构化空间标注，在保留更多有用样本的同时显著降低噪声标签，并在物体定位、空间推理和真实世界策略执行上优于现有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动化标注流水线虽能大规模生成结构化空间标注，但检测器置信度与标注正确性之间存在校准不良的问题，迫使研究者不得不在接受噪声标签或丢弃有用样本之间做出取舍。

Method: SPARC 利用机器人任务固有的时空结构生成可靠性信号，为每个空间标注分配校准后的可靠性分数；同时引入 IA-Bench 基准来评估模型在机器人演示中定位交互物体位置的准确性。

Result: 在涵盖多种实体和场景的 1.7k 人工标注演示上，SPARC 在定位精度上显著优于纯检测基线，并在高精度操作点保留了 3 倍以上的样本；微调后的模型在物体定位和指代基准上达到同类模型 SOTA，在真实世界杂乱场景中策略表现也优于基线。

Conclusion: SPARC 有效解决了自动化标注中的质量控制问题，无需人工验证或标注训练数据即可实现高性能的空间推理和机器人策略学习。

Abstract: This work introduces Spatial Annotations from Robot Demonstrations with Reliability Calibration (SPARC), a risk-aware framework that automatically labels robot demonstrations with structured spatial annotations and assigns each annotation a reliability score. Structured spatial annotations, such as bounding boxes, object trajectories, and manipulation phase labels, benefit a broad range of robotics applications from training grounded robot policies and embodied foundation models to motion planning and hierarchical task composition. Existing automated pipelines generate such annotations at scale but provide no reliable quality signal: detector confidence is poorly calibrated for annotation correctness, forcing a choice between accepting noisy labels or discarding useful samples. In contrast to existing automated pipelines, SPARC leverages the spatio-temporal structure inherent to robot tasks to generate a reliability signal, reducing noisy labels and retaining more useful samples. We further introduce Interaction-Aware Bench (IA-Bench), a benchmark that measures model accuracy in grounding the locations of interacted objects in robot demonstrations. On 1.7k human-annotated demonstrations spanning diverse embodiments and scenarios, SPARC significantly outperforms detection-only baselines in localization accuracy while retaining three times more samples at high-precision operating points. Our experiments demonstrate that models finetuned on our annotations achieve state-of-the-art results on object-grounding and pointing benchmarks among similarly sized models, while remaining competitive on broader spatial-reasoning suites without manually verified or annotated training data. Furthermore, policies trained on SPARC-generated annotations outperform baselines in cluttered, visually ambiguous real-world scenes. Code, data, and models are available at intuitive-robots.github.io/sparc-labeling.

</details>


### [42] [$\texttt{WEAVER}$, Better, Faster, Longer: An Effective World Model for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13672)
*Arnav Kumar Jain,Yilin Wu,Jesse Farebrother,Gokul Swamy,Andrea Bajcsy*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出WEAVER，一种多视角世界模型架构，通过流匹配损失预测未来潜变量和奖励值，同时满足高保真度、长程一致性和高效率三个期望，在机器人操作任务中实现了SOTA结果，在策略评估（ρ=0.870）、策略改进（成功率提升38%）和测试时规划（成功率提升14%，速度提升5-10倍）方面均表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界模型在机器人领域的潜力巨大（策略评估、策略改进、测试时规划），但现有方法难以同时满足三个关键期望：保真度（模拟轨迹与真实世界相关）、一致性（长程轨迹连贯性）和效率（快速生成模拟轨迹）。本文旨在设计一种能够同时实现这三个目标的世界模型架构。

Method: WEAVER是一种多视角世界模型架构，采用流匹配损失（flow-matching loss）来预测未来潜在表征和奖励值。模型设计围绕三个关键维度进行优化：模型架构、记忆机制和预测目标，以处理长程动态操作任务。

Result: 在机器人硬件上验证：策略评估相关系数ρ=0.870；在π0.5机器人基础模型基础上策略改进实现38%的真实世界成功率提升；测试时规划实现14%成功率提升且速度比之前的世界模型快5-10倍；在分布外场景中表现优于先前方法。

Conclusion: WEAVER通过精心设计的架构决策，首次同时实现了世界模型所需的保真度、一致性和效率三个期望，为机器人操作任务中的策略评估、改进和规划提供了有效且高效的工具。

Abstract: The potential impacts of world models (WMs, i.e., learned simulators) on robotics are far-reaching -- policy evaluation, policy improvement, and test-time planning -- all with limited real-world interaction. To unlock these downstream capabilities, a WM needs to jointly satisfy three desiderata: $\textit{(i)}$ fidelity (i.e., producing simulated trajectories that correlate with reality), $\textit{(ii)}$ consistency (i.e., producing simulated trajectories that are coherent over long horizons), and $\textit{(iii)}$ efficiency (i.e., producing simulated trajectories quickly). We propose $\texttt{WEAVER}$ (World Estimation Across Views for Embodied Reasoning): a WM architecture that simultaneously achieves all three desiderata, providing state-of-the-art results on robotic manipulation tasks. $\texttt{WEAVER}$ is a multi-view WM trained to predict future latents and reward values via a flow-matching loss. We distill the key design decisions across model architecture, memory, and prediction objectives required to unlock the kinds of long-horizon dynamic manipulation tasks that have confounded prior world modeling approaches. We apply $\texttt{WEAVER}$ in robotic hardware, demonstrating its effectiveness at policy evaluation ($ρ$=0.870 correlation with real-world success rate), policy improvement (real-world success rate improvement of $38\%$ on top of the $π_{0.5}$ robot foundation model), and test-time planning (real-world success rate improvement of $14\%$ with a $5-10\times$ speedup over prior WMs). $\texttt{WEAVER}$ also demonstrates better performance than prior WMs when evaluated on out-of-distribution scenarios. Code, models, and videos at: https://arnavkj1995.github.io/WEAVER/ .

</details>


### [43] [Improving Robotic Generalist Policies via Flow Reversal Steering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13675)
*Andy Tang,William Chen,Andrew Wagenmaker,Chelsea Finn,Sergey Levine*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Flow Reversal Steering (FRS)方法，利用流匹配通用策略的逆向过程，将次优动作映射到通用策略的高质量动作模式中，从而提升零样本控制、知识蒸馏和强化学习引导等任务表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用策略虽然能从多样化数据中学习广泛技能，但在解决新任务时，直接指令调用往往失败，需要一种能从策略丰富的行为先验中推断并调用正确动作的方法。

Method: FRS方法：获取次优但"合理"的动作，通过流策略逆向过程找到其潜在噪声，然后将这些噪声映射到邻近的通用策略动作模式上。

Result: 在模拟和真实操作环境中，FRS将粗粒度语义引导转化为优质机器人动作，提升零样本控制；通过行为克隆蒸馏，在不到一分钟训练内实现高达95%的绝对任务成功率提升；引导强化学习在标准RL无法改进的任务上取得进步。

Conclusion: FRS是一种有效利用通用策略行为先验的方法，能够改善零样本控制、知识蒸馏和策略改进，尤其适用于直接策略指令失败的情况。

Abstract: Generalist policies can learn a wide range of skills from diverse robot datasets. In order to solve or improve on challenging news tasks, we need a way to infer and invoke the appropriate actions from the policy's rich behavioral prior, especially when directly commanding the policy fails. We focus on flow matching generalists and propose Flow Reversal Steering (FRS): a method that takes suboptimal but ``reasonable'' actions, finds their latent noises by passing them through the flow policy in reverse, and maps them to nearby generalist action modes. We evaluate FRS across many simulated and real-world manipulation settings. First, FRS can turn coarse semantic guidance from humans or vision-language models (VLMs) into corresponding good robot actions, improving zero-shot control. These gains can be distilled with behavioral cloning by training an auxiliary policy to output noises that the generalist maps to good actions -- showing up to 95% absolute task success rate boosts in under a minute of training. Finally, FRS enables policy improvement by bootstrapping reinforcement learning with semantic knowledge, improving on several tasks that standard RL fails to improve on.

</details>


### [44] [Mana: Dexterous Manipulation of Articulated Tools](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13677)
*Zhao-Heng Yin,Guanya Shi,Pieter Abbeel,C. Karen Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Mana是一个受计算机动画启发的通用仿真到现实框架，通过粗到细的管线将程序化生成的关键帧转换为操作轨迹，实现了零样本迁移的灵巧关节工具操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 关节工具操作因需要协调内部自由度和接触丰富的交互而具有物理复杂性和学习难度，现有工作多聚焦于刚体物体，关节工具操作尚未得到充分探索。

Method: 提出Mana框架，受计算机动画启发，采用粗到细管线：通过程序化生成抓取关键帧，再结合运动规划和强化学习生成操作轨迹；数据生成过程自动化，仅需少量鼠标点击指定功能可供性。

Result: 在四种不同尺度和关节类型的工具上，Mana实现了抓取和手内操作的零样本仿真到现实迁移。

Conclusion: Mana展示了一种可扩展的灵巧关节工具操作方法，以动画视角重新诠释灵巧操作问题，为关节工具的通用操作提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Articulated tool manipulation remains a major challenge in dexterous robotics due to the need to coordinate internal degrees of freedom and contact-rich interactions. While prior work has largely focused on rigid objects, articulated tool use remains underexplored because of its physical complexity and the difficulty of learning functional grasping and manipulation policies. We present Mana (Manipulation Animator), a general sim-to-real framework that reinterprets dexterous manipulation as an animation problem. Inspired by computer animation, Mana employs a coarse-to-fine pipeline that transforms procedurally-generated grasp keyframes into manipulation trajectories through motion planning and reinforcement learning. The data generation process is largely automatic, requiring only a few mouse clicks to specify functional affordances (<1 minute per tool). Across four articulated tools spanning different scales and joint types, Mana achieves zero-shot sim-to-real transfer for both grasping and in-hand manipulation, demonstrating a scalable approach to dexterous articulated tool use.

</details>

### [45] [OpenRoundup: Multi-Table Data Wrangling Through Interactive Visualization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12648)
*Stephen Kasica,Charles Berret,Tamara Munzner*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: OpenRoundup是一个开源、基于浏览器的数据整理系统，支持数据记者通过无代码界面整合多个表格，采用DuckDB-WASM客户端架构保护数据隐私，通过Stack和Pack两种操作实现表格合并，并在复制研究和部署研究中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有交互式数据整理工具以单表为处理单元，但数据记者经常需要整合多个独立发布的表格进行问责报道，缺乏以多表集合为基本工作单元的工具。

Method: 提出开放式整合系统OpenRoundup，包含五个协调面板实现schema优先、按需取值范式；引入两个概念贡献：早期表格整合（在整理阶段早期通过交互式增量组装构建复合表）和声明式整合词汇表（Stack和Pack两种操作）；采用DuckDB-WASM客户端架构在浏览器中运行。

Result: 复制研究成功复现了17个已发表的记者编程工作流；部署研究表明该工具对理解连接概念但缺乏编程技能的专业记者有用，并发现了其在数据新闻教育中的意外价值。

Conclusion: OpenRoundup有效覆盖了现实世界中的表格整合任务，为缺少编程技能的数据记者提供了可用的整合工具，并在数据新闻教育方面展现出额外价值。

Abstract: Data journalists routinely integrate records across multiple independently published sources to support accountability reporting, yet no existing interactive wrangling tool treats the collection of tables -- rather than the single table -- as its primary unit of work. We present OpenRoundup, an open-source, browser-based system that enables data journalists to consolidate multiple tables into a single analysis-ready output without writing code. The interface comprises five coordinated panels that implement a schema-first, values-on-demand paradigm with live schema previews, ambient data quality alerts, and a recursive treemap visualization of the evolving operation tree. A client-only architecture powered by DuckDB-WASM runs in the browser, providing strong data privacy guarantees suited to sensitive journalism data. The system introduces two conceptual contributions: eager table consolidation, in which a composite table is assembled early in the wrangling phase via interactive, incremental assembly of multiple source tables; and a declarative vocabulary for table consolidation consisting of two operations, Stack and Pack. We evaluate the system through a replication study in which the authors reproduce 17 published journalist programming workflows using only the interface, and a deployment study with four professional data journalists. The replication study demonstrates expressive coverage of real-world consolidation tasks. The deployment study confirms utility for practitioners who understand joins conceptually but lack the programming skills to execute them, and surfaces an unanticipated secondary value for data journalism education.

</details>


### [46] [A Multiplexing Design Space: Theory, Method, and Application](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12719)
*Yiwen Xing,Afrah Farea,Saiful Khan,Min Chen*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在受限应用空间中探索视觉复用（visual multiplexing）设计空间的系统方法，并以机器学习工作流中多个2D标量场的关系分析为案例，通过三步设计流程和预设计步骤，识别出相对最优的默认复用设计及领域专家可控的细微变体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉复用现象在设计中被广泛但非系统地使用，设计空间庞大，缺乏针对特定应用约束的系统性探索方法。

Method: 提出一种约束于具体应用的设计方法，包含预设计阶段（领域基础与理论分析）和三步设计流程，结合领域专家的协同设计与评估活动。

Result: 识别出相对最优的默认复用设计方案，以及领域专家可通过用户界面控制的细微变体。

Conclusion: 该设计方法能够有效缩小视觉复用的搜索空间，在机器学习PDE模型开发场景中产出实用且可控的设计模式。

Abstract: Many visualization designs feature phenomena referred to as ``visual multiplexing'', where multiple pieces of information associated with the same data point are conveyed simultaneously. Although visualization designers are able to bring such phenomena, often unconsciously, into their designs, the design space of visual multiplexing is huge, and it is uncommon to explore visual multiplexing systematically as design patterns. In this paper, we propose a design method for exploring a smaller design space constrained by an application. As an illustrative case study, we focus on machine learning (ML) workflows for developing ML models that approximate partial differential equations (PDEs). In these workflows, ML researchers need to analyze the inter-relationships among multiple 2D scalar fields frequently. Since superimposing one heatmap on top of another is not an effective design, we formulate three design steps to explore the design space of visual multiplexing in the context of multiple 2D scalar fields. Our design method also includes a pre-design step for domain grounding and theoretical analysis, and involves domain experts in both co-design and evaluation activities. The design process enables us to identify relatively optimal default multiplexing designs as well as the need for small variations that domain experts can control through a user interface.

</details>


### [47] [Exploring How Agent Voice Accents Shape Human-AI Collaboration in K-12 Group Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12805)
*Prerna Ravi,Carúmey Stevens,Ben Hurt,Brandon Hanks,Grace Lin,Emma Anderson*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文通过33名教师的混合方法实验，研究GenAI语音代理的不同口音（英式、印度式、非裔美式）如何影响小组协作中的参与者感知、信任与互动动态。发现英式口音的代理被视为工具，而印度式和非裔美式口音的代理更易被拟人化并被接受为同伴。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 协作是21世纪教育的核心，但教师仍面临促进有效同伴互动的挑战。LLM对话同伴代理为调解小组协作提供了新可能，然而代理的口音特征如何影响小组动态尚不明确，现有研究多局限于一对一场景。

Method: 采用组间混合方法研究，以33名教师为被试，考察不同口音（英式、印度式、非裔美式）的GenAI语音代理对协作和代理感知的影响。通过问卷、小组互动分析和人工制品等多源数据进行分析。

Result: 口音显著影响参与者的心智模型及代理在小组互动中扮演的角色：英式口音代理被视作工具，以疏离、实用方式使用；印度式和非裔美式口音代理更易被拟人化并被整合为同伴。这些角色期望进一步影响了信任、参与度和依赖程度。

Conclusion: 本研究推进了学界对GenAI社会语言学设计特征如何塑造CSCL中小组动态的理解，为设计具有文化包容性的AI学习伙伴提供了启示。

Abstract: Collaboration is widely recognized as a cornerstone of 21st-century education, yet teachers still encounter persistent challenges in fostering productive peer interaction. LLM conversational peer agents introduce new possibilities for mediating in-person group work, raising questions about how persona design, particularly their voice characteristics, shapes learners' perceptions, trust, and interactional dynamics. While prior work has examined agent accent effects in one-to-one settings, little is known about how these effects manifest in groups. We conducted a between-subjects mixed-methods study with 33 teachers examining how a GenAI voice agent with different accents (British, Indian, and African American) influenced collaboration and agent perception. Across surveys, group interaction analyses, and artifacts, we find that accent shaped participants' mental models and the roles the agent assumed in group interaction. The British-accented agent was largely treated as a tool and engaged in detached, utility-based ways, whereas Indian- and African American-accented agents were more readily anthropomorphized and integrated as peers. These role expectations influenced trust, engagement, and reliance over time. This work advances understanding of how GenAI's sociolinguistic design features shape group dynamics in CSCL, with implications for designing culturally inclusive AI partners in group learning.

</details>


### [48] [From Prompts to Preferences: An Open-Source Platform for Generative AI-Enhanced Conjoint Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12972)
*Philipp Brauner*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个基于生成式AI、可自托管运行的开源联合分析调查平台，支持文本和视觉刺激生成，并提供向导式设计、AI辅助属性建议和实时数据分析功能，并通过一项关于养老护理机器人偏好的验证研究（N=55）展示了其可用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联合分析工具要么昂贵，要么缺乏端到端调查基础设施，导致无商业平台访问权限的研究者面临显著障碍。作者旨在提供一个低成本、开源且功能完整的替代方案。

Method: 构建开源自托管Web应用：使用大语言模型生成文本场景描述，使用文生图模型生成视觉刺激；通过参数化基础提示词和可选的面注释实现生成；设计结构化设置向导、AI辅助属性建议和实时数据分析模块；提供包含所有刺激、提示词和响应数据的完整导出包。

Result: 通过一项关于环境辅助生活（AAL）中护理机器人偏好的验证研究（N=55）展示了平台有效性，该研究使用了AI生成的视觉刺激。

Conclusion: AI辅助可以降低联合分析的技术门槛，但理论根基仍须由研究者负责；生成式AI产生的刺激形式可以拓展人机交互及相关领域的方法学工具集。

Abstract: Conjoint analysis is a widely used preference measurement method in marketing research, political science, healthcare, and human-computer interaction. Despite broad adoption, researchers without access to commercial platforms face significant barriers, as existing tools are either expensive or lack end-to-end survey infrastructure. This paper presents an open-source, self-hosted web application for designing, deploying, and analysing conjoint surveys. Beyond conventional tabular stimuli, the platform uses generative AI to produce integrated stimuli formats: textual scenario descriptions generated by a large language model, and visual stimuli by a text-to-image model. A researcher-defined base prompt is parameterised with the conjoint profile, and optional LLM-facing level annotations enrich the generation. A structured setup wizard, AI-assisted attribute suggestion, and live data analysis lower the technical barriers for researchers new to conjoint methodology. A full export bundle including all stimuli, their generating prompts, and response data facilitates transparency and reproducibility. The platform is demonstrated through a proof-of-concept study on care robot preferences for ambient assisted living (AAL, N=55) using AI-generated visual stimuli. The paper discusses the role of AI assistance in conjoint design, arguing that theoretical grounding must remain the researcher's responsibility, and outlining how genAI-generated stimuli can broaden the methodological repertoire for HCI and related fields.

</details>


### [49] [Mod-Guide: An LLM-based Content Moderation Feedback System to Address Insensitive Speech toward Indigenous Ethnic and Religious Minority Communities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13397)
*Dipto Das,Achhiya Sultana,Ankit Singh Chauhan,Saadia Binte Alam,Mohammad Shidujaman,Shion Guha,Sunandan Chakraborty,Syed Ishtiaque Ahmed*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文研究了LLM内容审核系统在识别针对少数群体（孟加拉国印度教和查克马社区）的文化不敏感言论方面的局限性，并通过检索增强生成（RAG）技术将社区叙事融入审核流程，开发了Mod-Guide工具。混合方法评估表明，RAG增强的审核回应更具语境准确性，且不同族群感知存在差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM内容审核系统可能无法识别文化不敏感言论（通过隐性抹除、误述或规范性框架而非公开敌意实施的边缘化），缺乏对少数群体文化宗教视角的理解，需要探索纳入少数群体视角的方法。

Method: 与孟加拉国印度教和查克马社区成员共同创建了文化基础的不敏感言论语料库，利用检索增强生成（RAG）将社区叙事整合到审核流程中，开发了Mod-Guide工具，并通过少数群体和多数群体参与者进行混合方法评估。

Result: RAG增强的审核回应比传统LLM审核更具语境准确性；不同族群（少数群体与多数群体）对审核回应的感知存在显著差异。

Conclusion: 本研究通过将恢复性正义和诠释学包容置于内容审核系统设计的核心，推进了人机交互、AI伦理和社会计算领域的研究。

Abstract: Language operates as a mechanism of both marginalization and resistance, especially for minority communities navigating insensitive and harmful speech online. As content moderation increasingly depends on large language models (LLMs), concerns arise about whether these systems can recognize culturally insensitive speech-language that disregards or marginalizes the cultural and religious perspectives of historically underrepresented communities, often through implicit erasure, misrepresentation, or normative framing, rather than overt hostility. Focusing on Bangladesh's Hindu and Chakma communities -- the country's largest religious and Indigenous ethnic minorities, respectively -- this paper investigates the epistemic limits of LLM-based moderation systems and explores methods for incorporating minority perspectives. We co-created a culturally grounded corpus of insensitive speech with community members and integrated their narratives into moderation pipelines using retrieval augmented generation (RAG). Our tool, Mod-Guide, improves LLM sensitivity to minority viewpoints by leveraging contextual cues derived from lived experience. Through mixed-method evaluations involving both minority and majority participants, we demonstrate that RAG-enhanced moderation responses are more contextually accurate and perceived differently across ethnic lines. This work advances research in human-computer interaction, AI ethics, and social computing by foregrounding restorative justice and hermeneutical inclusion in the design of content moderation systems.

</details>


### [50] [Ride, Track, and Recover: Pilot Randomized Trial of a Wearable Digital Self-Management Intervention During a Veteran Endurance-Cycling Program](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13529)
*Alan Ta,Nilsu Salgin,Caleb Armstrong,Kala Phillips Reindel,Farzan Sasangohar*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 通过智能手表检测高唤醒事件并结合数字化自我管理工具，相比仅进行身体活动，能更有效地稳定退伍军人的PTSD症状，但个体差异显著，提示需要个性化设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 退伍军人的PTSD常伴随持续高唤醒及共病焦虑抑郁症状，且难以在临床环境外监测和管理。

Method: 将13名参与德州骑行活动的退伍军人随机分为数字化干预+身体活动组和仅身体活动组，另设7名居家监测对照组。使用智能手表连续感测心率和加速度特征检测高唤醒事件，并由参与者实时确认，同时每周收集焦虑、抑郁和PTSD严重程度的自我报告数据，采用广义加性混合模型分析非线性轨迹。

Result: 数字化干预组的高唤醒轨迹呈现结构化稳定，而仅身体活动组在研究后期出现恶化；两组在耐力活动中均有急性症状改善，但数字化干预组维持效果更佳；居家对照组症状逐渐下降；ML检测精度与症状严重程度正相关。

Conclusion: 可穿戴检测与数字化自我管理工具的结合可能支持高唤醒的稳定和症状改善，同时强调了在可穿戴心理健康系统中个性化和人本设计的重要性。

Abstract: Post-traumatic stress disorder (PTSD) in veterans is characterized by persistent hyperarousal and comorbid anxiety and depressive symptoms that are difficult to monitor and manage outside clinical settings. Thirteen veterans participating in a Project Hero cycling event in Texas were randomized by computer-generated sequence in a naturalistic setting to two arms: (1) digital intervention plus physical activity, or (2) physical activity only, plus a third at-home monitoring control cohort consisting of 7 veterans selected from the broader Project Hero veteran community. Continuous smartwatch sensing combined heart rate and accelerometer features to detect hyperarousal events, which were confirmed in real time by participants. Weekly self-report measures of anxiety, depression, and PTSD severity were collected. Generalized additive mixed models characterized nonlinear trajectories over time. Baseline-normalized hyperarousal trajectories differed significantly across conditions, with the digital intervention group (n=7) showing structured stabilization compared to late-study escalation in the physical-only group (n=3). Both cycling groups exhibited acute symptom improvements during the endurance event; however, the digital intervention group demonstrated a higher overall maintenance of gains. The at-home control group (n=4) showed gradual symptom declines. Perceived precision of ML detections varied substantially across individuals and was positively associated with symptom severity, with higher-severity participants confirming a greater proportion of detected events. These results suggest that coupling wearable detection with digital self-management tools may support stabilization of hyperarousal and symptom improvement while emphasizing the importance of personalization and human-centered design in wearable mental health systems.

</details>

### [51] [A catalog of fast matrix multiplication algorithms with frontier-closure search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13408)
*Benoit Chatain Lacelle*

Main category: cs.SC

TL;DR: 本文构建了涵盖多种代数域、形状至32×32×32的统一可机器验证的低秩矩阵乘法方案目录，通过前沿闭合搜索重组已知核心，并提出了“非重叠属性”以划清“发现新双线性核心”与“组合已知核心”之间的界限，解决了文献中的归属争议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近年来小规模矩阵乘法爆发式进展（AlphaTensor、AlphaEvolve等）产生了大量分散在不同领域、署名惯例和序列化格式中的优秀结果，缺乏统一整理；同时Perminov的开源翻转图框架进一步发现了更多低秩方案，亟需一个统一目录来整合这些成果并理清归属关系。

Method: 1）构建涵盖有理数、整数、实数、复数、二元域及交换算法轴线的统一机器可验证目录；2）采用前沿闭合搜索（frontier-closure search）进行目录条目的重组，包含轴翻转、Kronecker积、轴拼接、意外乘积、带分配的重组（可选输出剥离和配对融合）以及向下投影；3）提出非重叠属性（non-overlap property），确保重组不会重新发现共享双线性乘积（如Strassen、Laderman等手工构造）。

Result: 建立了覆盖形状至32×32×32的统一低秩方案目录，刷新了DIS09对比表，按域和交换性列拆分，并提供了自动再生工具。明确了“发现更巧妙双线性核心”与“组合已知核心”两条进展路径的边界。

Conclusion: 通过系统性重组方法统一了分散的矩阵乘法低秩成果，解决了文献中的归属争议，并为目录的持续演化提供了工具支持。

Abstract: The 2022--2026 burst of activity in small-format matrix multiplication (AlphaTensor 2022, AlphaEvolve 2025, Schwartz--Zwecher 2025) has produced striking individual results but scattered them across different fields, attribution conventions, and serialisation formats. A complementary line of work -- Perminov's open-source flip-graph framework~\cite{perminov2026fast,perminov2025fast} -- instead drives existing construction methods, notably flip-graph and \emph{meta-flip-graph} search, at scale across large format spaces, discovering many new low-rank schemes (including ternary-integer ones) that further enrich the landscape this catalog must unify. We present a unified, machine-checkable catalog covering shapes up to \nmpshape{32}{32}{32} over \Rationals, \Integers, \Reals, \Complex, and \Ftwo, with a separate axis for commutative algorithms (Waksman 1970, Makarov 1986, Rosowski 2019).
  Derivation over this catalog is performed by a \emph{frontier-closure search} that recombines catalog entries by axis-flip, Kronecker, axis concatenation, serendipitous products, recombination-with-allocation (with optional output peeling and pair fusion), and downward projection. A central methodological point is the \emph{non-overlap property}: our recombination does not, and cannot, rediscover the shared bilinear products that hand-crafted constructions (Strassen, Laderman, Smirnov, AlphaTensor) are built around. This draws a clean line between the ``find a cleverer bilinear core'' and ``compose known cores'' axes of progress, and resolves several attribution puzzles in the literature. We refresh the DIS09 comparison tables, split per field and with a commutative column, and provide the tooling to regenerate them automatically as the catalog evolves.

</details>

### [52] [Mixed-Categorical Black-Box Optimization via Information-Geometric Bilevel Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12885)
*Marc Ong,Shinichi Shirakawa,Youhei Akimoto*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 针对混合类别-连续优化中变量间强交互导致现有CMA-ES方法性能下降的问题，本文提出一种双层优化框架，外层优化类别变量、内层优化连续变量，并通过信息几何随机松弛和暖启动策略提升效率，实验表明该方法在交互处理能力和计算效率上均优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于进化策略（如CMA-ES）的混合变量优化方法假设类别变量与连续变量独立，导致在变量间存在强交互时性能严重下降，亟需一种能显式建模这种交互关系的方法。

Method: 提出双层优化框架：外层循环优化类别变量，内层循环在每个类别配置下优化连续变量；两层均采用信息几何优化下的随机松弛形式；引入暖启动策略，通过选择缓存中最佳配置并迭代更新缓存来加速内层搜索。

Result: 在二元-连续变量域的基准测试中，所提方法在交互处理能力上优于现有最先进方法，同时在包含已知和新型交互类型的多个基准上计算效率更高。

Conclusion: 双层优化框架能有效捕获混合变量间的交互关系，结合暖启动策略缓解了双层优化的高计算成本问题，在性能和效率上均达到更优。

Abstract: Mixed categorical-continuous optimization arises in many practical domains, yet remains challenging. In the black-box setting, evolution strategy-based approaches have shown promise in extending the efficiency and robustness of the CMA-ES to mixed-variable spaces. However, these methods exhibit worsened performance when strong categorical-continuous interactions are present, as their underlying search distributions assume independence between categorical and continuous variables. To address this limitation, we propose a bilevel optimization framework that explicitly captures such interactions by optimizing over categorical variables in an outer loop, and over continuous variables conditioned on each categorical configuration in an inner loop. We formulate each level of the bilevel problem as a stochastic relaxation under information-geometric optimization. To mitigate the high computational cost inherent to bilevel optimization, we introduce a warm-starting strategy that accelerates the lower-level search by selecting the best among multiple cached configurations and updating the cache after each iteration. Experimental results on binary-continuous domain demonstrate that the proposed method outperforms existing state-of-the-art approaches in interaction-handling capability while also being more computationally efficient across benchmarks encompassing both previously reported and newly proposed types of interaction.

</details>


### [53] [Multi-Objective Coevolution of Prompts and Templates for Circuit Approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13089)
*Martin Tomasovic,Lukas Sekanina*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种协同进化算法，利用现成的大语言模型（LLM）自动设计优化的8位近似乘法器，无需领域特定训练，通过同时演化候选电路种群和提示模板种群，在多个设计目标下实现了比EvoApproxLib库中高度优化电路更优的误差-面积权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近似乘法器通过放宽计算精度来换取功耗、延迟和面积上的收益，适用于神经网络等容错应用。然而，手动设计优化的近似乘法器耗时且需要专业知识，现有自动化方法可能无法充分探索设计空间。因此，需要一种无需领域特定训练、能自动发现更优近似乘法器设计的方法。

Method: 提出一种协同进化算法，结合现成的大语言模型（LLM）自动设计8位近似乘法器。该方法同时维护两个进化种群：候选电路种群（表示具体乘法器设计）和提示模板种群（表示指导LLM进行电路修改的指令模板）。在每一代中，使用提示模板引导LLM对候选电路进行变异操作，通过协同进化机制使两类种群共同优化。

Result: 在多个目标设计目标下的实验结果显示，该方法发现的近似乘法器在误差-面积权衡上优于EvoApproxLib库中高度优化的电路。

Conclusion: 该协同进化方法能够有效利用现成LLM的生成能力自动化设计高性能近似乘法器，无需领域特定训练，为利用LLM辅助硬件设计自动化提供了一种可行范式。

Abstract: Approximate multipliers deliberately relax computational accuracy to achieve gains in power efficiency, latency, and silicon area, which makes them well-suited for error-resilient applications such as neural networks. In this work, we introduce a co-evolutionary algorithm that leverages an off-the-shelf large language model (LLM) without requiring domain-specific training to automate the design of optimized 8-bit approximate multipliers. The approach simultaneously evolves a population of candidate circuits and a population of prompt templates that steer LLM-driven modifications. Experimental results for several target design objectives demonstrate that the proposed method discovers approximate multipliers with improved error-area trade-offs compared to highly optimized circuits from the EvoApproxLib library.

</details>


### [54] [Runtime Analysis of the $(μ+ 1)$-ES in a Homogenous Progress Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13323)
*Johannes Lengler,Raghu Raman Ravi*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于研究进化策略(ES)适应度进展的简化模型，该模型绕过底层适应度景观，假设突变个体的适应度相对于父代由不变分布Z决定。作者严格分析了连续稳态(μ+1)-ES在该模型中的期望增长率R_μ，通过构建改进过程来夹逼原始过程的增长率，并在Z为均值偏移高斯分布且μ≤e^δ的条件下给出了R_μ与R_1的关系式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 进化策略的适应度进展分析通常因底层适应度景观复杂而难以处理。现有方法在处理重叠世代的(μ+1)-ES时面临复杂的数学依赖关系，需要一种简化模型来近似优化过程，特别是当进化算法远离全局最优时。

Method: 提出一个简化模型，假设突变个体的相对适应度由不变分布Z决定（如均值偏移高斯分布）。通过构造改进过程来夹逼原始(μ+1)-ES过程的增长率，使得改进过程更易分析但仍能给出紧密的期望增长率上界。

Result: 当Z = N(-δ, 1)且μ ≤ e^δ时，证明(μ+1)-ES的期望增长率满足R_μ = (log^{1+o(1)} μ)/μ * R_1。

Conclusion: 该简化模型为分析进化策略在复杂优化问题（如机器学习超参数调优）中的行为提供了可处理的分析框架，并给出了(μ+1)-ES策略增长率与种群规模之间的定量关系。

Abstract: We introduce a new simple model to study the fitness progress of Evolution Strategies (ES) in generic problems. In this model, we bypass the underlying fitness landscape and assume that the mutation of any individual produces an offspring whose fitness relative to the parent is given by an invariant distribution $Z$, such as a mean-shifted Gaussian. This serves as a prototypical model for the optimisation landscape when an evolution algorithm operates far from the global optimum. This simple model can be used to approximate the optimisation process for problems where it is intractable to model the exact fitness function, including tasks such as hyperparameter tuning in machine learning models.
  We rigorously analyse the expected growth rate $\mathcal{R}_μ$ of the continuous steady-state $(μ+1)$-ES in this model. Unlike comma-selection strategies, the steady-state $(μ+1)$-ES maintains overlapping generations, introducing complex mathematical dependencies among surviving parents that make it harder to analyse. We give a general technique to analyse the the $(μ+ 1)$-ES by constructing modified processes whose growth rates provably sandwich that of the original process. These modified processes are then easier to analyse but still close enough to the true process to give a tight bound on the expected growth rate. When $Z = \mathcal{N}(-δ, 1)$ and $μ\le e^δ$, we show that $\mathcal{R}_μ = \frac{\log^{1 + o(1)} μ}μ \mathcal{R}_1$.

</details>


### [55] [Improved Runtime Bound for the $(μ+ 1)$ EA on BinVal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13344)
*Joris Belder,Johannes Lengler,Raghu Raman Ravi*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 针对Binary Value函数BinVal，研究了(μ+1) EA的优化时间，证明当μ=o(n/log n)时，至多需要O(μ log μ·n log n)次函数评估即可找到最优解，显著改进了此前O(μ^5 n log(n/μ^4))的上界，且该结果适用于包括标准位变异在内的多种变异算子。该边界意味着(μ+1) EA在BinVal上比OneMax最多慢O(log μ·log n)倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 改进(μ+1) EA在Binary Value函数上的已知运行时间上界，从O(μ^5 n log(n/μ^4))降低到更紧的O(μ log μ·n log n)，并揭示该算法在BinVal和OneMax之间的性能差距仅为一个对数因子。

Method: 通过理论分析(μ+1) EA在BinVal上的优化过程，利用μ较小（μ=o(n/log n)）的条件，推导出更紧的运行时间上界，并证明结果适用于多种变异算子（包括标准位变异）。

Result: 得到了O(μ log μ·n log n)的运行时间上界，比此前O(μ^5 n log(n/μ^4))的上界有显著改进；证明了(μ+1) EA在BinVal上比OneMax最多慢O(log μ·log n)倍。

Conclusion: (μ+1) EA在Binary Value函数上的运行时间上界可以大幅收紧，且与OneMax的性能差距仅为对数级别，理论分析为进化算法在非线性函数上的行为提供了更精确的刻画。

Abstract: We study the $(μ+1)$ EA on the Binary Value function BinVal. We show that it needs at most $O(μ\log μ\cdot n \log n)$ function evaluations to find the optimum when $μ= o(n/\log n)$. This substantially improves upon the recent upper bound of $O(μ^5 n \log(n/μ^4))$ by Krejca, Neumann and Witt. Our results hold for several mutation operators including standard bit mutation. In particular, our bound implies that the $(μ+1)$ EA is at most a factor $O(\log μ\cdot \log n)$ slower on BinVal than on OneMax.

</details>


### [56] [The $(1 + 1)$-EA in Dynamic Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13360)
*Georg Hasebe,Johannes Lengler,Raghu Raman Ravi*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文研究了(1+1)-EA在动态线性环境中的优化行为，发现突变率参数χ存在一个尖锐阈值：低于阈值时期望优化时间为O(n log n)，高于阈值时变为2^{Ω(n)}。对于动态二进制值问题，还发现了第二个阈值，量化了优化停滞的距离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解进化算法在动态环境中的行为，特别是突变率对优化效率的影响，以及验证和量化先前的实证发现。

Method: 分析了两种动态线性环境：动态二进制值问题（每代使用均匀随机排列的2的幂次权重）和均匀权重变体（权重独立服从Unif(0,1)分布），对突变率χ/n进行理论分析，寻找优化时间的相变阈值。

Result: 两种模型均存在突变参数χ的尖锐阈值：低于阈值优化时间为O(n log n)，高于阈值为2^{Ω(n)}。在指数权重下，还发现了第二个阈值，即优化过程在某个距离处停滞，达到更小距离需要指数时间。

Conclusion: 量化并证明了先前关于动态线性环境中进化算法行为的实证发现，揭示了突变率对优化效率的关键影响。

Abstract: We study the $(1 + 1)$-EA in dynamic linear environments, where in every generation selection is performed with respect to a freshly sampled linear function with positive weights. We consider the Dynamic Binary Value problem, where each generation uses a uniformly random permutation of $1,2,4,\dots,2^{n-1}$, and a Uniform weight variant, where the weights are drawn independently from $\mathrm{Unif}(0,1)$. Both of them have recently been integrated into the IOHprofiler platform and empirically studied.
  For both models we prove a sharp threshold in the mutation parameter $χ$ for mutation rate $χ/n$. Below the threshold, the expected optimisation time is $\mathcal{O}(n\log n)$, whereas above it the runtime becomes $2^{Ω(n)}$.
  For the Dynamic Binary Value problem in the exponential regime, we also quantify at what distance from the optimum the optimisation process stagnates. We show that there is a second threshold: a distance that is efficiently reached, but reaching any smaller distance takes exponential time. This quantifies and proves previous empirical findings.

</details>


### [57] [Adaptive-Frequency Resonate-and-Fire Neurons for Spectral Estimation of Streaming Radar Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13516)
*Stefano Chiavazza,Sen Yuan,Marc Geilen,Francesco Fioranelli,Federico Corradi*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于自适应共振-放电（ARF）神经元的类脑信号处理方法，用于FMCW雷达的距离-速度估计，通过逐样本处理替代传统FFT批处理，使内存需求仅与跟踪目标数成正比，适用于资源受限的边缘计算场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统FMCW雷达基于FFT的方法需要存储和处理大量数据块，在内存受限或低延迟应用中成为瓶颈，亟需一种内存高效、低延迟的替代方案。

Method: 将ARF神经元建模为离散时间动力系统，每个神经元动态调整内部频率以匹配输入雷达信号的主频分量；样本逐点处理，内存需求与跟踪目标数成正比；引入反馈机制，使多个神经元能在多目标场景下锁定不同频率分量。

Result: 在仿真和实验数据上成功实现多目标跟踪；相比传统FFT方法，内存使用量显著降低（仅与目标数成比例）。

Conclusion: 所提方法适合资源受限和边缘计算雷达应用，在保持多目标跟踪能力的同时大幅降低内存需求。

Abstract: Frequency Modulated Continuous Wave (FMCW) radar systems traditionally rely on Fourier-based methods, such as the Fast Fourier Transform (FFT), to estimate target range and velocity. While computationally efficient, these approaches require storing and processing large blocks of data, which can become a bottleneck in memory-constrained or low-latency applications. In this work, we propose a neuromorphic-inspired signal processing method based on adaptive resonate-and-fire (ARF) neurons formulated as a discrete-time dynamical system. Each neuron dynamically adjusts its internal frequency to match dominant frequency components of the input radar signal, enabling direct estimation of target ranges and velocities without computing the full frequency spectrum. The proposed model operates in a sample-by-sample fashion, resulting in memory requirements that scale with the number of tracked targets rather than the signal length. A feedback mechanism is also introduced to enable multiple neurons to lock on distinct frequency components in multi-target cases. Results on simulated and experimental data demonstrate that the method can successfully track multiple targets. Compared to conventional FFT-based approaches, the proposed method offers reduced memory usage proportional only to the number of tracked targets, making it suitable for resource-constrained and edge-based radar applications.

</details>

### [58] [nomp: A Framework for Building Domain Specific Compilers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12650)
*Thilina Ratnayaka,Kaushik Kulkarni,Nipuna Fernando,Pubudu Hewavitharana,Hirumal Priyashan,Poorna Gunathilaka,Nagitha Abeywickrema,Ravindu Hirimuthugoda,Tarun Prabhu,Kirshanthan Sundararajah,Sanath Jayasena*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 本文提出了nomp框架，通过基于pragma的编程模型和运行时系统，利用领域特定的优化模式，在保持性能和可移植性的同时提高程序员生产力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低级GPU编程模型（CUDA、HIP、OpenCL等）学习曲线陡峭，降低程序员生产力；而高级模型（OpenMP、OpenACC等）虽提高了生产力，但难以达到与低级模型相当的性能。现有方法在生产力、可移植性和性能之间存在固有权衡，缺乏三者兼顾的通用解决方案。

Method: 提出nomp框架，包含基于pragma的编程模型和运行时系统。运行时能够根据用户提供的元数据进行代码转换和生成，通过重用特定领域的优化模式来同时实现生产力、性能和可移植性。

Result: nomp框架能够构建领域特定编译器，在保持与低级模型相当性能的同时提高程序员的生产力，并保持良好的可移植性。

Conclusion: 通过重用领域特定的优化模式，可以在不牺牲性能和可移植性的前提下改善程序员生产力，nomp框架是这一思路的有效实现。

Abstract: The low-level GPU programming models (CUDA, HIP, OpenCL, etc.) provide detailed control of the data flow and execution plan of a program in order to extract close-to-metal performance. However, these have a steep learning curve due to the intricacies of their syntax and semantics. This reduces programmer productivity. On the other hand, high-level models (OpenMP, OpenACC, etc.) that serve as abstractions over the low-level models are aimed at improving programmer productivity but achieving performance on-par with the low-level models is a challenge. There are inherent trade-offs between productivity, portability and performance in both approaches and there is no one-size-fits-all solution which achieves all three simultaneously. However, we believe there is room to improve programmer productivity without sacrificing performance and portability by reusing optimization patterns specific to a given domain. To this end, we propose nomp: a framework for building domain specific compilers. nomp consists of a pragma based programming model and a runtime capable of code transformation and generation based on user provided metadata.

</details>


### [59] [Functional Cache Grafting: Robust and Rapid Code-Policy Synthesis for Embodied Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13097)
*Saehun Chun,Wonje Choi,Sera Choi,Sanghyun Ahn,Honguk Woo*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: FCGraft通过缓存已验证的函数级代码骨架及其KV缓存，采用拼接（stitching）和修补（patching）两种机制合成新策略，消除了冗余预填充计算，在开放域具身策略生成中相比RAGCache实现了18.31%更高的任务成功率和2.3倍的策略合成加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CodeLLMs在开放域具身环境中生成可执行代码策略存在两大局限：(i) 长提示重复预填充导致的延迟解码，以及(ii) 完全生成式解码带来的鲁棒性不足（API不匹配、安全防护缺失、控制逻辑不稳定）。

Method: FCGraft维护一个函数级已验证代码骨架库及其对应的提示级Transformer KV缓存。给定新任务时，检索相关函数并嫁接其KV缓存，通过stitching（拼接缓存函数段为复合策略）和patching（局部适配必要代码区域满足任务参数和约束，只需少量额外解码）合成新策略。

Result: 相比RAGCache，FCGraft实现了18.31%更高的任务成功率和2.3倍更快的策略合成速度。

Conclusion: 通过消除冗余预填充计算降低生成延迟，同时重用已验证的控制结构提升鲁棒性，FCGraft在开放域具身策略生成中优于基于提示级缓存的方法。

Abstract: Code-writing large language models (CodeLLMs) generate executable code policies for embodied agents by translating natural language goals and environmental constraints into structured control programs. However, policy generation in open-domain embodied environments suffers from two fundamental limitations: (i) delayed decoding caused by repetitive prefill computation over long prompts, and (ii) limited robustness due to fully generative decoding, which often produces API mismatches, missing safety guards, and unstable control logic. To address these limitations, we present FCGraft, a Functional Cache Grafting framework. FCGraft maintains a library of function-level validated code skeletons and their associated prompt-level Transformer key-value (KV) caches, and synthesizes new policies by retrieving relevant functions and grafting their KV caches when a new task is provided. Given retrieved function caches, FCGraft performs cache grafting via stitching, which composes cached function segments into a composite policy, and patching, which locally adapts only the necessary code regions to satisfy task-specific parameters and constraints with minimal additional decoding. By eliminating redundant prefill computation, this approach reduces generation latency, while reusing validated control structures improves robustness over prompt-level caching methods RAGCache, achieving 18.31% higher task success rate and 2.3x faster policy synthesis.

</details>


### [60] [Probabilistic, Resource-Aware, Asynchronous, Out-of-Order Choreographies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13520)
*Mako Bates,Steven Baldasty,Ernest Hyun,Christian Skalka,Joseph P. Near*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 本文提出了AsInst，一种概率性、资源感知的语言，用于建模具有乱序执行的异步编排语义，并通过时序贝叶斯网络来量化执行概率和耗时。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于期货的乱序编排实现能显著提升延迟和吞吐量，但其实际行为受通信延迟、计算时间、故障和恢复等资源影响。现有形式化模型（如Ozone的O3）仅描述可能的执行序列，无法解释执行的概率分布和耗时。

Method: 提出AsInst语言，将程序解释为时序贝叶斯网络，同时建模运行时产生的值及其可用时间。证明了该核心语义正确对应未来风格的网络语义，并编码了Ozone风格的选择-合并条件语句。

Result: 通过案例研究建模通信故障恢复并分析运行时性能，验证了AsInst在量化执行概率和耗时方面的有效性。

Conclusion: AsInst为乱序编排的概率性、资源感知建模提供了理论基础，弥补了现有模型在性能量化分析方面的空白。

Abstract: Futures-based implementations of out-of-order choreographies can substantially improve latency and throughput, but their actual behavior depends on resources such as communication delay, computation time, failures, and recovery. Existing formal models such as Ozone's O3 describe which executions are possible, but do not directly explain how likely those executions are or how long they take. In this work we present AsInst, a probabilistic, resource-aware language for modeling the semantics of asynchronous choreographies with out-of-order execution. AsInst programs are interpreted as temporal Bayesian networks that model both the values produced at runtime and the times at which they become available. We prove that this central semantics correctly captures a corresponding futures-style network semantics. We also show that AsInst can encode Ozone-style select-and-merge conditionals, and we use case studies to model communication-failure recovery and analyze runtime performance.

</details>


### [61] [Specifying Hardware Communication as Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13659)
*Ernest Ng,Nikil Shyamsunder,Francis Pham,Adrian Sampson,Kevin Laeufer*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 提出一种DSL，用同一份协议规范同时实现硬件驱动（生成信号事务）和监控（从波形推断事务级别跟踪），替代传统分别编写driver和monitor的做法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法中driver和monitor为每个硬件协议分别独立实现，导致手动工作量大且容易产生不一致。

Method: 设计一种DSL，用户以简洁的命令式程序指定硬件通信协议；基于同一规范，工具可从波形自动推断出事务级跟踪。

Result: 提出了工具设计，能根据DSL规范和波形自动推断事务级跟踪；计划在Wishbone和AXI-Stream等真实互连协议上评估。

Conclusion: 同一份协议规范可同时用于驱动设计和监控事务，消除了分别实现的冗余和不一致风险。

Abstract: To test and debug hardware modules, it is common to write two programs: a driver, which translates high-level transactions into interactions on the module's input and output signals, and a monitor, which analyzes a signal-level execution trace and recognizes a transaction. These two programs are commonly implemented separately for each hardware protocol, but this separation entails manual effort and risks inconsistencies.
  We advocate an alternative approach. We present a DSL in which users specify hardware communication protocols as succinct imperative programs. Crucially, the same specification can be used to both drive designs and monitor transactions. We present the design of a tool, which given a specification in our DSL and a waveform, automatically infers a transaction-level trace consistent with the waveform. We discuss plans to evaluate our DSL on real-world interconnects such as Wishbone and AXI-Stream.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [62] [Positional Encoding in the Context of Memristor-Based Analog Computation for Automatic Speech Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13379)
*Benedikt Hilmes,Nick Rossenbach,Ralf Schlüter*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现忆阻器计算中，经过变换的位置编码的大输出值会导致模数转换（ADC）性能严重下降。通过调整特定忆阻器层的权重与ADC精度位比例，可在能耗基本不变的情况下将执行退化降低约50%；若ADC无法修改，则通过移除编码相关的线性变换可降低约30%的退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 忆阻器为NLP神经模型的计算提供了一种资源高效的新途径，但当前这些器件在权重编程和执行过程中存在较大的失真问题，亟需解决。

Method: 1) 识别出变换后的位置编码的大输出值是导致忆阻器计算中ADC退化的主要原因；2) 调整特定忆阻器层的权重与ADC精度位比例；3) 在ADC无法修改时，移除编码相关的线性变换。

Result: 调整权重与ADC精度位比例后，执行退化降低约50%，且能耗保持稳定；在ADC不可修改时，移除线性变换可降低约30%的退化。

Conclusion: 通过针对性的结构调整或预处理优化，可以有效缓解忆阻器计算中因位置编码输出过大导致的ADC退化问题，提升计算可靠性。

Abstract: Memristors provide a new chance for resource-efficient computation of neural models for natural language processing by enabling analog execution of vector-matrix-multiplication. Yet, computations on these devices are currently subject to larger distortion, both in weight programming and execution. In this work, we identify large output values of transformed positional encodings to cause major degradation within analog-to-digital conversion (ADC) as part of memristor-based computation. By adjusting the proportion of weight and precision bits of the ADC of specific memristor layers, we reduce the degradation of the execution by ~50% relative, while keeping the estimated energy consumption stable. Additionally, we investigate scenarios where the ADC cannot be modified. In that case the degradation can be reduced by ~30% relative after removing encoding-related linear transformations.

</details>


### [63] [Individual Control Barrier Functions-Guided Diffusion Model for Safe Offline Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12640)
*Qingyun Guo,Junyi Shi,Jianuo Huang,Tianyu Shi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种将神经个体控制障碍函数嵌入扩散模型的离线多智能体强化学习算法，在保证奖励竞争力的同时显著提升安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有离线强化学习的扩散模型方法主要关注单智能体场景，多智能体环境下的安全性挑战尚未得到充分研究。

Method: 将神经个体控制障碍函数嵌入扩散模型中以增强轨迹生成的安全性，通过逆动力学恢复控制策略。

Result: 在多个基准测试中，算法在保持竞争力奖励的同时，实现了显著的安全性提升。

Conclusion: 扩散模型结合神经个体控制障碍函数可有效解决离线多智能体强化学习中的安全问题，为安全关键任务提供可行方案。

Abstract: Offline reinforcement learning allows control policies to be learned directly from data without online interaction, making it suitable for safety-critical tasks. Recent studies have applied diffusion models to offline reinforcement learning to leverage their strong capacity for modeling complex data distributions. However, existing approaches primarily focus on single-agent settings, leaving the safety challenges in multi-agent environments largely unexplored. In this work, we propose a safe offline multi-agent reinforcement learning algorithm that embeds neural individual control barrier functions into the diffusion model to enhance safety during trajectory generation, with control policies recovered through inverse dynamics. We evaluate our algorithm across diverse benchmarks, demonstrating substantial safety improvements while maintaining competitive rewards.

</details>


### [64] [Quickest Detection of Hallucination Onset: Delay Bounds and Learned CUSUM Statistics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12476)
*Igor Itkin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将幻觉检测建模为快速变化检测问题，使用CUSUM方法检测令牌级幻觉，在RAGTruth数据集上验证了延迟与误报率的权衡，发现因果循环标注器在匹配误报率下检测延迟为11-13个令牌，远优于线性基线，并通过信息率最优理论解释了性能差距来源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有令牌级幻觉检测器以AUC等分类指标评估，忽略了流式监控场景中检测延迟这一关键维度，导致分类指标掩盖了检测延迟结构，无法反映实际部署性能。

Method: 将幻觉检测建模为快速变化检测问题，构建忠实/幻觉状态的马尔可夫模型，使用CUSUM方法结合因果循环标注器（作为带学习增量的CUSUM），在RAGTruth数据集上验证，并应用Donsker-Varadhan型信息率最优定理分析性能差距。

Result: 在误报率0.01下，Lorden下界为约1.3个令牌；因果循环标注器检测延迟为11-13个令牌，优于线性基线的31个令牌；优势主要来自更好的逐令牌分数而非时间累积；学习分数仅实现了特征携带散度的1/4.5。

Conclusion: 分类指标掩盖了检测延迟结构，需要引入序贯分析方法进行可量化的延迟评估；当前检测器的延迟远未达到理论下界，仍有大幅提升空间。

Abstract: Token-level hallucination detectors are evaluated as classifiers, by AUC over all tokens, yet a streaming monitor is judged by its reaction time: the number of tokens that pass between the onset of a hallucination and the alarm. We formulate hallucination onset detection as a quickest change detection problem. A first-order Markov model of the latent faithful/hallucinated state, validated on RAGTruth, places the task inside classical change-point theory and yields Lorden's lower bound on detection delay: about 1.3 tokens at a false-alarm rate of 0.01. We then show that a causal recurrent labeler acts as a CUSUM with a learned increment; at a matched false-alarm rate it detects in 11-13 tokens, against 31 for a linear per-token baseline, and a controlled decomposition attributes most of this advantage to a better per-token score rather than to temporal accumulation. An information-rate optimality theorem of Donsker-Varadhan type explains the remaining order-of-magnitude gap: the learned score realizes only 1/4.5 of the divergence the features carry, a deficit that recalibration cannot remove, with the remainder a finite-horizon effect. Classification metrics conceal this delay structure; sequential analysis makes it measurable

</details>


### [65] [Two-Layer Linear Auto-Regressive Models Estimate Latent States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12691)
*Yahya Sattar,Sunmook Choi,Leo Maynard-Zhang,Yassir Jedra,Maryam Fazel,Sarah Dean*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了两层线性自回归模型在部分观测线性动力系统数据上通过经验风险最小化训练时，会自然学习到近似卡尔曼滤波的功能，其潜表示与卡尔曼滤波的状态估计在相似变换下等价，并提供了预测误差、参数估计误差和潜在状态恢复的有限样本保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归模型在序列数据（从语言到视频）中表现出强大能力，但其学习潜在表示的理论机制尚不明确。本文旨在从理论上解释自回归模型为何以及如何学习到有意义的潜在表示。

Method: 1) 建立卡尔曼滤波可被有限截断的自回归模型良好近似的理论；2) 证明两层线性自回归模型的优化景观是良性的（所有驻点要么是严格鞍点，要么是全局最小值）；3) 提供预测误差、参数估计误差和潜在状态恢复的有限样本保证。

Result: 两层线性自回归模型在部分观测线性动力系统上训练后，其学习到的隐表示与最优卡尔曼滤波的状态估计在相似变换下一致；提供了有限样本下的理论保证；数值模拟验证了理论结果。

Conclusion: 自回归模型虽无显式的动力学或状态知识，但通过经验风险最小化训练可自然恢复卡尔曼滤波的潜状态估计，为理解自回归模型的学习机制提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Auto-regressive models have emerged as powerful tools for sequential data, from language to video. Understanding how and why these models learn latent representations remains an open theoretical question. In this work, we demonstrate that when trained by empirical risk minimization on data from partially observed linear dynamical systems, two-layer linear auto-regressive models naturally learn to approximate Kalman filtering. In particular, we show that the learned hidden representation coincides, up to a similarity transformation, with the state estimates produced by the optimal (Kalman) filter, even though the model has no explicit knowledge of the underlying dynamics or state. The result follows from three main insights. First, we establish that the Kalman filter is well approximated by an auto-regressive model with bounded truncation error. Second, we show that despite non-convexity, the two-layer optimization landscape is benign, i.e., all stationary points are either strict saddles or global minima. Finally, as our main contributions, we provide finite-sample guarantees on prediction error, parameter estimation error, and latent state recovery. Numerical simulations support the theoretical results and demonstrate that the latent representations of auto-regressive models recover state estimates.

</details>


### [66] [Boltzmann Attention: Learnable Ising Couplings for Cooperative Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12478)
*Gilhan Kim,Daniel K. Park*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出玻尔兹曼注意力（Boltzmann attention），通过引入伊辛模型的可学习成对耦合，将标准注意力机制推广为能量基模型，使注意力能够显式建模位置间的协同或对抗交互。实验表明该方法在序列建模任务上优于标准softmax注意力，且量子退火可作为有效训练策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准注意力机制仅通过查询-键相似度计算独立的相关性，无法显式建模注意力决策之间的可学习交互（如协同或对抗的共注意力结构），限制了其表达能力。

Method: 将注意力模式建模为伊辛模型，在标准数据依赖的局部场基础上增加可学习的成对耦合项，从而允许模型捕捉位置间的相关性。训练可采用精确玻尔兹曼计算或非绝热量子退火采样。

Result: 在字符级语言建模和括号匹配任务上，玻尔兹曼注意力一致优于标准softmax注意力，优势随序列长度增加而扩大。四项消融实验证实改进来自可学习成对耦合。

Conclusion: 显式的位置间交互为基于注意力的序列建模提供了原则性增强，且伊辛公式为量子计算采样策略开辟了自然路径。

Abstract: Attention mechanisms are central to modern sequence models, yet standard attention computes relevance primarily through individual query--key similarities. Although softmax normalization introduces competition among positions, a standard attention layer does not explicitly parameterize learnable interactions between attention decisions. This limits its ability to directly model cooperative or antagonistic co-attention structure within the attention mechanism itself. We propose Boltzmann attention, an energy-based generalization in which attention patterns are governed by an interacting Ising model. The method augments the usual data-dependent local fields with learnable pairwise couplings, allowing the model to represent inter-position correlations beyond those captured by softmax or sigmoid attention. Experiments on character-level language modeling and synthetic bracket matching show that Boltzmann attention consistently improves over standard softmax attention within a standard Transformer architecture, with the advantage becoming more pronounced as sequence length increases. A four-way ablation confirms that the improvement arises from the learnable pairwise couplings. These results suggest that explicit inter-position interactions provide a principled enhancement for attention-based sequence modeling. Moreover, the Ising formulation opens a natural path toward quantum-computing-based sampling strategies: we demonstrate that diabatic quantum annealing provides a practical training method while maintaining competitive performance with exact Boltzmann computation.

</details>


### [67] [ReCal: Reward Calibration for RL-based LLM Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12479)
*Qihang Yu,Hanwen Tong,Zhengqi Zhang,Bo Zheng,Feng Wei,Shengyu Zhang,Zemin Liu,Fei Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ReCal 是一个面向基于强化学习的 LLM 路由的奖励校准框架，通过分层奖励分解与分布感知优化策略，解决异构任务中奖励信号模糊、优化偏差等问题，在7个数据集上持续提升路由性能与训练稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于强化学习的 LLM 路由方法在异构任务下缺乏信息量丰富且可比较的学习信号：多个目标被聚合为单一标量奖励，导致信用分配模糊与优化冲突；同时奖励信号在不同实例间差异大，优化偏差倾向于简单样本而非信息丰富的样本。

Method: 提出 ReCal 框架，包含两个核心模块：1) 分层奖励分解机制，结合组件级优势估计；2) 分布感知优化策略，通过方差感知重加权和每个数据集级别的归一化来校准优化变异性。

Result: 在7个数据集上的实验结果表明，ReCal 在路由性能和训练稳定性方面持续优于基线方法。

Conclusion: ReCal 通过奖励校准有效提升了 RL 驱动的 LLM 路由在异构任务下的表现，代码已开源。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) routing has emerged as an effective paradigm for leveraging the complementary strengths of multiple LLMs through dynamic model and reasoning-strategy selection. Recent reinforcement learning (RL)-based routing methods further improve routing quality by optimizing routing policies from interaction feedback. However, they still struggle to provide informative and comparable learning signals under heterogeneous tasks with varying difficulty. In practice, multiple objectives (e.g., correctness, format behavior) are aggregated into a single scalar reward, leading to ambiguous credit assignment and conflicting optimization signals. Moreover, reward signals exhibit significant variability across instances, where some instances produce higher or more variable rewards, introducing optimization bias that favors trivial samples over informative ones. To address these issues, we propose \textbf{ReCal}, a \textbf{\underline{Re}}ward \textbf{\underline{Cal}}ibration framework for RL-based LLM routing. We first introduce a hierarchical reward decomposition mechanism with component-wise advantage estimation. We further propose a distribution-aware optimization strategy that calibrates optimization variability through variance-aware reweighting and per-dataset normalization. Experiments on seven datasets demonstrate that ReCal consistently improves routing performance, and training stability over baselines. Code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/ReCal.

</details>


### [68] [Representing Time Series as Structured Programs for LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12481)
*Jaeho Kim,Changhun Oh,Seokhyun Lee,Irina Rish,Changhee Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出T2SP方法，将时间序列转化为结构化符号程序表示，使现成LLM无需微调即可有效推理时间序列，在编辑、标注和问答三个任务上优于原始数值序列表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法（原始数值序列或微调LLM）存在模态不匹配、长序列性能下降和计算开销大的问题，需要一种更有效的表示方式让LLM理解时间序列。

Method: T2SP是一种确定性、无需训练的方法，将时间序列分解为趋势、周期和显著事件，以结构化的符号程序格式表示，与LLM原生训练的文本和代码模态对齐。

Result: 在编辑、标注和问答三个推理任务上，T2SP相比原始字符串表示持续提升了性能，减少了推理时间，并降低了失败率。

Conclusion: T2SP为时间序列和LLM之间提供了有效的接口，通过将时序结构提取从模型转移到表示本身，使LLM能直接利用现有推理能力处理时间序列。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated strong reasoning and instruction-following capabilities, making them potentially powerful tools for time-series analysis. However, time series lie outside their native textual modality, raising a fundamental question: how should time series be represented so that LLMs can reason about them effectively? Existing work typically serializes raw numerical sequences or fine-tunes pre-trained LLMs on time-series data. These approaches place the burden of extracting temporal structure directly on the LLM, creating a modality mismatch that often degrades performance on long sequences and introduces substantial computational overhead. In this work, we introduce Time-Series-to-Structured-Program representation (T2SP), a deterministic, training-free method that represents a time series as a structured symbolic program. T2SP decomposes time series into trends, periods, and salient events, expressing them in a program-friendly format aligned with the textual and code-like modalities on which LLMs are natively trained. By shifting temporal-structure extraction from the model to the representation itself, T2SP enables off-the-shelf LLMs to leverage their existing reasoning capabilities for time-series understanding. We evaluate T2SP on three reasoning tasks -- editing, captioning, and question answering -- where it consistently improves performance, reduces reasoning time, and lowers failure rates compared with raw-string representations. Our results demonstrate that T2SP provides an effective interface between time series and LLMs.

</details>


### [69] [Scalable anomaly detection via a univariate Christoffel function](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12483)
*Florian Grivet,Didier Henrion,Jean-Bernard Lasserre,Louise Travé-Massuyès*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出UCF（单变量Christoffel函数），通过基于查询点与支撑点之间的平方距离，解决了Christoffel函数方法在高维数据中因矩阵求逆导致的维度灾难问题，在ADBench基准上优于14个最先进基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Christoffel函数方法在异常检测中具有坚实的数学基础和计算 frugality 优势，但因其需要求逆一个随数据维度指数增长的矩阵，导致在中高维数据集上计算不可行，亟需解决其维度可扩展性瓶颈。

Method: 提出UCF（单变量Christoffel函数），基于查询点与支撑点之间的平方距离构建，避免了原始Christoffel函数需要在高维空间中进行大矩阵求逆的计算负担。

Result: 在ADBench基准上的大量实验表明，UCF在平均精度（Average Precision）上持续优于14个最先进的基线方法。

Conclusion: 通过解决Christoffel函数的可扩展性瓶颈，本文为异常检测工具库增添了一种鲁棒、有理论根基且普遍适用的方法。

Abstract: Anomaly detection plays a critical role in identifying unusual patterns across domains such as fraud detection, network intrusion, and system fault diagnosis. Recently, Christoffel function-based methods, rooted in polynomial optimization, have emerged as promising alternatives to deep learning due to their strong mathematical foundations and computational frugality. However, their practical applicability is hindered by the need to invert a matrix whose size grows exponentially with the data dimension, rendering the method intractable even for moderate-dimensional datasets. This paper addresses the dimensionality limitations of Christoffel function-based anomaly detection while preserving its key theoretical properties, i.e., the on-off support dichotomy behavior and the accurate support shape capture. We introduce UCF, a univariate Christoffel function which is based on the squared distance between the query point and the support points. Extensive experiments on the ADBench benchmark demonstrate that UCF consistently outperforms 14 state-of-the-art baselines in terms of Average Precision. By resolving the scalability bottleneck of the Christoffel Function, this work expands the toolkit of anomaly detection methods with a robust, theoretically grounded, and universally applicable approach.

</details>


### [70] [Speculative Rollback Correction for Quality-Diverse Web Agent Imitation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12485)
*Longkun Hao,Hongyu Lin,Hao Li,Zhichao Yang,Haojie Hao,Dongshuo Huang,Haitao Yang,Hongyu Ge,Ming jie Xie,Yanjun Wu,Zi Hao Yin,Yan Bai,Yihang Lou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Speculative Rollback Correction (SRC)，一种面向可重置智能体环境的分支级模仿学习框架。SRC通过固定视界分支审查机制，让学生在教师审查前先执行短分支，仅当局部进度中断时教师才定位首次有害偏离。回滚保留有用前缀，成功轨迹经硬验证器筛选后存入轻量级质量多样性档案。在WebArena-Infinity上，SRC收集977条通过验证的轨迹和9,183个下一动作示例，在恢复质量与查询次数之间实现了优于步级审查的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有交互式Web智能体的模仿学习中，专家干预时机是关键挑战：延迟干预导致早期错误累积使页面状态不可恢复，而过早或过度干预则使智能体过度依赖专家策略，陷入单一刚性轨迹的局部最优。

Method: 提出Speculative Rollback Correction (SRC)，一种分支级模仿学习框架。核心机制为固定视界分支审查（fixed-horizon branch review）：学生先执行一段短推测性分支，教师仅在局部进度中断时定位第一个有害偏离；回滚操作保留有用前缀，成功轨迹经硬验证器过滤后存入轻量级质量多样性档案（quality-diversity archive）。收集的数据用于下一动作的有监督微调。

Result: 在WebArena-Infinity上，SRC收集了977条通过验证器验证的轨迹和9,183个下一动作训练样本；固定视界审查在恢复质量与查询次数的权衡上优于步级审查，同时保留了通过验证的解决方案变体。

Conclusion: SRC通过分支级回滚校正策略，有效平衡了专家干预时机问题，在减少专家查询次数的同时保持较高的轨迹恢复质量和解决方案多样性。

Abstract: Training interactive web agents through imitation learning from expert trajectories has emerged as a highly effective approach. However, determining the optimal timing for expert intervention presents a critical challenge in this context. Delayed intervention often leads to the accumulation of early-stage errors, pushing the page state into an irrecoverable regime. Conversely, premature or excessive intervention causes the agent to become overly reliant on expert policies, trapping the model in local optima characterized by a single, rigid trajectory. We propose Speculative Rollback Correction (SRC), a branch-level imitation framework for resettable agent environments. Instead of requesting teacher labels at every visited state or correcting only after a completed trajectory, SRC uses fixed-horizon branch review: the student executes a short speculative segment before teacher review, and the teacher localizes the first harmful deviation only when local progress breaks. Rollback preserves useful prefixes, while successful rollouts are filtered by a hard verifier and retained in a lightweight quality-diversity archive. The resulting data supports next-action supervised fine-tuning on both localized corrections and verifier-passing trajectories. On WebArena-Infinity, SRC collects 977 verifier-passing trajectories and 9,183 next-action examples; fixed-horizon review improves the recovery-versus-query tradeoff over step-level review while retaining verifier-passing solution variants. Code is available at https://github.com/LongkunHao/SRC_gui_agent.

</details>


### [71] [Circuit Synchronization Precedes Generalization: Causal Evidence from Fourier Structure in Grokking Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12966)
*Achyuthan Sivasankar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出频域同步度（FSD）指标，可在Grokking现象（模运算Transformer从随机精度突变为近乎完美精度）发生前500-3000步检测到傅里叶电路的同步，并发现权重衰减与Grokking时间呈严格反比关系（Δ_t ∝ 1/λ），为理解其因果机制提供了最早的可预测指标和定量控制规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Grokking现象（Transformer在模算术任务上的泛化突变）被认为与傅里叶电路有关，但其发生时机、因果结构及可控性尚不清楚。现有研究缺乏无需先验电路知识即可提前预测Grokking的指标，也缺乏对Grokking时机可调性的定量理解。

Method: 1) 提出频域同步度（FSD），一种归一化、经置换检验的傅里叶电路同步度量，无需先验电路知识；2) 在9种模加法配置（素数p∈{53,71,97,113,131}，3种随机种子）上验证FSD领先Grokking 500-3000步；3) 进行训练分叉实验，在FSD达到峰值时切换不同权重衰减λ，测量Grokking提前量Δ_t与λ的关系；4) 进行架构消融实验（仅注意力、仅MLP、单层模型）。

Result: 1) FSD在Grokking前平均领先1722步（符号检验p~0.004），超越已有的受限logit损失基线；2) 在FSD峰值处分叉训练并变化λ，发现Δ_t与1/λ严格单调正相关，拟合R²=1.00和0.99；3) 仅注意力模型可Grok且FSD先导明显，仅MLP模型从不Grok，单层模型FSD滞后，表明FSD先导是多层电路特性。

Conclusion: Grokking的相变间隙本质上是正则化现象，FSD是已知最早的Grokking预测指标，且通过权重衰减可精确控制Grokking发生时间（Δ_t ∝ C/λ），该规律与(1/λ)log(||W_mem||/τ)的理论形式一致。

Abstract: Grokking -- where a transformer on modular arithmetic suddenly transitions from near-chance to near-perfect validation accuracy -- is attributed to a Fourier circuit, but its timing, causal structure, and controllability remain poorly understood. We introduce the Frequency Synchronization Degree (FSD), a normalised, permutation-tested metric for Fourier circuit synchronisation requiring no prior circuit knowledge. Across nine modular addition configurations (primes p in {53, 71, 97, 113, 131}, three seeds), FSD synchronises 500-3,000 steps before grokking (mean lead +1,722 steps; all nine positive, sign-test p~0.004), and precedes a restricted-logit loss baseline (Nanda et al.'s excluded loss) in all nine cases, making it the earliest available predictor. We provide direct causal evidence that the inter-phase gap is a regularisation phenomenon: forking training at the FSD-ceiling step and varying weight decay lambda produces strictly monotone earlier grokking, with Delta_t proportional to 1/lambda. This law replicates across three primes (p in {53,97,131}; R^2=1.00 and R^2=0.99 for two clean cases), captured as Delta_t ~ C/lambda, consistent with (1/lambda)*log(||W_mem||/tau). Architecture ablations show an attention-only model groks with a strong FSD precursor; an MLP-only model never groks; a single-layer model's FSD lags, confirming the precursor is a multi-block circuit property.

</details>


### [72] [An Empirical Study on Predictive Maintenance for Component X in Heavy-Duty Scania Trucks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12486)
*Valeriu Dimidov,Sasan Jafarnejad,Raphaël Frank*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出并验证了一种基于条件维护的预测性维护（PdM）方法，该方法假设被监测部件的磨损状态可表示为单调非递减时间序列，通过选取最新观测数据并转换为表格格式，使用机器学习模型进行分类，在Scania Component X数据集上相比现有SOTA方法降低了成本并简化了建模过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 卡车车队的条件预测性维护虽受关注，但面临数据量大、故障检测复杂以及实现成本效益平衡困难等挑战，亟需一种既能降低成本又能简化实现过程的维护方法。

Method: 提出一种基于磨损状态为单调非递减时间序列假设的PdM方法：仅选取时间序列中的最新观测数据，将其转换为表格格式，然后使用适用于表格数据的机器学习模型（通过AutoML）进行分类。

Result: 在Scania Component X数据集上，所提出的方法相比当前SOTA方法降低了成本。

Conclusion: 该PdM方法能有效降低成本，且通过AutoML简化了建模过程，为卡车车队的预测性维护提供了一种更实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Condition-based Predictive Maintenance (PdM) for truck fleets has gained momentum in recent years. This maintenance strategy aims to minimize unplanned downtimes and reduce costs by monitoring the health status of vehicles and taking proactive action based on their condition. However, the implementation of condition-based PdM systems is challenging due to the large volume of data generated by the trucks, the inherent complexity of detecting failures through sensor data and the difficulties in finding cost-effective trade-offs in the solution's implementation. In this paper, we define and validate a condition-based PdM methodology built on the assumption that the wear-and-tear state of the monitored component can be represented as a monotonically non-decreasing time series. It involves selecting only the most recent observations from the time series and transforming them into a tabular format for classification using machine learning (ML) models designed for tabular data. Our results indicate that the proposed methodology reduces costs on the Scania Component X dataset compared to current state-of-the-art (SOTA) approaches, while also simplifying the modeling process through AutoML.

</details>


### [73] [MP3: Multi-Period Pattern Pre-training forSpatio-Temporal Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13119)
*Lilan Peng,Yandi Liu,Qingren Yao,Chongshou Li,Tianrui Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MP3（多周期模式预训练）插件，用于解决时空预测中的"时间幻象"问题——即相似短窗口输入可能对应不同未来趋势。MP3通过多周期模式学习、多周期空间建模和跨周期模式交互三大创新，可无缝集成到现有STGNN骨干网络中，在5个数据集和5个基线上平均降低MAE 4.7%、RMSE 5.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有STGNN无法有效识别时空数据中的"时间幻象"（相似短窗口输入产生不同未来趋势，反之亦然），其根本原因在于短窗口输入存在周期观测不完整、全局空间异质性关联以及跨周期叠加因果性。

Method: 提出MP3（Multi-Period Pattern Pre-training）插件，包含三个核心模块：(1) 多周期模式学习：利用边卷积（edge convolution）识别不同周期模式；(2) 多周期空间建模：通过瓶颈投影和全局记忆库捕获异质全局空间关系；(3) 跨周期模式交互：采用因果增强型Transformer捕获跨周期模式依赖。该插件即插即用，可集成到现有STGNN骨干中。

Result: 在5个STGNN基线模型和5个真实世界数据集（含大规模CA数据集）上验证，MP3带来一致且稳健的性能提升，平均降低MAE 4.7%、RMSE 5.0%。

Conclusion: MP3作为一种即插即用的预训练插件，能有效提升现有STGNN在时空预测中应对"时间幻象"的能力，具有良好的可扩展性和强适应性。

Abstract: Spatio-Temporal forecasting is crucial in diverse fields, such as transportation, climate, and energy. Urban spatio-temporal data exhibits temporal mirage: similar short-window inputs have divergent future trends, and vice versa. Existing spatio-temporal graph neural networks (STGNNs) cannot effectively identify such mirages. We argue that the core reason lies in the short-window inputs that have incomplete period observation, heterogeneous global spatial correlation, and cross-period superposition causality. To bridge this gap, we develop a novel Multi- Period Pattern Pre-training (MP3), a plug-and-play pre-training plugin for distinguishing temporal mirages. MP3 presents two core innovations: (1) The multi-period pattern learning is designed to learn multi-period patterns from long time series. Specifically, multi-period temporal modeling leverages edge convolution to identify different multi-period patterns. Multi-period spatial modeling uses a bottleneck project and a global memory bank to capture heterogeneous global spatial relations efficiently. Cross-period pattern interaction employs a causality-enhanced Transformer to capture dependencies across different period patterns. (2) This plugin can seamlessly integrate into existing STGNN backbones to strengthen their forecasting performance. The experiment on five STGNN baselines across five real-world datasets (including a large-scale dataset CA) verify the effectiveness, superior scalability and strong adaptability of MP3, which brings consistent and robust performance improvements across all evaluated baselines. On average, MP3 reduces the MAE 4.7% and the RMSE 5.0%. The code can be available at https://github.com/YAN-outlook/MP3.

</details>


### [74] [DynamicPTQ: Mitigating Activation Quantization Collapse via Residual-Stream Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12487)
*Zimo Zhao,Maolin Wang,Bowen Yu,Bowen Liu,Xiao Han,Xiangyu Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DynamicPTQ，一种动态后训练量化策略，通过识别残差流中量化敏感的层并为其分配8-bit精度（其余保持4-bit），解决了W4A4KV4量化中由于跨层残差动态变化导致的激活量化不稳定问题，在LLaMA-2/3上提升了困惑度和零样本QA性能，并实现1.05-1.07倍的吞吐量提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PTQ方法在同时量化权重、激活和KV缓存到4-bit时面临挑战，主要难点在于大规模激活的极端值主导量化范围并放大误差。当前SOTA方法（如正交旋转、仿射缩放等）通过变换平滑缓解该问题，但忽略了残差流的跨层动态特性，无法完全解决由跨层残差变化引起的动态量化不稳定性。

Method: 1) 发现大规模激活在深度网络中呈阶段性出现和消失的模式，导致残差发生大的变化；2) 提出Jump Ratio和Historical Feature SNR两个指标来表征跨层残差动态行为；3) 基于此提出DynamicPTQ，动态识别量化敏感层，仅对这些层使用8-bit激活精度，其余权重、KV缓存和激活保持4-bit；4) 可直接集成到QuaRot、SpinQuant、FlatQuant等基线中。

Result: 在LLaMA-2和LLaMA-3上的实验表明，在W4A4KV4量化下，DynamicPTQ一致地提升了困惑度和零样本QA性能，同时实现了1.05-1.07倍的吞吐量提升，内存开销适中。

Conclusion: DynamicPTQ提供了一条实现鲁棒的低比特LLM推理的实用路径，证明了考虑跨层残差动态特性对于提升量化效果的重要性。

Abstract: Post-training quantization (PTQ) is essential for efficient large language model inference, but reliably quantizing activations remains challenging when weights, activations, and KV caches are all quantized to 4-bit precision.
