Conclusion: 本文设计的策略在理论上同时保证了可采纳性和基线性能，为随机优化中的在线到批次转换提供了更优且安全的替代方案。

Abstract: We study the problem of selecting the largest among $n$ unknown values $x_1,\dots,x_n$ given only a single unbiased estimate $y_i$ for each $x_i$. We design strategies that are simultaneously admissible (not uniformly dominated by any other strategy) and also never worse than a given baseline such as uniform random selection. We provide an application to stochastic optimization, where we obtain online-to-batch conversion bounds with a desirable "no-compromise" guarantee: they are never worse than standard random iterate selection, and yet can be significantly better in benign settings.

</details>


### [111] [Scale Buys Interpolation, Structure Buys a Horizon: Certified Predictability for Equivariant World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13092)
*Hongbo Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明等变世界模型具有可计算的预测视界认证：轨迹误差在对称轨道上恒定，并由李雅普诺夫谱分层界定。非等变模型无论规模多大都无法获得这种认证。实验表明等变网络能恢复完整李雅普诺夫谱（R²=0.98），且认证可在无校准数据的情况下事先指导感知预算分配，并可直接审计预训练模型（如TD-MPC2、V-JEPA），揭示参数规模不改善校准这一反直觉现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界模型的平均误差无法反映单次预测的可信度或有效时长，缺乏对可预测视界的可计算认证。作者试图建立一种能够为个体预测提供可证明的、分层级的可信视界的方法，并证明这种认证是等变结构的独有特性。

Method: 理论层面：证明等变潜在世界模型的T步rollout误差在对称轨道上恒定（定理A），并由李雅普诺夫谱按通道分层界定，给出上下界。实验层面：在40维Lorenz-96系统中对比Z_N-等变网络与密集/循环基线；在TD-MPC2和V-JEPA预训练模型上进行无训练审计。

Result: 等变网络在Lorenz-96上恢复完整李雅普诺夫谱（R²=0.98），密集/循环基线失败。认证可事先指导感知预算分配。在TD-MPC2上，认证与实际校准程度高度吻合（强扩张比0.94-1.02），且参数规模从1M到317M不改善校准。V-JEPA审计正确覆盖了过度乐观的正切谱。

Conclusion: 规模买来的是插值能力，而非校准后的预测视界。结构（等变性）才是可认证、可审计的预测可靠性的保障。交叉验证的审计结果，而非原始数值，才是可部署的对象。

Abstract: Scale buys interpolation; structure buys a certified horizon. A world model's average error says nothing about whether a particular prediction can be trusted, or for how long. For equivariant latent world models we give a computable, multi-step certificate of the predictable horizon: $T$-step rollout error is provably constant over each symmetry orbit (Theorem A) and stratified channel-by-channel by the predictor's Lyapunov spectrum, $T_j(ε)\sim\log(1/ε)/λ_j$. The horizon is two-sided -- a matching lower bound makes approximate equivariance provably horizon-limited -- and the certificate is exclusive to structure: orbit-constant error characterizes equivariance, so no non-equivariant model has it at any scale. Empirically, on 40-D Lorenz-96 only a $\mathbb{Z}_N$-equivariant network recovers the full Lyapunov spectrum ($R^2{=}0.98$); dense and recurrent baselines fail.
  Because the spectrum is faithful, the certificate acts, a priori: under a fixed sensing budget a $c\times$-inflated certificate provably needs $c\times$ the budget, and the equivariant certificate meets a budget its inflated dense counterpart cannot -- with zero calibration data. The same read-out, unchanged, audits public pretrained world models training-free: TD-MPC2 checkpoints land on the certificate's own scope taxonomy -- calibrated where strongly expansive (ratio 0.94-1.02), optimistic where weakly expansive, correctly abstaining where contracting -- a map a deployed monitor replicates cell-by-cell, out-of-sample. Across the official 1M-317M multitask ladder, calibration does not improve with parameters. On V-JEPA 2-AC (1B, real robot data) the measured cross-check correctly overrides an over-promising tangent spectrum -- the cross-validated audit, not the raw number, is the deployable object. Scale buys interpolation, not a calibrated horizon.

</details>


### [112] [ProPlay: Procedural World Models for Self-Evolving LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12780)
*Yijun Ma,Zehong Wang,Yiyang Li,Ziming Li,Xiaoguang Guo,Weixiang Sun,Chuxu Zhang,Yanfang Ye*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ProPlay 通过程序级别的世界模型和预演机制，使智能体在部分可观测环境中通过构建程序图（记录任务阶段间的因果转换及可靠性记录）来实现自我进化；智能体在每轮前基于图结构模拟未来轨迹作为软引导，执行后利用环境反馈细化图结构，从而闭环地持续优化对环境动态的理解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体方法虽有记忆或规划模块，但很少能闭环地将二者结合起来持续精炼对环境动态的内在理解，尤其是在部分可观测、需主动探索且反馈有限的场景下，智能体难以实现无需外部监督的自我进化。

Method: ProPlay 将成功的轨迹抽象为程序（procedure），并构建程序图（procedure graph）来捕获任务阶段间的因果转换；每个转换关联一个可靠性记录嵌入（reliability record embedding），用于估计其对特定任务的贡献。每轮前，智能体基于已知图结构模拟未来程序轨迹作为结构化软引导；执行后，利用环境反馈细化图结构，形成闭环自我进化。

Result: 在公开基准测试上的实验表明，ProPlay 在环境理解和自我进化能力上持续优于强基线方法。

Conclusion: ProPlay 通过程序级世界模型有效闭环了记忆与规划，使智能体能够不断精炼对环境动态的理解，实现了更优的自我进化效果。

Abstract: Self-evolving agents are expected to improve through interaction without external supervision, but this remains difficult in partially observable environments where agents must explore actively, learn from limited feedback, and decide when to trust prior experience. Existing LLM-agent methods often rely on memory or planning modules, yet they rarely close the loop between them to continually refine an internal understanding of environment dynamics. We introduce ProPlay, a procedural world model that supports procedure-level preplay, where agents can rehearse future procedural paths using the learned world knowledge. Rather than representing experience as isolated rules or low-level action constraints, ProPlay abstracts successful trajectories into procedures and organizes them in a procedure graph that captures causal transitions among task stages. Each transition is associated with a reliability record embedding to estimate its task-specific contribution from past outcomes. Before each episode, ProPlay simulates future procedural trajectories over known graph structures as structured soft guidance; after execution, it refines the graph using environment feedback. Experiments on public benchmarks show that ProPlay consistently improves environment understanding and self-evolution capability over strong baselines. Our code has been released in https://github.com/antman9914/proplay.

</details>


### [113] [PolyFlow: Safe and Efficient Polytope-Constrained Flow Matching with Constraint Embedding and Projection-free Update](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13400)
*Jianming Ma,Qiyue Yang,Yang Zhang,Liyun Yan,Zhanxiang Cao,Yazhou Zhang,Yue Gao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PolyFlow提出了一种多面体约束流匹配框架，通过将约束直接嵌入模型和流动力学中，实现了零约束违反、高分布保真度，并显著降低了推理延迟，适用于安全关键物理系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于流的生成模型在安全关键物理系统中部署时面临严格的约束要求，而现有的后验校正方法计算开销大且可能扭曲学习到的分布。

Method: 提出PolyFlow框架，包含离散时间流形式和无需投影的架构，将多面体约束直接嵌入模型和流动力学中，消除了离散化误差，保证严格满足任意多面体约束，无需昂贵的迭代求解器。

Result: 在多个规划和控制任务中，PolyFlow实现了零约束违反，同时保持高分布保真度；相比最先进的约束生成基线，显著降低了推理延迟，在安全性、效率和生成质量之间实现了有利的权衡。

Conclusion: PolyFlow通过将约束嵌入生成过程而非后验修正，有效地解决了安全关键系统中约束满足与生成质量的权衡问题。

Abstract: While flow-based generative models have demonstrated strong performance across a wide range of domains, deploying them in safety-critical physical systems remains challenging due to strict constraint requirements. Existing approaches typically enforce safety through post-hoc corrections, which incur substantial computational overhead and may distort the learned distribution. We propose PolyFlow, a polytope-constrained flow matching framework that embeds constraints directly into the model and flow dynamics. PolyFlow introduces a discrete-time flow formulation and a projection-free architecture, which eliminate the discretization error and guarantee strict satisfaction of arbitrary polyhedral constraints, without the need for expensive iterative solvers. Experimental results show that PolyFlow achieves zero constraint violation while maintaining high distributional fidelity across a range of planning and control tasks. Compared to state-of-the-art constrained generation baselines, PolyFlow significantly reduces inference latency and demonstrates a favorable trade-off between safety, efficiency, and generative quality. Code is available on https://github.com/MJianM/PolyFlow.

</details>


### [114] [SymQNet: Amortized Acquisition for Low-Latency Adaptive Hamiltonian Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12808)
*Yash Vardhan Tomar,Dheeraj Peddireddy,Vaneet Aggarwal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SymQNet，一种基于摊销强化学习的低延迟自适应哈密顿量学习方法。SymQNet离线学习后验条件采集策略，在线快速执行，相比传统贝叶斯主动学习方法（如Fisher信息搜索和BALD），在5量子位时决策延迟降低47.1倍和72.6倍，12量子位时完整模拟步骤仅需1.02秒（对比BALD的13.27秒），使自适应学习在实际低延迟场景中可行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自适应哈密顿量学习是量子设备校准与表征的核心，但传统贝叶斯自适应控制器每次后验更新后需重新计算实验设计，该步骤耗时数秒。在数百次射击的实验中，累积的延迟成为显著的计算开销，限制了自适应方法的实际应用。

Method: 提出SymQNet，采用摊销强化学习框架：离线阶段学习一个后验条件化的采集策略（acquisition policy），在线阶段仅需快速前向传播即可完成实验选择，同时保留贝叶斯后验反馈机制。该方法将计算密集型贝叶斯设计规则离线训练，在线推理时延极低。

Result: 在横向场伊辛模型基准测试中：5量子位时，相比有界Fisher信息搜索和有界两步BALD，决策延迟分别降低47.1倍和72.6倍；12量子位时，SymQNet完整模拟步骤耗时1.02秒，而有界两步BALD需13.27秒。

Conclusion: 学习式采集策略（SymQNet）使自适应哈密顿量学习能够适用于重复性低延迟工作负载，在保持贝叶斯反馈质量的同时大幅降低在线计算开销。

Abstract: Adaptive Hamiltonian learning is central to calibrating and characterizing quantum devices. In an adaptive controller, choosing the next experiment is itself a computation. Bayesian design rules are recomputed after every posterior update, and that step can take seconds. Across hundreds of shots, those seconds become a significant wall-clock cost for adaptivity. We introduce SymQNet, an amortized reinforcement-learning approach for low-latency adaptive Hamiltonian learning. SymQNet learns a posterior-conditioned acquisition policy offline, then uses a fast policy forward pass online while retaining Bayesian posterior feedback. On transverse-field Ising benchmarks, SymQNet substantially reduces acquisition latency relative to bounded Fisher-information search and bounded two-step Bayesian active learning by disagreement (BALD). At five qubits, it reduces acquisition-only decision latency by $47.1\times$ and $72.6\times$ relative to these online baselines; at twelve qubits, full simulated steps take $1.02$ s for SymQNet versus $13.27$ s for bounded two-step BALD. Overall, we show that learned acquisition can make adaptive Hamiltonian learning practical for repeated low-latency workloads.

</details>


### [115] [CLARITree: Cholesky and Lookahead Accelerations for Regression with Interpretable Piecewise Linear Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12840)
*Yixiao Wang,Hayden McTavish,Varun Babbar,Margo Seltzer,Cynthia Rudin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合前瞻搜索策略与高效秩一Cholesky更新的近最优稀疏分段线性回归树算法，在计算效率、预测精度和稀疏性之间取得良好平衡，且显著优于现有最优方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管基于动态规划和分支定界的最优方法能构建性能远优于贪心方法的回归树，但其计算开销过大，难以应用于一般的线性回归树。近期研究虽在分类任务中通过前瞻策略改善了这一问题，但回归场景下仍缺乏高效方案。

Method: 提出一种新颖算法，将前瞻式搜索策略与高效的秩一Cholesky更新技术相结合，用于求解近最优的稀疏分段线性回归树。通过Cholesky更新高效维护Gram矩阵，显著降低计算复杂度。

Result: 理论和实验均表明，该方法在计算效率、预测精度和稀疏性之间实现了良好的权衡，并且扩展性显著优于当前最先进的方法。

Conclusion: 本文证明了结合前瞻搜索与高效矩阵更新的策略能够有效解决线性回归树的近最优构建问题，为可解释且高效的回归模型提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Regression trees are among the most interpretable yet expressive model classes in machine learning. Historically, greedy induction has been the dominant approach for constructing well-performing regression trees. While optimal methods based on dynamic programming and branch-and-bound exist, they are computationally prohibitive for general linear regression trees, despite often achieving substantially better performance than greedy approaches. Recent work has shown that specialized lookahead strategies can dramatically improve runtime while maintaining near-optimal performance, primarily in classification settings. In this work, we develop a novel algorithm for near-optimal, sparse, piecewise linear regression trees that combines a lookahead-style search strategy with efficient rank-one Cholesky updates of the Gram matrix. We demonstrate, both theoretically and empirically, that our method achieves a favorable trade-off between computational efficiency, predictive accuracy, and sparsity, and scales significantly better than the current state of the art.

</details>


### [116] [TimeROME-DLM: Temporal Causal Tracing and Low-Rank Inference-Time Knowledge Editing for Masked Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12841)
*Zhengtao Yao,Liuyang Song,Hongbo Zhang,Chenhao Wei,Haoyan Xu,Guang Yang,Siheng Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TimeROME-DLM，首个无需训练、无需梯度的推理时知识编辑框架，适用于掩码扩散语言模型（MDLMs），通过时序因果追踪和低秩残差编辑记忆实现高效知识编辑，在TOFU任务上大幅降低遗忘集对数概率，且零额外VRAM开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有知识编辑方法（如ROME、MEMIT）仅适用于自回归Transformer，无法直接用于MDLMs的迭代去噪过程；而基于梯度更新的方法会导致巨大的显存开销（数十GB），且在标准学习率下会导致MDLMs崩溃。因此亟需一种无需训练、无需梯度的MDLM知识编辑方法。

Method: TimeROME-DLM由两个组件构成：（1）时序间接效应（TIE）因果追踪协议，定位每个事实中最能驱动后期去噪步骤中目标预测的坐标；（2）闭式低秩残差编辑记忆，聚合所有遗忘事实的subject键和目标增量，在每次扩散前向传播时对该坐标施加一个岭回归正则化更新，并通过稀疏化限制效用溢出。骨干权重完全冻结，仅需调优三个超参数（alpha, lambda, q）。

Result: 在TOFU forget01任务上，对TOFU微调的LLaDA-8B-Base，TimeROME-DLM将遗忘集对数概率降低约83 nats；同一配置可直接迁移至LLaDA-8B-Instruct、Dream-7B、MMaDA-8B、DiffuLLaMA-7B、LLaDA-MoE-1.4B等多种MDLM架构；保留集对数概率几乎不变（在安全操作点偏差约1 nat）；50个连续插入事实时，相比最强训练时基线获得4-14倍加速且零额外显存；可亚线性扩展至400个事实。

Conclusion: TimeROME-DLM以极低的计算成本弥合了自回归LLM与MDLM之间的定位-编辑差距，证明了训练自由的推理时编辑策略在扩散语言模型上的有效性。

Abstract: Masked diffusion language models (MDLMs) such as LLaDA now rival autoregressive (AR) LLMs, but every existing knowledge-editing and unlearning method (ROME, MEMIT, etc.) targets AR transformers and either makes assumptions that fail under iterative denoising, or requires gradient updates whose backward-pass activations cost tens of GB of extra VRAM and which collapse MDLMs at standard learning rates. We introduce TimeROME-DLM, the first training-free, gradient-free, inference-time knowledge-editing framework for MDLMs. It couples two components: a Temporal Indirect Effect (TIE) causal-tracing protocol that identifies, for each fact, the coordinate whose intervention most strongly drives the object prediction at later denoising steps; and a closed-form, low-rank residual edit memory that aggregates subject keys and target deltas across all forget facts and applies a single ridge-regularised update at that coordinate at every diffusion forward, with sparsification to limit utility spillover. Backbone weights stay frozen; only three hyperparameters (alpha, lambda, q) are tuned on a small validation split. On TOFU forget01 with TOFU-finetuned LLaDA-8B-Base, TimeROME-DLM cuts forget-set log-probability by roughly 83 nats. The same configuration transfers to LLaDA-8B-Instruct, Dream-7B, MMaDA-8B, DiffuLLaMA-7B, and LLaDA-MoE-1.4B. It keeps retain-set log-probability nearly flat (within ~1 nat at the utility-safe operating point) across 50 sequentially inserted facts, delivers a four- to fourteen-fold wall-clock speedup with zero additional VRAM over the strongest converged training-time baseline, and scales sub-linearly to 400 facts. TimeROME-DLM closes the locate-then-edit gap between AR LLMs and MDLMs at a fraction of the computational cost.

</details>


### [117] [Interpretable Factor Decomposition for Decision Intelligence in Large-Scale Financial Markets: Evidence from China's A-Share Market](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12843)
*Xiao Han,Yao Xiao,Zhen Zhang,Moxuan Zheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种可解释的机器学习流程，使用XGBoost和TreeSHAP方法对中国A股横截面收益可预测性进行归因分解。在2009-2019年3632只股票上，模型实现了月均2.38%的多空收益差（年化夏普2.23），且经Carhart四因子调整后alpha仍显著。SHAP归因显示行为信号（换手率和动量）贡献了58.2%的预测归因，远超估值比率（10.7%）。消融分析与SHAP的差异揭示了特征可替代性结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统横截面收益可预测性研究缺乏可解释性，难以将机器学习模型的预测能力分解为可审计的因子贡献，尤其是在特征存在共线性和替代性的复杂关系下。

Method: 使用XGBoost模型结合TreeSHAP归因方法，对3632只中国A股（2009-2019）进行60个月滚动窗口的55个月样本外预测。采用AUC和多空组合收益评估性能，通过SHAP分解进行因子归因，并与消融分析交叉验证。

Result: XGBoost模型平均AUC为0.547，多空收益差为+2.38%/月（Newey-West t=5.94，年化夏普2.23），Carhart四因子调整后仍达+2.31%/月（t=7.48）。SHAP归因显示行为信号占58.2%，估值比率仅10.7%。SHAP与消融分析的差异揭示了特征可替代性结构。

Conclusion: 可解释机器学习流程能有效分解股票收益可预测性，行为信号（换手率和动量）是中国A股收益预测的主导因子。SHAP与消融分析的联合使用可以揭示单一方法无法识别的特征替代性结构。

Abstract: We present an interpretable machine learning pipeline to decompose Cross-Sectional Equity Return Predictability into auditable factor contribution. We apply an XGBoost model with TreeSHAP attribution and conduct stress testing on 3632 Chinese A-share stocks from 2009 until 2019. Using 60-month, rolling windows over 55 months of out-of-sample data, XGBoost obtains a mean AUC of 0.547 and +2.38%/month (Newey-West t = 5.94; Annualized Sharpe 2.23) long-short spread for the top vs bottom quintiles. This alpha is persistent after adjusting for the Carhart four-factor model (+2.31%/month; t = 7.48). SHAP Decomposition indicates that behavioral signals (turnover and momentum) account for 58.2% of predictive attribution compared to 10.7% for valuation ratios, on average, across 55 industry groups. Ablation analysis serves to cross-validate this ranking and provides evidence that SHAP and ablation diverge in a manner that highlights feature substitutability structure that is largely invisible to either method used in isolation.

</details>


### [118] [Multimodal Graph Negative Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12863)
*Zhengyu Wu,Xu Wang,Hongchao Qin,Xunkai Li,Guang Zeng,Rong-Hua Li,Guoren Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出GraphMNL，一种图感知多模态负学习框架，通过负学习（Negative Learning）引导多模态属性图（MAG）中不同语义分支的学习，解决节点级分支语义不平衡问题——即不同模态分支在不同节点上信息可靠性和判别性不一致，避免传统对齐方法中主导分支偏差传播的缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态属性图（MAG）包含图拓扑、文本和图像等多种语义来源，不同分支在不同节点上的语义信息量和可靠性存在差异（节点级分支语义不平衡）。现有方法通过跨分支对齐或一致性约束来缓解异质性，隐含地将主导预测作为可靠监督，但当主导分支存在偏差时，强制模仿会将偏差传播到其他分支并抑制原始有用语义。

Method: GraphMNL框架包括：（1）构建分支库，整合结构拓扑、文本和视觉等多模态分支；（2）通过图感知可靠性仲裁机制识别主导分支与劣势分支；（3）门控不稳定传输，防止不可靠指导传播；（4）应用目标保留负学习（Target-preserving Negative Learning），对非目标类别施加负学习约束，使监督损失学习正确类别，负学习在不稳定的分支一致时抑制不可能类别。

Result: 在Grocery数据集上达到72.47%准确率，在Reddit M数据集上达到76.60 F1分数，综合实验表明GraphMNL取得了最佳性能。

Conclusion: GraphMNL通过将负学习作为跨分支指导机制，有效解耦了目标监督与分支指导，解决了多模态属性图中节点级分支语义不平衡问题，避免了偏差传播，并保留了对分类有用的原始语义。

Abstract: Multimodal attributed graphs (MAGs) integrate graph topology with heterogeneous modality attributes, such as text and images, thereby enabling richer modeling of complex relational systems. However, such expressiveness also makes learning on MAGs depend on multiple semantic sources, including structural topology, textual and visual attributes, each of which can be regarded as a branch for node representation. Node-level branch semantic imbalance arises when these branches differ across nodes in semantic informativeness and reliability: a branch that provides discriminative semantics for one node may mislead another due to bias in modality quality or structural context. Existing methods often mitigate such heterogeneity through cross-branch agreement or alignment, implicitly treating the dominant prediction as reliable supervision. When the dominant branch is biased, forced imitation may propagate its bias to other branches and suppress original semantics that are useful for classification. We propose GraphMNL, a graph-aware multimodal negative learning framework that addresses this issue by using Negative Learning as cross-branch guidance. Instead of forcing inferior branches to imitate a teacher prediction, the model teaches them which classes a node is unlikely to belong to. GraphMNL builds a branch library, identifies dominant and inferior branches via graph-aware reliability arbitration, gates unstable transfer, and applies target-preserving negative learning over non-target classes. This design decouples target supervision from branch guidance so that supervised losses learn the correct class, while Negative Learning suppresses unlikely alternatives when branch agreement is unreliable. Through the comprehensive experimental evaluation, GraphMNL achieves the best performance on Grocery datasets with 72.47% accuracy and 76.60 F1 score on Reddit M datasets.

</details>


### [119] [SMGFM: Spectral Multimodal Graph Pretraining for Multimodal-Attributed Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12867)
*Zhengyu Wu,Xu Wang,Hongchao Qin,Xunkai Li,Guang Zeng,Rong-Hua Li,Guoren Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SMGFM，一种频谱多模态图预训练框架，通过图频率分解为模态节点信号分配不同语义角色（低频拓扑一致/高频模态特异），再进行跨模态融合，在MAG数据集上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统图学习方法在图拓扑与节点特征耦合设计下，难以区分结构诱导语义（平滑拓扑变化）与模态内禀语义（局部细粒度区分），导致跨模态融合前语义角色混淆。

Method: 利用图频率变化作为先验，将模态节点信号分解为低频（拓扑一致语义）和高频（模态特异语义）；使用可扩展Chebyshev滤波器构建频率分辨模态令牌，通过拓扑条件路由估计耦合可靠性，在融合前进行频带-模态交互；频率路由目标同时优化平滑共识路径和模态特异路径。

Result: 在多模态属性图数据集上，SMGFM在图级和模态级任务上均达到SOTA性能。

Conclusion: 通过频谱分解解耦结构诱导与模态内禀语义，避免空间域纠缠和统一跨模态对齐，是MAG学习的有效范式。

Abstract: Multimodal-attributed graphs (MAGs) couple graph topology with node semantics from text, images, and other modalities. Traditional graph learning contextualizes node semantics by coupling topology with node features. However, this coupling design becomes troublesome in MAGs, where structure-induced and modality-intrinsic semantics may contribute differently to downstream tasks. Structure-induced semantics promote relational consistency through smooth topological variation, whereas modality-intrinsic semantics often encode local, fine-grained distinctions that should not be uniformly smoothed or aligned. Therefore, the key challenge is to identify semantic roles before cross-modal fusion. To this end, we leverage graph-frequency variation as a prior, where low-frequency components capture topology-consistent semantics and high-frequency components preserve modality-specific semantics. Based on this intuition, we propose SMGFM, a spectral multimodal graph pretraining framework that decomposes each modality-specific node signal into graph-frequency bands and assigns band-level semantic roles before cross-modal interaction. Concretely, SMGFM constructs frequency-resolved modality tokens with scalable Chebyshev filters, estimates their coupling reliability through topology-conditioned routing, and performs band-modality interaction before fusion. Its frequency-routed objectives align smooth consensus routes while preserving modality-specific routes, mitigating spatial-domain entanglement and uniform cross-modal alignment. Extensive experiments conducted on the MAG datasets demonstrate that SMGFM achieves state-of-the-art performance across graph-level and modality-level tasks.

</details>


### [120] [Multi-Bitwidth Quantization for LLMs Using Additive Codebooks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12876)
*Liza Babaoglu,Shuangyi Chen,Ashish Khisti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Drop-by-Drop，一种基于信息论和逐次细化的多比特宽度后训练量化框架，通过单一模型实现LLM权重的推理时精度控制，利用Matryoshka式监督和加法码本结构，在不同精度下保持竞争性能，显著降低存储开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在异构硬件上部署时面临资源约束差异，现有的量化方法通常需要为每种精度训练独立模型，无法在不重新训练的情况下自适应地管理性能与效率的权衡，因此需要一种能从单一模型实现多精度控制的方法。

Method: 基于信息论和逐次细化理论，利用LLM权重服从高斯分布的特性，在加权均方误差失真下实现最优重构；引入Matryoshka式监督到损失函数中，利用加法码本的结构特性，使有序子集码本在各精度下均能产生准确的部分重构。

Result: 单一检查点即可服务多种比特宽度，显著降低存储和内存开销；在Qwen、LLaMA、Gemma、Mistral等主流架构上保持有竞争力的困惑度和准确率。

Conclusion: Drop-by-Drop通过理论驱动的多比特宽度量化框架，有效解决了LLM在不同硬件上的自适应部署问题，实现了性能-效率权衡的动态调整，同时避免了为每个精度重新训练的开销。

Abstract: As large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed across heterogeneous hardware with varying resource constraints, the ability to adaptively manage the trade-off between performance and efficiency without retraining is critical. We propose Drop-by-Drop, a novel multi-bitwidth post-training quantization framework that enables inference-time precision control over LLM weights from a single trained model. Our method is theoretically grounded in information theory and successive refinement. We establish that LLM weights, which commonly follow a Gaussian distribution, can be optimally reconstructed with increasing fidelity as additional bits are incorporated, under a weighted mean squared error distortion motivated by LLM loss functions. To realize this in practice, Drop-by-Drop incorporates Matryoshka-style supervision into the loss function, exploiting the structure of additive codebooks. Drop-by-Drop produces a single model where ordered subsets of codebooks yield accurate partial reconstructions at each precision level. This approach significantly reduces storage and memory overhead by allowing a single checkpoint to serve multiple bitwidths, while maintaining competitive perplexity and accuracy across major architectures, such as Qwen, LLaMA, Gemma, and Mistral.

</details>


### [121] [LongSpike: Fractional Order Spiking State Space Models for Efficient Long Sequence Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12895)
*Xinrui He,Qiyu Kang,Xuhao Li,Zheng-Jun Zha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LongSpike提出了一种将分数阶状态空间模型（f-SSM）引入脉冲神经网络（SNN）的框架，通过分数阶微积分扩展传统整数阶ODE，解决SNN在长序列任务中存在的“无记忆”瓶颈，在多个基准上取得了优于现有SNN的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SNN依赖一阶ODE描述神经元状态，这带来了“无记忆”瓶颈，限制了模型对长序列任务中复杂长程依赖关系的捕获能力。

Method: 将控制理论中的分数阶状态空间模型（f-SSM）扩展到脉冲域，取代传统的一阶整数阶ODE；利用状态空间公式实现高效的并行训练，克服分数阶算子带来的计算开销和并行化困难。

Result: 在Long Range Arena (LRA)、WikiText-103、Speech Commands等基准上，LongSpike在准确率上超越现有最优SNN，同时保持稀疏突触计算优势。

Conclusion: 分数阶状态空间建模有效提升了SNN的长序列建模能力，为脉冲神经网络处理长程依赖任务提供了新范式。

Abstract: Spiking Neural Networks (SNNs) are well-regarded for their biological plausibility and energy efficiency in processing sequential data. However, dominant SNN architectures typically rely on first-order Ordinary Differential Equations (ODEs) to govern neuronal state transitions. This first-order assumption imposes a "memoryless" bottleneck, limiting the model's capacity to capture the complex, long-range dependencies inherent in long-sequence tasks. In this work, we propose LongSpike, a novel SNN framework that integrates fractional-order State-Space Modeling, or f-SSM, from control theory into the spiking domain. By extending traditional integer-order SSMs to the fractional-calculus regime, LongSpike enables the hierarchical integration of neuronal dynamics with long-memory kernels. To mitigate the computational overhead and parallelization challenges typically associated with fractional operators, we leverage a state-space formulation that supports efficient, parallel training. Empirical evaluations on challenging benchmarks, including Long Range Arena (LRA), large-scale WikiText-103, and Speech Commands, demonstrate that LongSpike outperforms state-of-the-art SNNs in accuracy while preserving sparse synaptic computation. The code is available at https://github.com/xinruihe389-commits/LongSpike.

</details>


### [122] [Selecting Samples on Graphs: A Unified Dataset Pruning Framework for Lossless Training Acceleration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12913)
*Dongyue Wu,Zilin Guo,Xiaoyu Li,Jiajia Liu,Jingdong Chen,Nong Sang,Changxin Gao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出统一图基数据集剪枝框架，将数据集建模为加权图，通过最大权重团问题选择样本，在保证近似理论保证下，实现ImageNet-1k上训练时间减少40%以上且不损失精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有剪枝方法依赖内在信号（样本独立评估）或外在信号（通过成对关系促进多样性），各自只捕捉样本效用的一个方面，在不同剪枝率或数据分布下缺乏鲁棒性。

Method: 将数据集建模为加权图，节点权重编码内在价值，边权重编码外在价值，将数据集剪枝转化为最大权重团问题（MWCP），并设计基于样本边际增益的贪心求解算法。

Result: 在ImageNet-1k上使用ResNet-50，训练时间减少40%以上且不牺牲准确率；在多个基准上优于现有剪枝方法。

Conclusion: 该统一框架在温和条件下具有形式化的近似保证，适用于广泛的样本重要性度量，并为实际设计提供了指导原则。

Abstract: The rapid growth of modern training datasets has significantly increased computational cost, motivating dataset pruning~(DP) methods which retain only a subset of informative samples to reduce training cost.
  Existing pruning criteria typically rely on either intrinsic signals that assess samples independently or extrinsic signals that promote diversity via pairwise relations.
  While effective in their own specific regimes, each captures only one aspect of sample utility and lacks robustness across different pruning ratios or data distribution.
  In this work, we present a unified graph-based DP framework.
  By modeling the dataset as a weighted graph, where node weights encode intrinsic value and edge weights encode extrinsic value, DP can be cast as a Maximum Weight Clique Problem (MWCP).
  Although MWCP is NP-hard, its structure admits a principled greedy solution based on sample-wise marginal gains.
  Under a few mild conditions, we further prove that this unified objective enjoys a formal approximation guarantee, which applies to a broad family of importance metrics and provides practical design guidelines.
  Extensive experiments show that our method outperforms existing DP methods while substantially reducing training cost, reducing training time by over 40\% without sacrificing accuracy on ImageNet-1k with ResNet-50.

</details>


### [123] [Where Computation Lives Inside TabPFN: Causal Localisation of Attention Head Function](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12917)
*Atharva Gupta,Dhruv Kumar,Murari Mandal,Saurabh Deshpande*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次对表格基础模型TabPFN 2.5进行因果机制分析，发现其特征注意力头存在明显的时间特化现象：一个主导注意力头的因果必要性在峰值层比其他头高2-5倍，其主导层随任务复杂度变化，而其余头呈现对称的后期层特征。通过注意力熵和激活修补提供了收敛证据。此外，对比激活引导在跨样本转移时失败，归因于TabPFN的上下文学习机制依赖上下文注意力而非稳定参数方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解表格基础模型TabPFN 2.5的内部计算机制，特别是其基于特征的注意力头如何在层间分配计算，以及探索推理时的可控性方法。

Method: 使用激活修补、消融实验和注意力熵分析，在两个合成回归数据集上进行因果机制分析，同时尝试对比激活引导进行推理时操控。

Result: 发现一个主导注意力头的因果必要性在峰值层比其他头高2-5倍；主导层的深度随任务复杂度变化；其余头呈现对称的后期层分布；对比激活引导无法跨样本迁移。

Conclusion: TabPFN的计算存在高度特化的注意力头分工，其上下文学习机制依赖于动态的上下文注意力，而非语言模型中稳定的参数方向，因此基于激活引导的推理时操控方法在此类模型中失效。

Abstract: We present the first causal mechanistic analysis of a tabular foundation model, investigating how TabPFN 2.5's feature wise attention heads distribute computation across layers. Using activation patching, ablation, and attention entropy across two synthetic regression datasets, we find clear temporal specialisation: one head's causal necessity dominates that of the others by 2 to 5 times at peak layer, with its dominant layer shifting across tasks of different complexity, while the remaining heads exhibit symmetric late layer profiles. Attention entropy and patching provide convergent evidence for the computationally active layers of the dominant head. We additionally investigate inference time steerability via contrastive activation steering, which fails to transfer across samples. We attribute this result to TabPFN's in context learning mechanism, which encodes task structure through context dependent attention rather than the stable parametric directions that make steering tractable in language models.

</details>


### [124] [LoRA-Muon: Spectral Steepest Descent on the Low-Rank Manifold](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12921)
*Franz Louis Cesista,Katherine Crowson,Cédric Simal,Stella Biderman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LoRA-Muon优化器，将Muon的谱最速下降规则应用于低秩微调，解决了LoRA训练中对初始化敏感、最优学习率跨秩迁移性差等问题，在计算匹配条件下低秩代理能恢复全秩最优学习率，且比现有方法更节省内存和计算资源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LoRA虽然大幅降低了微调的计算和内存成本，但使用AdamW等因子级优化器时存在初始化敏感、最优学习率无法跨秩迁移、难以超越全秩基线等问题，亟需一种更适合低秩场景的优化方法。

Method: 将Muon优化器的谱最速下降规则适配到低秩设置，提出LoRA-Muon，并引入分裂权重衰减规则（split weight-decay rule）。

Result: 在TinyShakespeare计算匹配实验中，rank-2 LoRA-Muon代理恢复了全秩最优学习率；rank-32 LoRA-Muon在种子平均扫描中达到比全秩基线更低的平均验证损失。最优学习率跨秩、宽度、深度和因子重缩放均保持迁移性。

Conclusion: LoRA-Muon是全秩Muon和Shampoo系列优化器的良好低秩代理，避免了QR分解和二阶矩存储，比LoRA-RITE和Spectron更高效、更稳定。

Abstract: Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) significantly reduces compute and memory costs for finetuning Deep Learning models but is often harder to tune than dense training: when using factor-wise optimizers such as AdamW, it is sensitive to initialization choices, its optimal learning rates transfer poorly across ranks, and it often fails to beat dense baselines. We derive LoRA-Muon by applying the Muon optimizer's spectral steepest-descent rule to the low-rank setting. Along with our split weight-decay rule, our main claim is that LoRA-Muon is a good low-rank proxy for full-rank Muon and Shampoo-family optimizers. Its optimal learning rates transfer across rank, width, depth, and factor-rescaling. In our compute-matched TinyShakespeare study, a rank-$2$ proxy recovers the dense best tested learning rate, and a rank-$32$ LoRA-Muon run attains lower mean validation loss than the dense baseline in the seed-averaged sweep. We further show that the Spectron optimizer depends on arbitrary factor scaling, so it would likely be a poor fit when finetuning starts from badly imbalanced factors, and that LoRA-RITE's simplified QR-coordinate core implements the same spectral update. LoRA-Muon computes that update without QR-decomposition and avoids storing second moments, making it more accelerator-friendly and memory-efficient.

</details>


### [125] [Order Is Not Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12923)
*Gareth Seneque,Lap-Hang Ho,Nafise Erfanian Saeedi,Jeffrey Molendijk,Tim Elson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出“秩序不等于控制”的核心论点，定义了基于接收器门控响应律（分母索引算子）的控制框架，并在生物（小鼠ALM、线虫、斑马鱼）、大语言模型、适配器和随机算子面板中验证了局部控制的存在性，发现控制具有介质依赖的局部特性（可接纳、饱和、变号、泄漏、过驱），并通过实验排除了坐标同一性和控制器等强结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对齐、可解释性、激活引导和神经扰动研究识别了诱导秩序的物体，但未能区分秩序与控制。本文旨在严格定义控制并建立可实验检验的控制理论框架。

Method: 定义分母索引算子（接收器门控响应律）作为控制的数学形式，在生物神经（小鼠ALM、线虫、斑马鱼）、LLM生成输出、适配器条件化、随机算子等四类面板中进行响应律拟合与预测验证，通过有限努力驱动目标位移的准则判定控制是否成立。

Result: LLM生成输出响应向量在四种材料条件下分量符号预测准确率72.8-73.7%，非零分量达84.3-84.8%；留出观察者对系统效应预测93.6%，目标/预言家家族预测91.7%。生物面板提供物理响应算子证据，适配器和随机算子面板分别展示了介质重塑和机会-策略分离。

Conclusion: 实验支持局部可接纳控制和可测量随机响应算子存在，但将部署前生成控制、隐藏/logit因果充分性、生物-LLM坐标同一性及字面热力学量排除在结论范围之外。

Abstract: AI alignment, interpretability, steering, and neural perturbation studies identify order-inducing objects. We argue that order is not control. Control requires a receiver-gated response law: a denominator-indexed operator mapping material state, action/drive, bath, and receiver state to response displacement, sinks, effort, and basin projection. We identify it across biological, LLM, adapter, and stochastic-operator panels. The laws are local: an intervention can be admitted, saturated, sign-changing, leaky, or overdriven depending on medium, bath, receiver state, action port, and comparator. Control is assigned when finite effort moves a target or outcome-readout class under the same denominator while damage, null/evasive, invalid format, overdrive, and unnecessary effort stay bounded. Mouse ALM, C. elegans, and zebrafish panels provide physical response-operator evidence while excluding coordinate identity and controller conclusions. LLM panels show generated-output response laws: across four material conditions, response vectors are predictable at 72.8-73.7% component-sign accuracy, rising to 84.3-84.8% on nonzero components; held-out observers predict system-effect and target/oracle families at 93.6% and 91.7% accuracy. Constitution-conditioned adapters reshape susceptibility as prepared media, and stochastic-operator panels separate measured opportunity from deployable action policies. This gives a driven-dissipative response-system account at the mesoscopic control level: drives act through prepared media, baths, and receivers, producing admitted movement, impedance, sinks, or overdrive. The evidence supports local admitted control and measurable stochastic response operators, while leaving deployable pre-generation control, hidden/logit causal sufficiency, biological-to-LLM coordinate identity, and literal thermodynamic quantities outside scope.

</details>


### [126] [Is Spurious Correlation Removal Always Learnable?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12930)
*Yibo Zhou,Bo Li,Hai-Miao Hu,Hanzi Wang,Xiaokang Zhang,Ruifan Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了不变学习中的计算困难性：即使不变结构在统计上可识别，在标准计算假设下仍存在计算障碍。当环境多样性不足时，不变性目标的曲率变差，导致样本效率与实际计算可行性之间存在根本性差距。本文推导了最小最大风险界，并揭示了标签偏移下的相变现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究不变学习在统计可识别情况下的计算可行性边界，揭示统计可识别性与计算可学习性之间的根本性差距。

Method: 通过黑盒可采样稀疏恢复原语建立计算下界；利用分离参数γ量化环境多样性；推导最小最大风险界；分析标签偏移下的相变行为；通过合成和真实数据集验证理论预测。

Result: 证明了在标准计算假设下，存在可采样多环境实例使得指数级搜索可行但多项式时间算法失败；推导出最小最大风险为Θ(k(d-k)/(n|ℰ|))；发现标签偏移下在n*∝k(d-k)/(|ℰ|γ²)处发生相变，估计误差与1/γ²成比例。

Conclusion: 不变学习面临计算-统计鸿沟：统计可识别性不等于计算可学习性；环境多样性是关键影响因素；简单多样性诊断可指导实际应用。

Abstract: Invariant learning can fail even when the invariant structure is statistically identifiable. We show a conditional computational barrier: under a black-box samplable supervised sparse recovery primitive motivated by average-case sparse-recovery reductions, there exist \emph{samplable} multi-environment instances with a one-dimensional predictive invariant subspace ($k=1$) that are learnable with polynomial samples by exhaustive search, while any polynomial-time constant-accuracy recovery algorithm would contradict the primitive. We further quantify environment diversity by a separation parameter $γ$, which controls identifiability and the curvature of invariance objectives. Under sufficient diversity and local Gaussian regularity, the minimax risk is $\mathbb{E}[\dist(\hat{V},V_{\mathrm{inv}})^2]=Θ(k(d-k)/(n|\mathcal{E}|))$, and under label-induced shifts a phase transition occurs at $n^*\propto k(d-k)/(|\mathcal{E}|γ^2)$ with refined estimation error scaling proportional to $1/γ^2$. Synthetic and real datasets illustrate the predicted gaps and transitions and motivate simple diversity diagnostics.

</details>


### [127] [Predicting Cognitive Load from Speech and Interaction Dynamics in Dyadic Conversations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12971)
*Tahiya Chowdhury*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究自然对话中通过语音和交互动态预测认知负荷的可靠性，分析53组双人协作任务的音频数据，使用静态声学、动态和交互特征训练GRU编码器，发现对话交互特征（如话轮转换动态、参与不平衡）能有效预测时间压力、脑力工作等认知负荷维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往认知负荷研究主要在受控实验室环境中进行，对其在自然协作对话中的可靠性了解有限，需要探索真实对话场景下认知负荷的预测能力。

Method: 分析53个双人组执行9项协作任务的音频，提取静态声学、动态和交互特征，训练双头门控循环单元(GRU)编码器预测认知负荷评分。

Result: 对话交互信号能有效预测认知负荷（时间压力、脑力工作、努力程度、任务表现）；时间需求与话轮重叠和说话人切换相关；脑力需求与说话人间参与不平衡相关。

Conclusion: 任务结构和对话交互对于在自然协作环境中建模认知负荷具有重要意义。

Abstract: Estimating cognitive load from speech has largely been studied in controlled laboratory settings, with limited understanding of its reliability in natural collaborative conversations. We investigate whether speech and interaction dynamics predict perceived cognitive load during dyadic conversations. We analyze audio from 53 dyads performing nine collaborative tasks and extract static acoustic, dynamic, and interaction features to train a two-head Gated Recurrent Unit encoder to predict cognitive load scores. Results show conversational interaction provides useful signals for predicting cognitive load related to time pressure, mental work, effort, and task performance. Temporal demand is associated with turn-taking dynamics such as overlap and speaker switch, while mental demand is linked to imbalanced participation between speakers. These findings highlight the importance of task structure and conversational interaction for modeling cognitive load in natural collaborative settings.

</details>


### [128] [EPM-JEPA: Operator-Side Experience Modulation in JEPA-Family World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12979)
*Vedant Pandya*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文比较了两种将累积经验注入JEPA预测器的方法（EI-JEPA通过残差注入，EPM-JEPA通过LoRA权重调制），在Moving MNIST重力偏移任务上发现二者差异不显著（EPM-JEPA相对EI-JEPA提升4.74%但未达显著标准），但EPM-JEPA一致优于无记忆基线1.90%而EI-JEPA劣于基线。核心贡献在于机制分析：D_shift轨迹由缓冲循环、EMA目标漂移和LoRA固有暂态（+0.021）三个独立动力学过程组成，而非收敛到均衡状态，并据此提出PEM-JEPA作为改进方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: JEPA系列世界模型使用静态预测器，当测试时动态分布与训练时不同时，预测器权重不会自适应调整，这限制了模型在分布偏移下的泛化能力。本文旨在探索如何将累积经验有效注入JEPA预测器以应对分布偏移。

Method: 比较两种经验注入机制：操作数侧注入（EI-JEPA）将压缩经验表示作为残差添加到预测器隐藏状态；算子侧调制（EPM-JEPA）通过LoRA生成低秩权重增量来调制预测器权重。在Moving MNIST重力偏移任务上进行预注册比较实验。

Result: EPM-JEPA (D_shift^{n=50}=0.7848±0.0078)与EI-JEPA (0.8238)差异为4.74%，根据预设标准为无效结果（Outcome C）。EPM-JEPA较无记忆基线(0.8000)提升1.90%且跨种子一致，而EI-JEPA低于基线。机制分析发现D_shift轨迹由三个独立动力学过程组成，而非收敛到均衡。

Conclusion: 两种经验注入方法在统计上无显著差异，但算子侧调制（EPM-JEPA）相比无记忆基线有稳定提升，而操作数侧注入（EI-JEPA）甚至不如基线。核心发现是指标轨迹由多个独立动力学过程组成，而非简单收敛，这为后续提出PEM-JEPA这一物理启发的改进方案提供了理论基础。

Abstract: JEPA-family world models use a static predictor whose weights do not adapt when test-time dynamics diverge from training. We compare two mechanisms for incorporating accumulated experience into a JEPA predictor under distribution shift: operand-side injection, where a compressed experience representation is added as a residual to the predictor's hidden state (EI-JEPA), and operator-side modulation, where the same representation generates low-rank weight deltas via LoRA applied to the predictor's weights (EPM-JEPA). On a pre-registered comparison (Moving MNIST, gravity shift), EPM-JEPA (D_shift^{n=50} = 0.7848 +/- 0.0078, three seeds) differs from EI-JEPA (0.8238) by delta = 4.74% - Outcome C: a null result - by our stated criterion, a valid outcome. As a secondary, non-pre-registered observation, EPM-JEPA improves 1.90% over a no-memory baseline (0.8000), consistently across seeds, while EI-JEPA underperforms the baseline, indicating the benefit is specific to weight-level modulation. Our primary contribution is a mechanism analysis: the D_shift^{n=50} trajectory reflects three independent dynamical processes - buffer cycling, EMA target drift, and an intrinsic LoRA settling transient of +0.021 - rather than convergence to equilibrium. These findings motivate PEM-JEPA, a physics-grounded successor addressing this dynamical-peak limitation.

</details>


### [129] [Exposure Bias as Epistemic Underidentification in Recursive Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12990)
*Riku Green,Zahraa S. Abdallah,Telmo M Silva Filho*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明递归多步预测中模型在部分可观测或状态截断条件下存在 epistemic underidentification 问题，即使确定性潜在动力学下，一步贝叶斯监督也无法识别递归部署时的预测器，并提出了基于诱导状态和来源变量的误差分解框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上将递归多步预测的挑战归因于分布偏移，但本文认为这种框架不完整。在部分可观测性或状态截断条件下，递归 rollout 还存在认知不确定性下的未识别问题，需要更深入的理论分析。

Method: 通过理论证明建立诱导状态 Z 和来源变量 P 的形式化框架，推导出诱导状态误差分解为教师强制/rollout 不匹配、表示-类别逼近和来源信息缺口三部分；并通过实验验证了诱导状态机制、局部校正任务以及闭环增益来源。

Result: 证明了 rollout 会进入不同的诱导状态区间；固定诱导状态定义了一个独特的局部校正任务；闭环增益不仅来自局部自适应，也来自 rollout 过程中诱导状态的变化；来源感知校正能进一步改善性能，但增益是有条件的而非统一的。

Conclusion: 本文重新将曝光偏差（exposure bias）解释为在自诱导的认知不确定性下的推理问题，为理解和缓解递归多步预测中的泛化难题提供了新的理论视角。

Abstract: Recursive multi-step forecasting is usually framed as distribution shift: models are trained on observed histories but deployed on their own predictions. We show this framing is incomplete by proving that, under partial observability or state truncation, recursive rollout is also an epistemic underidentification problem. Even with deterministic latent dynamics, one-step Bayes supervision identifies behavior only on observed contexts and need not identify the deployed recursive predictor once rollout queries self-generated induced states whose correct local targets are not determined by numeric state alone. We formalize this with induced states $Z$ and provenance variables $P$, and derive a decomposition of induced-state error into teacher-forcing/rollout mismatch, representation--class approximation, and provenance information gaps. Empirically, we show that rollout enters a distinct induced-state regime, that fixed induced states define a distinct local corrective task, and that closed-loop gains arise not only from local adaptation but also from changing the induced states visited during rollout. Using a simple binary provenance encoding, provenance-aware correction can further improve performance, though gains are conditional rather than uniform. These results recast exposure bias as reasoning under self-induced epistemic uncertainty.

</details>


### [130] [DeepJEB++: Foundation Model-Driven Large-Scale 3D Engineering Dataset via 2D Latent Space Augmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12994)
*Soyoung Yoo,Leekyo Jeong,Jinsu Ra,Dongeon Lee,Sunwoong Yang,Hyogu Jeong,Namwoo Kang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DeepJEB++是一个基于预训练基础模型的数据增强框架，通过2D潜空间扩散生成新设计，再提升为3D网格并自动进行有限元标注，最终将不足400个种子设计扩展为15,360个带仿真标签的3D支架数据集（40倍扩展），且仅需单GPU运行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据驱动的工程设计中缺乏大规模带物理性能标签的3D数据集，现有3D数据增强方法难以保留细微几何变化，且自动化仿真标注过程因边界条件随生成几何变化而困难重重。

Method: 三阶段框架：Stage 1在数据丰富的2D潜空间中进行扩散模型微调与潜变量插值合成新视图，并通过VLM质量过滤器保留可制造设计；Stage 2将验证后的图像通过领域适应的生成基础模型提升为3D网格；Stage 3自动识别载荷和螺栓接口并分配有限元标签（质量、应力、位移），无需人工干预。

Result: 从不足400个种子设计出发，DeepJEB++生成15,360个带仿真标签的3D支架，实现40倍扩展，且每个阶段仅需单GPU。评估维度包括可制造性、标签保真度（与SimJEB真值对比）和分布一致性。

Conclusion: DeepJEB++有效解决了工程设计中3D数据匮乏的瓶颈，展示了基础模型驱动增强的可行性，数据集将公开以支持可复现的工程AI研究。

Abstract: Data-driven engineering design is constrained by the lack of large-scale 3D datasets that pair geometry with physics-based performance labels. In particular, existing 3D data augmentation techniques have limitations in preserving subtle and diverse geometric variations, and it remains difficult to automate the subsequent simulation-labeling process, where boundary conditions vary depending on the generated geometry. We present DeepJEB++, a foundation-model-driven data-augmentation framework that expands a small seed set of jet engine brackets into a large, simulation-labeled 3D dataset under constrained resources. Our key idea is to augment in the data-rich 2D latent space, then transfer to 3D. In Stage 1, we fine-tune a pretrained 2D latent diffusion model on multi-view renders and synthesize novel views by latent interpolation, retaining manufacturable designs through a vision-language-model (VLM) quality filter. In Stage 2, the validated images are lifted to 3D meshes by a domain-adapted generative foundation model. In Stage 3, an automated pipeline recognizes the load and bolt interfaces on each mesh and assigns finite-element labels -- mass, stress, and displacement -- without manual intervention. We assess augmentation quality along three intrinsic axes: manufacturability, label fidelity against the SimJEB ground truth, and distributional consistency. Starting from fewer than 400 seed designs, DeepJEB++ yields 15,360 simulation-labeled 3D brackets -- a 40x expansion -- using a single GPU per stage. The dataset will be made publicly available to support reproducible engineering-AI research.

</details>


### [131] [Reliability of Probabilistic Emulation of Physical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12997)
*Sam F. Greenbury,Radka Jersakova,Paolo Conti,Marjan Famili,Christopher Iliffe Sprague,Edwin Brown,Jason D. McEwen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了两种主流概率物理系统预测方法——生成模型（扩散/流匹配）与CRPS训练的确定性集成模型——在同等模型规模和计算预算下的可靠性。结果表明，CRPS训练的集成模型在不确定性校准、推理速度方面更优；生成模型仅在原始空间（非潜空间）训练时可达到类似覆盖率但推理延迟显著更大。同时发布了AutoCast和AutoSim两个开源工具包。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管生成模型和CRPS训练的集成模型在概率物理系统预测中均展现出强预测精度，但两者不确定性预测的可靠性尚未被系统评估。作者旨在填补这一空白，在控制模型大小和计算预算的前提下，对两类方法进行公平对比。

Method: 构建统一评估框架，在多种二维时空物理系统上比较生成模型（潜空间/原始空间训练）与CRPS训练的集成模型。评估指标包括经验覆盖率（可靠性）、预测精度和计算效率，并控制模型规模和计算资源一致。

Result: CRPS训练的集成模型在单步预测和自回归 rollout 中均展现出更可靠的不确定性校准和更快的推理速度。生成模型仅在原始空间训练时覆盖率与CRPS集成相当，但推理延迟显著更大。CRPS集成在潜空间训练时覆盖率无明显退化。两类方法预测精度均良好。

Conclusion: 在概率物理系统预测中，CRPS训练的集成模型是更实用且可靠的选择，尤其在不确定性校准和推理效率方面。同时，开源工具包有助于推动该领域的后续研究与应用。

Abstract: Two dominant approaches have emerged for generating probabilistic forecasts of physical systems: generative models, such as diffusion or flow matching; and ensembles of deterministic models with stochasticity injected, trained using the continuous ranked probability score (CRPS) loss. While both approaches have demonstrated strong predictive accuracy, the reliability of their uncertainties has not been systematically assessed. We address this gap by developing a framework to evaluate both approaches across diverse 2D spatiotemporal physical systems, under matched model size and computational budget. We assess the reliability of probabilistic emulation by inspecting the empirical coverage of predictive intervals, while also considering accuracy and computational efficiency metrics. CRPS-trained ensembles typically achieve more reliable uncertainties on both single-step prediction and autoregressive rollouts, demonstrating better coverage than the standard alternative of training generative models in a latent space. Moreover, the CRPS approach offers significantly faster inference. When generative models are trained in ambient rather than a compressed latent space, which is often infeasible for high-dimensional problems, they exhibit comparable coverage to CRPS-trained ensembles, though with substantially larger inference latency. In contrast, when CRPS-trained ensembles are trained in latent space they do not show a marked degradation in coverage with respect to ambient space. Both generative models and CRPS-trained ensembles demonstrate good predictive accuracy. To facilitate future research and application, we release AutoCast, a modular framework implementing both generative models and CRPS-trained ensembles, alongside AutoSim, a flexible dataset generation package for rapid prototyping.

</details>


### [132] [scLLM-DSC: LLM-Knowledge Enhanced Cross-Modal Deep Structural Clustering for Single-Cell RNA Sequencing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13007)
*Ping Xu,Pengjiang Li,Tian Du,Zaitian Wang,Jiawei Gu,Ziyue Qiao,Pengfei Wang,Yuanchun Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出scLLM-DSC框架，通过融合LLM驱动的基因语义知识（NCBI先验+Cell2Sentence嵌入）与结构感知的拓扑视图（图引导编码器），并引入跨模态对比对齐机制，解决了现有scRNA-seq聚类方法忽视基因生物学语义的问题，在多个基准上显著优于11种SOTA方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有scRNA-seq聚类方法仅挖掘数值统计模式，缺乏对基因编码的生物学功能语义的理解；LLM虽有语义能力，但其生成式预训练目标与判别式聚类任务存在结构性不匹配。

Method: 提出scLLM-DSC跨模态深度结构聚类框架，包含两个视图：1）知识驱动语义视图（NCBI基因先验+上下文Cell2Sentence嵌入）；2）结构感知拓扑视图（图引导编码器）。通过跨模态对比对齐机制在统一潜空间中强制生物学语义与转录组特征的一致性。

Result: 在广泛基准测试中，scLLM-DSC在聚类准确性上显著优于11种最先进基线方法。

Conclusion: 融合LLM语义知识能够有效弥补传统scRNA-seq聚类方法的语义盲区，跨模态对齐是连接生成式语义与判别式聚类的关键桥梁。

Abstract: Clustering is fundamental to scRNA-seq analysis, serving as a cornerstone for identifying cell populations and resolving tissue heterogeneity. However, existing methods focus on mining numerical statistical patterns, suffering from semantic agnosticism by neglecting the intrinsic biological functions encoded by genes. While Large Language Models (LLMs) offer promising semantic capabilities, their direct adaptation to cell clustering is hindered by the structural mismatch between generative pre-training objectives and discriminative downstream tasks. To bridge this gap, we propose scLLM-DSC, a novel LLM-Knowledge Enhanced Cross-Modal Deep Structural Clustering framework. Diverging from data-driven paradigms, scLLM-DSC establishes a semantically-grounded representation by synergizing two views: a Knowledge-Driven Semantic View derived from NCBI gene priors and contextualized Cell2Sentence embeddings, and a Structure-Aware Topological View extracted via a graph-guided encoder. Crucially, we introduce a cross-modal contrastive alignment mechanism to enforce consistency between biological semantics and transcriptomic features within a unified latent space. Extensive benchmarks demonstrate that scLLM-DSC significantly outperforms eleven state-of-the-art baselines in clustering accuracy.

</details>


### [133] [CausalMoE: A Billion-Scale Multimodal Foundation Model for Granger Causal Discovery with Pattern-Routed Heterogeneous Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13024)
*Bo Liu,Di Dai,Jingwei Liu,Jiarui Jin,Xiaocheng Fang,Guangkun Nie,Hongyan Li,Shenda Hong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CausalMoE是一个百亿规模的多模态格兰杰因果基础模型，通过模式路由混合专家架构显式建模时间序列中的块级异质性，结合因果感知自注意力机制和LLM/VLM多模态对齐，在完全监督和少样本场景下均取得最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经格兰杰因果发现方法采用"一刀切"范式，无法捕捉真实时间序列中的分布偏移和动态机制变化，导致表示纠缠和虚假因果图。

Method: 1) 模式路由混合异构专家（Pattern-Routed MoHE），动态识别潜在时间模式并将块路由至领域专家；2) 因果感知自注意力机制（Causality-Aware Self-Attention），通过近端优化生成稀疏格兰杰因果图；3) 首次集成LLM和VLM，对齐数值信号与文本/视觉先验知识。

Result: 在完全监督基准上达到新SOTA，并能有效泛化到传统方法失效的少样本场景。

Conclusion: CausalMoE通过显式建模机制异质性和多模态对齐，解决了动态系统中因果发现的分布偏移问题，为时间序列因果推断提供了可扩展的通用框架。

Abstract: Granger Causal Discovery (GCD) is fundamental for analyzing temporal dependencies in complex systems. However, existing neural GCD methods predominantly rely on a "one-size-fits-all" paradigm, struggling to capture distribution shifts and dynamic regime changes inherent in real-world time series. This often leads to entangled representations and spurious causal graphs. In this paper, we propose CausalMoE, a billion-scale multimodal Granger causal foundation model that explicitly models patch-level heterogeneity. CausalMoE introduces a Pattern-Routed Mixture of Heterogeneous Experts, which dynamically identifies latent temporal patterns and routes patches to specialized domain experts, effectively decoupling regime-specific mechanisms from shared dynamics. To ensure interpretable graph recovery, we design a Causality-Aware Self-Attention mechanism operating across variables, yielding sparse Granger causal graphs via proximal optimization. Furthermore, CausalMoE is the first to integrate LLMs and VLMs to align numerical signals with textual and visual priors, regularizing causal estimation in complex scenarios. Extensive experiments demonstrate that CausalMoE establishes a new state-of-the-art on fully supervised benchmarks, while effectively generalizing to few-shot settings where traditional methods fail.

</details>


### [134] [TWLA: Achieving Ternary Weights and Low-Bit Activations for LLMs via Post-Training Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13054)
*Zhixiong Zhao,Zukang Xu,Zhixuan Chen,Xing Hu,Zhe Jiang,Dawei Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TWLA是一个后训练量化框架，通过对权重进行1.58比特三值化压缩、激活值进行4比特量化，在保持高精度的同时实现显著的推理加速。该框架包含三个核心组件：E2M-ATQ（两阶段优化的非对称三值量化器）、KOTMS（Kronecker正交旋转实现权重三模态分布并抑制激活异常值）和ILA-AMP（考虑层间交互的混合精度比特分配）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大模型三值化压缩方法无法有效处理重尾激活分布，导致激活值只能保持高精度，从根本上限制了端到端的推理加速。

Method: 提出TWLA框架，包含三个组件：(1) E2M-ATQ：通过从欧几里得初始化到流形重定位的两阶段优化，最小化权重三值化下的层输出误差；(2) KOTMS：应用Kronecker结构正交旋转将权重重塑为三值友好的三模态分布，同时该旋转统计上抑制激活异常值；(3) ILA-AMP：引入相邻层二阶交互代价进行比特分配，联合优化因共享正交变换导致的层间量化增益差异，防止弱层引发级联效应。

Result: 实验表明TWLA在W1.58A4配置下保持高精度，同时实现显著的推理加速。

Conclusion: TWLA通过创新的三值量化与低比特激活量化方案，有效解决了大模型压缩中激活值难以低比特量化的瓶颈，实现了精度与效率的良好平衡。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) exhibit exceptional general language processing capabilities, but their memory and compute costs hinder deployment. Ternarization has emerged as a promising compression technique, offering significant reductions in model size and inference complexity. However, existing methods struggle with heavy-tailed activation distributions and therefore keep activations in high precision, fundamentally limiting end-to-end inference acceleration. To overcome this limitation, we propose TWLA, a post-training quantization (PTQ) framework that achieves 1.58-bit weight compression and 4-bit activation quantization while maintaining high accuracy. TWLA comprises three components: (1) Euclidean-to-Manifold Asymmetric Ternary Quantizer (E2M-ATQ) minimizes layer-output error under weight ternarization via a two-stage optimization from Euclidean initialization to manifold relocation; (2) Kronecker Orthogonal Tri-Modal Shaping (KOTMS) applies a Kronecker-structured orthogonal rotation to reshape weights into ternary-friendly tri-modal distributions, while the shared rotation statistically suppresses activation outliers; and (3) Inter-Layer Aware Activation Mixed Precision (ILA-AMP) explicitly introduces adjacent-layer second-order interaction costs in bit allocation and jointly optimizes for the layer-wise disparity of activation quantization gains induced by the shared orthogonal transform, preventing cascades triggered by a few weak layers. Extensive experiments demonstrate that TWLA maintains high accuracy under W1.58A4, while delivering significant inference acceleration. The code is available at <https://github.com/Kishon-zzx/TWLA>.

</details>


### [135] [A green solvent screening tool for emerging materials via uncertainty aware, transformer enhanced transfer learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13060)
*Ioannis Kouroudis,Simon Ternes,Zhaosu Gu,Gohar Ali Siddiqui,Marina Ustinova,Angelo Lembo,Alessio Gagliardi,Aldo Di Carlo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出利用预训练基础模型迁移学习，在有限数据下预测溶解度相关参数，并通过不确定性量化增强可信度，最终部署了可用于绿色溶剂筛选的工具，发现了已知和新型绿色溶剂替代品。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 溶解度预测对材料科学和可持续化学至关重要，尤其是有机光伏、电池、催化等新兴技术对溶剂需求快速增长，亟需用绿色溶剂替代传统溶剂。然而溶解度关键参数数据稀缺，限制了传统机器学习方法的效果。

Method: 将预训练于QM9目标的基础模型迁移到溶解度预测任务，仅需少量数据即可训练；集成不确定性量化机制评估预测置信度；部署易于使用、可与高通量实验室集成的定制化工具进行溶剂排序和筛选。

Result: 成功预测了存在大量数据库的Hansen溶解度参数和介电常数；在数据极有限的Gutmann给体和受体数等目标任务上实现了高模型性能；将溶解度描述符数据量级提升了多个数量级；重新发现了已知绿色溶剂替代品并提出了新候选物。

Conclusion: 该迁移学习框架有效克服了溶解度数据稀缺问题，不确定性量化增强了预测可靠性，部署的工具在绿色溶剂筛选和替代方面具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: Accurate prediction of solubility remains a central challenge across materials science and sustainable chemistry. In particular due to emerging technologies like organic and hybrid photovoltaics, batteries, and catalysis, solvent usage is expected to increase significantly within the coming years. Therefore, substituting solvents with greener alternatives is vital. This is where machine learning can have substantial impact. However, the limited data on critical parameters of solubility significantly constraints machine learning efficacy. In this work, we transfer a pre-trained foundational model on QM9 targets to our application with minimal data requirements. Additionally, the pipeline integrates uncertainty quantification, allowing the user to gauge the confidence of the predictions. As baseline, we succeed in predicting the Hansen solubility parameters and Dielectric Constant for which extensive databases exist. Importantly, we achieve high model performance on additional targets, such as Gutmann Donor and Acceptor numbers, where the available data is extremely limited. Overall, we augment data on solubility descriptors by orders of magnitude with high quality predictions. For effective dissemination, we deploy easy-to-use, easily integrateable with high throughput labs, customizable tool for ranking and screening possible solvent substitutes. Finally, we rediscovered known green solvent alternatives and proposed new candidates proving its relevance for finding eco-friendly solvents.

</details>


### [136] [Limits of spectral learning under noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13067)
*Sabin Roman,Ljupco Todorovski,Saso Dzeroski,Marta Sales-Pardo,Roger Guimera*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了带噪声监督回归中谱表示的学习稳定性，发现噪声导致系数向量发生可预测漂移，并推导出无噪声与带噪声系数重叠度的闭式表达式，揭示了由单一本征噪声尺度控制的普适退化曲线，证明谱学习存在基本噪声阈值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从含噪数据中学习函数关系是科学推理的核心问题，但谱方法在噪声下系数的稳定性尚不明确，亟需理论理解。

Method: 研究带加性标签噪声的监督回归，使用稀疏谱表示（多基多维度）；对经验特征几何进行白化处理，推导无噪声与带噪声系数向量重叠度的闭式表达式。

Result: 噪声导致学习到的系数向量产生可预测漂移，漂移幅度取决于有效活跃谱模式数量；存在由单一本征噪声尺度控制的普适退化曲线；在Fourier、Legendre、Bessel、Haar基上的数值实验验证了理论预测。

Conclusion: 谱学习存在基本噪声阈值，超过该阈值系数估计变得不稳定，从而限制了从含噪数据中恢复函数结构的固有能力。

Abstract: Learning functional relationships from noisy data is a central problem in scientific inference. Spectral methods approximate unknown functions by expanding them in a basis and estimating the corresponding coefficients from data, but the stability of these coefficients under noise remains poorly understood. Here we study supervised regression with additive label noise using sparse spectral representations across multiple bases and dimensions. We show that noise induces a predictable drift in the learned coefficient vector whose magnitude depends on the effective number of active spectral modes. After whitening the empirical feature geometry, we derive a closed-form expression for the overlap between noisy and noiseless coefficient vectors, revealing a universal degradation curve governed by a single intrinsic noise scale. Numerical experiments across Fourier, Legendre, Bessel, and Haar bases confirm the theoretical prediction. The results demonstrate that spectral learning exhibits a fundamental noise threshold beyond which coefficient estimates become unstable, placing intrinsic limits on recovering functional structure from noisy data.

</details>


### [137] [Emotional regulation improves deep learning-based image classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13081)
*Riccardo Emanuele Landi,João M. F. Rodrigues,Marta Chinnici*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出情感调节（Emotional Regulation）框架，通过引入人工主观体验来建模情感，在图像分类任务中超越现有情感增强深度学习方法，成为大规模视觉数据集上的新SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有情感增强深度学习方法仅依赖客观神经生理因素，忽视了情感的主观性。本文旨在弥合这一差距，通过人工主观体验将情感引入深度学习。

Method: 提出Emotional Regulation框架，基于情感刺激进行预训练，在下游任务优化中平衡非情感响应与情感影响响应。在ResNet和ViT架构上，使用四个情感数据集进行预训练，以CIFAR-10和CIFAR-100为目标基准进行图像分类实验。

Result: 在图像分类任务上超越原始ResNet和ViT骨干网络，并超越现有相关方法，成为情感增强深度学习在大规模视觉数据集上的新SOTA。

Conclusion: 情感状态能够改善机器学习任务优化，Emotional Regulation作为通过人工主观经验定义情感增强深度学习的有效方法，值得进一步探索情感启发式架构。

Abstract: Emotion significantly influences cognition, enhancing memory and learning under certain conditions. Drawing on this principle, emotion-augmented deep learning investigates how affective states can improve neural network architectures and learning paradigms, achieving better generalization than non-emotional models. However, existing methods often rely solely on objective neurophysiological factors, neglecting the role of subjectivity in emotion. To bridge this gap, the present study introduces Emotional Regulation, a novel framework for modeling emotion in deep learning through artificial subjective experience. The method employs pre-training based on affective stimuli, balancing non-emotional and emotionally-influenced responses in downstream task optimization. Extensive experimentation was conducted in image classification, pre-training ResNet and ViT architectures on four emotional datasets, using CIFAR-10 and -100 as target benchmarks. Results reveal improvements over the aforementioned backbones, providing evidence of Emotional Regulation as a promising method for defining emotion-augmented deep learning through artificial subjective experience. Furthermore, the proposed approach overcomes the related work in image classification based on CIFAR, revealing Emotional Regulation as the new state-of-the-art in emotion-augmented deep learning for large-scale vision datasets. The study also enforces evidence of the impact of affective states in improving machine learning tasks' optimization, encouraging further investigation on emotion-inspired architectures.

</details>


### [138] [Authority, Truth, and Citation Bias: A Large-Scale Multi-Domain Benchmark for Studying Epistemic Susceptibility in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13104)
*Aryan Khurana,Aravind Ramana RN,Dhruv Kumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出AuthorityBench基准，通过22万+提示的多域实验发现：引用（无论真假）会显著提升大模型的幻觉率，且伪造引用+真实陈述时效果最强（幻觉率提高3-22个百分点），而场所声望和作者人口统计学信息影响可忽略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究关注引用内容对模型的影响，但引用本身作为权威信号如何独立于事实内容影响大模型的认知行为（epistemic behavior）尚不明确。

Method: 构建220,564个提示的多域基准AuthorityBench，采用完全平衡的2x2因子设计（事实真假×引用真假），覆盖通用知识、科学、法律、医学四个领域，控制40种提示模板、4个场所声望等级和国别作者姓名数据集，在7个模型上评估12个结构化研究问题。

Result: 引用（无论真假）均增加幻觉率；伪造引用+真实陈述时幻觉率提升3-22个百分点，通用知识域达35-77%；法律领域相对稳健；场所声望和作者人口统计学影响可忽略。

Conclusion: 引用本身的权威信号足以扭曲模型行为，即使引用是伪造的也会导致更多幻觉，这揭示了当前LLM在引用增强场景下的脆弱性。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly deployed in citation-augmented settings, yet the effect of citation presence on model behavior independent of factual content remains poorly understood. We introduce AuthorityBench, a 220,564-prompt multi-domain benchmark that isolates how citation-based authority signals influence epistemic behavior in LLMs. The benchmark uses a fully balanced 2x2 factorial design crossing claim veracity with citation veracity, the first to do so, across four domains (general knowledge, science, law, and medicine), with controlled variation over 40 prompt templates, four venue prestige tiers, and a country-coded author name dataset. Evaluating seven models on 12 structured research questions, we find that citation presence, whether real or fabricated, consistently increases hallucination rates relative to a no-citation baseline. The effect is strongest when fabricated citations accompany true claims, raising hallucination rates by 3 to 22 percentage points and reaching 35 to 77% in the general knowledge domain, while legal claims are comparatively robust and venue prestige and author demographics show negligible impact. All datasets and evaluation code are available at: https://github.com/floating-reeds/AuthorityBench

</details>


### [139] [Disparate Impact in Synthetic Data Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13105)
*Paul Andrey,Michaël Perrot,Batiste Le Bars,Marc Tommasi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文重新审视了合成数据生成（SDG）中的"差异性影响"公平性概念，指出非差异性影响应实现合成分布与真实分布一致，而非如现有工作那样修正偏差。分析了SDG方法因表达能力、采样误差和差分隐私机制导致近似与估计误差在不同群体间不一致的问题，并提出分组学习SDG模型的策略来提升整体效用及其公平性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有公平SDG工作通过修正观测分布中的不当偏差来追求公平，但这实际上改变了真实数据分布。作者认为非差异性影响应当要求合成分布与真实分布保持一致，因此需要重新审视这一公平性定义，并探究SDG为何无法达到该目标。

Method: 从理论上分析了SDG方法在表达能力（相对于分布复杂度）、群体比例导致的采样误差、以及差分隐私机制引入的估计误差三个方面如何导致近似与估计误差在不同群体间产生差异性影响。在人工和真实数据上以概率图模型为例进行实验验证，并提出分组学习SDG模型的策略。

Result: 展示了SDG方法在多种设置下确实存在差异性影响问题，而分组学习SDG模型的策略能够在许多场景下同时提升整体效用及其公平性。

Conclusion: 公平SDG不应以改变真实分布为代价，而应追求合成分布与真实分布的一致性；分组学习策略是一种有效的缓解手段。

Abstract: We revisit the fairness notion of disparate impact for synthetic data generation (SDG), that assesses whether the utility of generated records is the same across sensitive groups. Our approach departs from existing work on fair SDG, that address the problem of correcting for undue biases in the observed distribution, hence redefining SDG as learning a distribution that is not that of the real data. By contrast, non-disparate impact is notably achieved when the synthetic and real distributions are the same. We expose reasons why SDG may fail to reach that solution and discuss why approximation and estimation errors occur and can be disparate across groups. We notably look into the expressive power of SDG methods relative to distribution complexity, sampling errors due to group proportions, and estimation errors induced by differential privacy mechanisms. We illustrate cases of disparate impact on both artificial and real-world data, focusing on SDG methods that rely on probabilistic graphical models. We also introduce a strategy of learning group-wise SDG models and illustrate how it can improve both the overall utility and its parity in many settings.

</details>


### [140] [Demystifying Hidden-State Recurrence: Switchable Latent Reasoning with On-Policy Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13106)
*Jiayu Yang,Chao Chen,Shengen Wu,Yinhong Liu,Yuxuan Fan,Lujundong Li,Songning Lai,Chengwei Qin,Zhijiang Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SWITCH框架通过引入离散边界标记<swi>和</swi>，使隐藏状态循环的潜在推理链能够兼容标准在线强化学习（GRPO），并支持因果机制分析，在性能上优于同类方法，且揭示了边界标记的局部化策略属性、潜在步骤的因果重要性以及计算集中于入口状态转移等发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有潜在思维链（latent CoT）使用连续隐藏状态循环替代可见推理轨迹，但难以用标准在线强化学习优化，且缺乏因果可解释性。

Method: 提出SWITCH框架：在潜在推理块两端插入离散边界标记<swi>和</swi>，使GRPO策略比率在每个决策点良定义；采用可见到潜在的课程学习策略，以及Switch-GRPO目标函数通过循环潜在计算传播梯度。

Result: SWITCH在相同规模下一致优于先前的隐藏状态循环潜在推理方法；通过边界标记的机制分析发现：(i) <swi>是局部化的学习切换策略；(ii) 潜在步骤执行问题特定的因果重要计算；(iii) 计算集中在入口处的单次隐藏状态转移。

Conclusion: 隐藏状态循环的潜在推理既可被在线RL训练，也可直接进行机制分析，包括理解RL如何从内部改进模型。

Abstract: Latent chain-of-thought compresses reasoning by replacing visible reasoning traces with continuous hidden-state recurrence, but existing formulations are difficult to optimize with standard on-policy reinforcement learning (RL) and hard to interpret causally. Our key insight is that a single pair of explicit boundary tokens can address both issues at once: discrete entry and exit anchors make the latent block compatible with standard on-policy RL, and the same anchors offer a natural foothold for mechanistic analysis. Motivated by this, we propose SWITCH, a switchable latent reasoning framework. The model emits <swi> to enter latent mode and </swi> to exit. Because the boundaries are ordinary discrete tokens, the GRPO policy ratio is well-defined at every decision point. The same anchors also expose the latent steps to direct probing and causal intervention. We train the model with a visible-to-latent curriculum and a Switch-GRPO objective that propagates gradients through recurrent latent computation. SWITCH consistently outperforms prior hidden-state-recurrence latent reasoning approaches at similar scale. Mechanistic analysis through the boundary tokens further reveals three findings: (i) <swi> is a sharply localised, learned switching policy rather than a stylistic artefact; (ii) the latent step it opens performs problem-specific, causally important computation rather than acting as an inert placeholder; and (iii) that computation is concentrated at a single hidden-state transition on entry. Together, these results show that hidden-state-recurrence latent reasoning is both RL-trainable and open to direct mechanistic analysis, including of how on-policy RL itself improves the model from the inside.

</details>


### [141] [Select and Improve: Understanding the Mechanics of Post-Training for Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13125)
*Akshay Krishnamurthy,Audrey Huang,Nived Rajaraman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过数学推理实验揭示了强化学习后训练中能力获取的两个核心机制：策略选择（strategy selection）和策略改进（strategy improvement），并探讨了SFT数据和RL数据各自的作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管强化学习已成为推理和编码模型训练的关键组成部分，但其底层机制尚不明确，缺乏从机理层面理解RL如何获取或增强能力的过程。

Method: 基于Qwen-2.5-1.5B模型进行受控的数学推理实验，分析RL后训练中能力获取的底层过程。

Result: 发现两个核心机制：策略选择和策略改进。SFT数据中的多样化推理策略有助于策略选择；RL数据中增加难度有助于策略改进。

Conclusion: 研究为RL训练提供了机理层面的洞见，并提出了继续扩展推理能力的实用干预措施。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning has rapidly emerged as a key component in the training of reasoning and coding models, yet it remains poorly understood from a mechanistic perspective. We study how and through what underlying processes capabilities are acquired or enhanced via reinforcement learning post-training. Our analysis, based on controlled math reasoning experiments with Qwen-2.5-1.5B, reveals two core mechanisms: strategy selection and strategy improvement. Our results highlight the role of SFT data and reinforcement learning data in activating these mechanisms, in particular showing how supervising the model on diverse reasoning strategies can enable strategy selection and how increasing difficulty in reinforcement learning data can enable strategy improvement. Taken together, our results provide mechanistic insight into RL training and suggest practical interventions to continue scaling reasoning capabilities.

</details>


### [142] [MiniPIC: Flexible Position-Independent Caching in <100LOC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13126)
*Nathan Ordonez,Thomas Parnell*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MiniPIC是一个轻量级的vLLM扩展，通过无位置编码的KV缓存和用户控制的缓存复用原语，实现了位置无关缓存（PIC），仅需不到100行核心引擎修改即可支持多种PIC方法，在2WikiMultihopQA上提升预填充吞吐量49%，并将缓存跨度首令牌延迟降低两个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的前缀缓存方案（如vLLM）要求请求共享相同前缀才能复用KV条目，而生产级推理服务器中的位置无关缓存（PIC）实现要么需要大量服务器代码修改，要么将KV状态保存在服务器外部导致主机到设备的传输开销，缺乏轻量级、高效的PIC解决方案。

Method: MiniPIC包含两个核心组件：1）无位置编码的KV缓存（存储未旋转的K向量，在注意力计算时按请求逻辑位置应用RoPE）；2）三个用户控制的令牌级原语（块对齐填充、跨度分隔符SSep、提示依赖PDep），用于修改哈希行为和块级因果注意力结构。仅需不到100行核心引擎修改和自定义注意力后端。

Result: 在2WikiMultihopQA上，MiniPIC配合交错调度相比基线vLLM提升预填充吞吐量49%，缓存跨度首令牌延迟降低高达两个数量级，保持未缓存跨度的线性预填充扩展，最坏情况开销仅为5.7%。

Conclusion: MiniPIC以最小化代码修改实现了多种PIC方法（包括Block-Attention、EPIC和Prompt Cache）在同一个vLLM实例中的原生集成，同时兼容KV缓存CPU卸载实现，为检索增强和智能体工作负载提供了高效、灵活的缓存复用方案。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented and agentic workloads repeatedly prefill recurring predictable structured inputs (which we call "spans") such as documents and code files. Yet, prefix caching in engines such as vLLM cannot reuse their KV entries unless they share identical prefixes with another request, while Position-Independent Caching (PIC) implementations within production-grade inference servers typically either require substantial server code changes or keep KV state outside the server, incurring host-to-device transfer overhead. We present Minimalistic PIC (MiniPIC): a minimal, flexible and fast vLLM design built from two ingredients: positional-encoding-free KV cache and user-controlled cache-reuse primitives. MiniPIC stores unrotated K vectors in the KV cache, applies RoPE to K tiles inside attention using per-request logical positions, and exposes three user-facing and token-level primitives: block-aligned padding, span separator (SSep), and prompt depend (PDep), that modify hashing behavior and effective block-level causal attention structure. With fewer than 100 lines of core-engine changes plus a custom attention backend, these primitives are sufficient to realize multiple PIC methods, including Block-Attention, EPIC, and Prompt Cache, within the same running vLLM instance, while natively integrating with KV cache CPU offload implementations. On 2WikiMultihopQA, MiniPIC with interleaved scheduling improves prefill throughput by 49% over baseline vLLM, reduces cached-span time-to-first-token by up to two orders of magnitude, preserves the linear prefill scaling of uncached spans, and incurs only 5.7% worst-case overhead.

</details>


### [143] [When Does Routing Become Interpretable? Causal Probes on Block Attention Residuals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13168)
*Aydin Javadov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究Block Attention Residuals（Block AttnRes）架构中路由权重的可解释性。通过对两种0.6B参数的检查点进行干预实验发现：仅靠暴露路由权重不足以进行机制解释——路由权重必须经过训练才能产生结构化模式，且平均路由质量与因果重要性之间存在显著分离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Block AttnRes通过用可学习的softmax替代固定残差连接，使跨层路由在前向传播中直接可观察，这为机制解释提供了诱人的目标。作者希望探究这种暴露是否足以支持机制解释。

Method: 对两个同规模（0.6B）Block AttnRes检查点进行相同的路由消融干预实验：一个是通过确定性近因偏差调度包装的vanilla Qwen3基线，另一个是从头训练的Block AttnRes Qwen3。比较两者的路由权重模式并进行因果干预分析。

Result: 1) 包装基线的路由权重与内容无关，复现了调度的分析预测。2) 训练的AttnRes检查点表现出三种局部路由模式：嵌入源路径（早期层MLP）、当前状态路径（早期层注意力和MLP）、旧历史路径（后期层注意力）。3) 平均路由质量与因果重要性存在显著分离：最大质量切片不是最大因果贡献，且一个源家族携带可观质量但无检测到的因果作用。

Conclusion: 架构暴露路由对机制解释是必要的但不充分的：结构化深度路由仅在路由参与训练时出现，即使如此，描述性路由摘要应被视为待测试的候选假设，而非机制本身的证据。

Abstract: Block Attention Residuals (Block AttnRes) by replace fixed additive residuals with a learned softmax over earlier depth-source representations, surfacing cross-layer routing as an inspectable tensor in the forward pass. This is a tempting interpretability target: information flow normally inferred indirectly is now directly observable. We ask whether such exposure suffices for mechanistic interpretation. We probe two same-scale ($0.6$B) Block AttnRes checkpoints under identical routing-ablation interventions: a vanilla Qwen3 inference-wrapped through a deterministic recency-bias schedule that the codebase admits as a routing-equivalent loading path, and a Block AttnRes Qwen3 trained from scratch with routing as part of optimisation. The wrapped baseline's routing weights are content-independent and reproduce the schedule's analytic prediction. The trained AttnRes checkpoint instead exhibits three localised routing motifs: an embedding-source pathway through early-layer MLP, a current-state pathway through early-layer attention and MLP, and an older-history pathway through late-layer attention. Beyond this stratification, we find a sharp dissociation between average routing mass and causal importance: in both sublayers, the largest mass slice is not the largest causal contribution, and one source family carries appreciable mass with no detectable causal role under intervention. Architectural exposure of routing is therefore necessary but not sufficient for mechanistic interpretation: structured depth routing emerges only when routing has been part of training, and even then, descriptive routing summaries should be treated as candidate hypotheses to be tested by causal interventions, not as evidence of mechanism in their own right.

</details>


### [144] [Detecting Explanatory Insufficiency in Learned Representations: A Framework for Representational Vigilance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13172)
*Jacques Raynal,Pierre Slangen,Elsa Raynal,Jacques Margerit*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: VER是一个用于监测学习表示充分性的概念性框架，通过识别持续存在的残差结构来检测表示的解释性不足，而非提出新的学习算法或损失函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估指标（如预测性能、鲁棒性、不确定性估计）可能掩盖表示中持续存在的残差结构，导致表示在操作上成功但在解释上逐渐失效，需要一种显式监测表示充分性的方法。

Method: VER定义了一个诊断流程，包括：表示识别、解释域界定、残差结构检测、解释阻力评估和警戒信号触发，将表示不充分性与普通预测误差、不确定性、噪声和分布偏移区分开。

Result: 提出了一个概念性框架VER，并概述了通过表示警戒基准进行实证评估的路径。

Conclusion: VER旨在补充而非取代现有评估方法，将表示充分性作为明确的探究对象，为表示诊断领域做出贡献。

Abstract: Learned representations are central to modern machine learning and are commonly evaluated through predictive performance, robustness, uncertainty estimation, or generalization. However, a learned representation may remain operationally successful while progressively failing to organize persistent residual structures that are not fully captured by conventional evaluation metrics. This article introduces VER, the Vigilant Evaluator of Representations, a conceptual framework for monitoring representational adequacy in learned representations. VER does not propose a new learning algorithm, loss function, or model architecture. Instead, it formalizes a diagnostic process through which persistent residual structures may be identified, analyzed, and interpreted as potential indicators of explanatory insufficiency. The framework distinguishes representational inadequacy from ordinary prediction error, uncertainty, noise, and distribution shift. It introduces a monitoring sequence based on representation identification, explanatory-domain delimitation, residual-structure detection, explanatory-resistance evaluation, and vigilance signaling. VER is intended as a contribution to representation diagnostics in machine learning. Its objective is not to replace existing evaluation methods but to complement them by treating representational adequacy as an explicit object of inquiry. A path toward empirical evaluation through representational-vigilance benchmarks is also outlined.

</details>


### [145] [Getting Better at Working With You: Compiling User Corrections into Runtime Enforcement for Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13174)
*Yujun Zhou,Kehan Guo,Haomin Zhuang,Xiangqi Wang,Yue Huang,Zhenwen Liang,Pin-Yu Chen,Tian Gao,Nuno Moniz,Nitesh V. Chawla,Xiangliang Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TRACE方法，通过将用户对话中的修正编译为运行时检查规则，解决了LLM智能体偏好记忆但执行不力的关键问题。相比Mem0记忆系统（57.5%的偏好检查被违反），TRACE在ClawArena上将分布内任务违规率从100.0%降至37.6%，分布外任务从100.0%降至2.0%，在MemoryArena任务上从100.0%降至60.5%，有效减少了用户重复纠正的摩擦。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交互式LLM智能体在跨会话的工作中无法可靠地记住并遵从用户之前的纠正偏好，即存在"偏好访问"与"偏好遵从"之间的差距。即便使用Mem0等记忆系统，仍有57.5%的适用偏好检查被违反，用户需要重复纠正相同问题，造成严重的交互摩擦。

Method: TRACE（Test-time Rule Acquisition and Compiled Enforcement）是一个即插即用的技能层流水线，包含三步：(1) 挖掘用户聊天中的纠正信息，(2) 将其重写为原子化规则，(3) 编译为运行时检查，在智能体完成任务前强制执行。该方法没有采用软性记忆（如提示注入），而是硬性规则编译强制执行。

Result: 在ClawArena编码任务上：分布内任务违规从100.0%降至37.6%，分布外任务从100.0%降至2.0%。在MemoryArena衍生任务上：分布内违规从100.0%降至60.5%，同时任务通过率匹配或超越最强记忆基线。相比Mem0记忆系统（57.5%违规率），TRACE显著提升了偏好遵从。

Conclusion: 将用户修正编译为运行时强制执行可以解决记忆系统单独无法可靠解决的重复摩擦问题，减少了用户在未来会话中重复陈述相同修正的需求。TRACE提供了一种有效的替代方案，弥补了记忆系统在偏好遵从方面的根本缺陷。

Abstract: Interactive LLM agents are becoming part of daily work, but they do not reliably become easier to work with over time: a correction remembered in one session may still be violated in the next. We study this gap between preference access and preference compliance. In tasks derived from anonymized real-user friction cases, Mem0 memory still leaves 57.5% of applicable preference checks violated. We introduce Test-time Rule Acquisition and Compiled Enforcement (TRACE), a drop-in skill-layer pipeline for coding-agent runtimes that mines user corrections, rewrites them as atomic rules, and compiles them into runtime checks that must pass before an agent completes future tasks. Unlike runtime checks written ahead of time by developers, TRACE skills come from the user's own chat corrections. We evaluate TRACE with simulated user-in-the-loop experiments on ClawArena coding-agent tasks and MemoryArena-derived memory-intensive tasks. On ClawArena, TRACE reduces held-out preference violation from 100.0% to 37.6% on in-distribution tasks and from 100.0% to 2.0% on out-of-distribution tasks. On MemoryArena-derived tasks, TRACE reduces in-distribution violation from 100.0% to 60.5% while matching or exceeding the strongest memory baseline on task pass. These results suggest that compiling corrections into runtime enforcement can address a repeated-friction failure mode that memory alone does not reliably solve, reducing the need for users to restate the same correction across future sessions. Experiment code is available at https://github.com/YujunZhou/TRACE_exp, and the deployable skill is available at https://github.com/YujunZhou/tellonce.

</details>


### [146] [Loss-Shift Transfer via Bayes Quotients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13178)
*Vasileios Sevetlidis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种与分布偏移正交的迁移学习失败模式——损失偏移（loss shift），即在固定数据分布下，不同损失函数需要不同表示，并通过贝叶斯商形式化证明了严格细化损失会带来信息丢弃，导致源表示无法直接用于更精细的目标损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的迁移学习主要研究分布偏移（distribution shift）导致的问题，但作者发现即使数据分布不变，损失函数的变化同样会导致表示失效，这是被忽视的故障模式。
