Method: 使用贝叶斯商（Bayes quotients）对损失进行排序和细化，从理论上证明了当目标损失严格细化于源损失时，源最小表示必然丢弃目标所需信息。对于有限输出的对数损失（log loss），将超额风险表示为表示所丢弃的条件信息量。

Result: 在受控实验、学习实验、合成图像和真实图像设置中验证了该效应：分类等价的表示在固定数据分布下可以具有不同的最优对数损失性能。

Conclusion: 损失偏移是迁移学习中与分布偏移正交的新故障模式，即使数据分布不变，迁移学习也可能因损失变化而失败，需要用贝叶斯商理论来指导表示选择。

Abstract: Transfer learning is usually studied as a consequence of distribution shift. This paper identifies an orthogonal failure mode in which the data distribution is fixed and the loss changes. This setting is called \emph{loss shift}. A loss determines which information in \(X\) is Bayes-relevant, and two losses may therefore require different representations even under the same joint law \(P(X,Y)\). The idea is formalized using Bayes quotients, which allow losses to be ordered by refinement. In the Bayes-quotient formulation, strict refinement gives an immediate qualitative obstruction. A source-minimal representation for a coarser loss is insufficient for a strictly finer target loss. For finite-output log loss, this obstruction becomes an exact quantitative identity. The excess risk is the conditional information about \(Y\) discarded by the representation. Experiments in controlled, learned, synthetic-image, and real-image settings show the predicted effect, i.e., classification-equivalent representations can have different optimal log-loss performance under a fixed data distribution.

</details>


### [147] [The Geometry of Phase Transitions in Generative Dynamics via Projection Caustics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13191)
*Ryosuke Sakamoto,Kotaro Sakamoto*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种几何视角来解释扩散模型和流匹配模型生成过程中的相变行为，指出尖锐转变出现在投影焦散点附近，并引入临界边界检测器（CBD）来诊断分数方向不稳定性，实验验证了CBD在定位模式承诺、预测干预敏感窗口和支持针对性控制方面的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型和流匹配模型虽采用连续反向时间动力学，但生成样本常出现突然的定性变化（如模式切换、语义塌缩），现有方法缺乏对这一相变行为的几何解释和诊断工具。

Method: 将去噪过程视为自由能景观上的梯度下降，揭示尖锐转变出现在近邻投影焦散点处（数据支撑上的最近点投影不再唯一），并基于此开发了临界边界检测器（CBD）作为分数方向不稳定性的实用诊断工具。

Result: 在玩具模型、标准扩散模型和潜在文本到图像扩散模型上，CBD能够定位模式承诺时刻、预测干预敏感窗口，并在几何敏感区域支持有针对性的控制。

Conclusion: 建立了数据几何结构与扩散生成动力学之间的联系，为理解和控制生成过程中的相变行为提供了理论框架和实用工具。

Abstract: Continuous-state generative samplers, including diffusion and flow-matching models, evolve through continuous reverse-time dynamics, yet their samples often undergo abrupt qualitative changes: trajectories commit to modes, semantic alternatives collapse, and small perturbations in narrow time windows can produce large downstream effects. This paper develops a geometric account of such phase-transition-like behaviour. We view denoising as gradient descent on a free energy landscape and show that sharp transitions arise near projection caustics, where the nearest-point projection onto the data support ceases to be unique. Motivated by this perspective, we introduce the Critical Boundary Detector (CBD), as practical diagnostics for score-direction instability. Across toy models, standard diffusion models, and latent text-to-image diffusion models, CBD localises mode commitment, predicts intervention-sensitive windows, and supports targeted control in geometrically sensitive regions. Our results connect geometry of data and dynamics of diffusion generation.

</details>


### [148] [WHAR Arena: Benchmarking the State of the Art in Efficient Wearable Human Activity Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13194)
*Maximilian Burzer,Tobias King,Till Riedel,Michael Beigl,Tobias Röddiger*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文构建了包含30个标准化数据集的WHAR基准测试，评估了17种架构共4760次训练运行，发现当前模型预测性能已趋近天花板，但部署效率和领域自适应方面仍有巨大提升空间，紧凑模型和随机森林在效率-性能Pareto前沿上占优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可穿戴人体活动识别领域存在可比性危机：研究使用不一致的数据集、自定义数据处理和不同评估协议，导致最优方法声称不可靠，亟需统一标准化基准。

Method: 整合30个多样化数据集，采用标准化预处理、统一模型接口和共享的跨被试评估协议；在Android参考设备上联合测量17种代表性架构的预测性能（macro-F1）及延迟、峰值内存、模型大小等部署效率指标，共进行4760次训练运行。

Result: 当前最优方法呈分布态而非单一架构主导；CNN-HAR取得最高平均macro-F1，但顶尖模型性能紧密聚集，表明预测性能已趋近天花板；紧凑模型（TinierHAR）和经典随机森林定义了实际相关的Pareto前沿，而大型循环/混合模型硬件成本高但性能增益不匹配。

Conclusion: WHAR预测性能已进入平台期，未来进展应聚焦于优化部署效率和改进领域自适应能力，而不是追求更高的准确率。

Abstract: Deep learning has become the dominant paradigm in Wearable Human Activity Recognition (WHAR), yet progress is obscured by a comparability crisis. Results are often reported using inconsistent datasets, custom data processing, and varying evaluation protocols, making state-of-the-art claims fragile. We address this with a large-scale, open-source benchmark that integrates 30 diverse datasets under standardized processing, unified model interfaces, and a shared cross-subject evaluation protocol. Evaluating 17 representative architectures across 4760 training runs, we jointly measure predictive performance alongside on-device latency, peak memory, and model size on an Android reference device. Our results reveal that the WHAR state of the art is distributed rather than dominated by a single architecture. While CNN-HAR achieves the highest mean macro-F1, top-performing models cluster tightly, indicating contemporary architectures have converged near a predictive performance ceiling. When accounting for deployment efficiency, compact neural models, such as TinierHAR, and classical Random Forests define the practically relevant Pareto frontier, whereas larger recurrent and hybrid models incur high hardware costs without corresponding performance gains. Consequently, while predictive performance has plateaued, substantial potential for future progress remains in optimizing deployment efficiency and improving adaptation to domain shifts. We release our full framework to support transparent reuse and extension.

</details>


### [149] [Understanding helpfulness and harmless tension in reward models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13209)
*Eshaan Tanwar,Pepa Atanasova*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究RLHF中奖励模型在有用性和无害性对齐目标下的内部机制，发现混合目标模型表现不如单一目标模型，识别了与各目标相关的神经元，并发现共享神经元对对齐张力有不成比例的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 奖励模型是RLHF的关键组件，但有用性和无害性这两个对齐目标之间的内部机制及冲突尚未被充分理解。

Method: 在有用性-only、无害性-only和混合目标设置下训练奖励模型，使用基于激活的方法识别与各目标相关的神经元，并通过定向消融研究其功能角色。

Result: 混合目标模型性能低于单一目标模型；目标相关神经元因果支持对应目标但常对对立目标产生负面影响；相当比例的神经元在有用性和无害性之间共享，且这些共享神经元对模型行为产生不成比例的影响，导致对齐张力。

Conclusion: 研究为对齐目标在奖励模型中的表征提供了机制性解释，揭示了多目标对齐困难的根源，激励未来在解耦和可控对齐方法上的研究。

Abstract: Reward models are a key component of reinforcement learning from human feedback (RLHF), aligning language models toward both helpful and harmless behaviour. However, the internal mechanisms underlying these objectives and their conflicts remain poorly understood. We study alignment tension in reward models trained under helpfulness-only, harmlessness-only, and mixed-objective settings. We find that mixed-objective models often underperform single-objective models, indicating interference between objectives. Using activation-based methods, we identify neurons associated with each objective and study their functional roles via targeted ablations. We find that these neurons causally support their corresponding objectives while often negatively affecting the opposing one. We find that a substantial proportion of neurons are shared between helpfulness and harmlessness, and that these shared neurons exert a disproportionate influence on model behaviour, contributing to alignment tension. Additionally, our results provide insights and mechanistic interpretation into how alignment objectives are represented in reward models and why multi-objective alignment remains challenging, motivating future work on disentangled and controllable alignment methods.

</details>


### [150] [From Uncertain Judgments to Calibrated Rankings: Conformal Elo Estimation for LLM Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13221)
*Bora Kargi,David Salinas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种低成本评估大语言模型的方法，通过局部不确定性量化（将硬标签替换为校准的胜率概率输入Bradley-Terry模型）和全局不确定性量化（分裂共形预测）两个层级，将LLM-as-a-judge的Elo评分误差从人类标注水平降至17.9 MAE，并提供分布自由的有界预测区间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM-as-a-judge虽比人工标注成本低，但存在位置偏差、自我偏好和不可传递性等系统性误差，导致模型排名严重失准。需要一种低成本、校准良好的评估工具，无需大规模人工标注即可提供可靠的Elo估计和诚实的不确定性边界。

Method: 局部层面：将校准后的胜率概率（而非硬标签）输入Bradley-Terry模型，通过传播裁判自身的分数差异来估计每场对战的不确定性。全局层面：对LLM-derived与human-derived Elo评分的残差间隙应用分裂共形预测（split conformal prediction），生成具有分布自由边际覆盖保证的预测区间。

Result: 在LMArena的55个留出模型上，该方法将LLM-derived Elo与human-derived Elo的平均绝对误差降至17.9 Elo。局部层级单独即带来了Elo估计精度的显著提升。

Conclusion: 两层级方法结合，提供了一种低成本的评估工具，使开发者无需大规模人工标注即可获得校准的Elo估计和诚实的置信区间，具有良好的实用性和可复现性。

Abstract: Evaluating new large language models typically requires costly human annotation campaigns at scale. LLM-as-a-judge offers a cheaper alternative, but judge scores carry systematic errors - such as position bias, self-preference, or intransitivity - that can strongly miscalibrate the resulting rankings. We quantify the resulting judge-human disagreement at two complementary levels. At the local level, we estimate per-battle uncertainty from the judge's own score differences by propagating calibrated win probabilities rather than hard labels into the Bradley-Terry procedure. This alone provides a drastic improvement to Elo estimation accuracy, bringing LLM-derived ratings within 17.9 Elo MAE of human-derived ones when averaged over 55 held-out models on LMArena. At the global level, we apply split conformal prediction to the residual gap between LLM-derived and human-derived Elo ratings across held-out models, producing prediction intervals with distribution-free marginal coverage guarantees that account for irreducible LLM-human disagreement. Together, these two layers yield a low-cost evaluation tool that provides developers with calibrated Elo estimates and honest uncertainty bounds, without access to large-scale human annotations.To facilitate reproducibility, we release our code at https://github.com/kargibora/SoftElo .

</details>


### [151] [Distributional Loss for Robust Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13223)
*Kathleen Anderson,Thomas Martinetz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新的分类损失函数，通过将分类器输出建模为双模态高斯分布，替代传统的单标签硬映射，以隐式处理类别模糊性、缓解过拟合并学习更鲁棒的决策边界，在低数据场景下提升尤为显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统监督分类任务强制每个输入样本映射到单一标签，忽略了类别间的模糊性与不确定性，容易导致过拟合和决策边界不鲁棒。

Method: 定义分类器所有输出上的优化目标为双模态高斯分布，以软目标形式替代单标签硬映射，不依赖额外标签信息。

Result: 在鲁棒性上取得一致改进，尤其在低数据场景下增益显著，且只需对标准训练流程做最小修改。

Conclusion: 所提损失概念通过软目标分布隐式处理类别模糊性，有效缓解过拟合、增强决策边界鲁棒性，在数据稀缺时尤为有效。

Abstract: This paper proposes a novel loss concept for supervised classification tasks. Rather than enforcing a direct mapping from each input sample to a single assigned label, we define an optimization objective over all classifier outputs as a bimodal Gaussian distribution. This softer target formulation implicitly captures class ambiguity, mitigates overfitting, and encourages the learning of more robust decision boundaries, all without requiring additional label information. Experimental results demonstrate consistent improvements in robustness, with particularly pronounced gains in low-data regimes, while requiring only minimal modifications to standard training pipelines.

</details>


### [152] [ReSET: Accurate Latency-Critical NVFP4 Reasoning via Step-Aware Temperature Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13233)
*Sihwa Lee,Janghwan Lee,Donghoon Yoo,Jae Gon Kim,Hanyul Ryu,Soojung Ryu,Jungwook Choi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ReSET方法，通过基于推理步骤熵的温度缩放解决NVFP4量化导致的大推理模型精度下降问题，并设计CUDA小M核提高小批量自回归解码速度，在保持推理精度的同时实现最高2.5倍核加速和约2倍端到端解码加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大推理模型（LRMs）通过生成长中间推理链提升复杂问题求解能力，但推理成本显著增加。NVFP4推理通过硬件支持的低精度执行可降低计算和内存成本，但直接应用NVFP4会导致推理精度下降，且现有NVFP4内核在小批量自回归解码中无法充分发挥延迟优势。

Method: 1）分析NVFP4量化对推理过程中token级不确定性的影响，发现量化会增加低熵符号token的错误采样，并导致高不确定性推理步骤中token过度集中于小集合。2）提出ReSET——基于推理步骤熵的温度缩放方法，在线估计步骤级不确定性，并利用token级和步骤级熵信号自适应调整解码温度。3）设计CUDA核心的小M NVFP4内核，用于延迟关键的自回归解码。

Result: 在多个推理基准和模型规模上，ReSET相比NVFP4基线提升高达约2个百分点的推理准确率。CUDA小M核在延迟关键的解码中，相比NVFP4 vLLM实现最高2.5倍核级加速，相比BF16实现约2倍端到端解码加速。

Conclusion: ReSET结合熵感知温度缩放与高效低精度内核，有效解决了NVFP4量化下大推理模型的精度-效率权衡问题，为低精度推理在LRMs中的实用化提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Large reasoning models (LRMs) improve complex problem-solving by generating long intermediate reasoning traces, but this substantially increases inference costs. NVFP4 inference offers a promising approach to reduce both computational and memory costs through hardware-supported low-precision execution. However, directly applying NVFP4 to LRMs introduces two practical limitations: reasoning accuracy degrades under quantization, and existing NVFP4 kernels do not fully realize latency benefits in small-batch autoregressive decoding. In this work, we analyze the effect of NVFP4 quantization on token-level uncertainty during reasoning. We show that quantization increases incorrect sampling at low-entropy symbolic tokens, while causing over-concentration on a small set of tokens in high-uncertainty reasoning steps. Based on this observation, we propose \textbf{ReSET}, a reasoning-step entropy-based temperature-scaling method that estimates step-level uncertainty online and adapts the decoding temperature using both token-level and step-level entropy signals. To address the latency gap, we further design a CUDA-core small-$M$ NVFP4 kernel for latency-critical autoregressive decoding. Across reasoning benchmarks and model scales, ReSET improves NVFP4 reasoning accuracy by up to $\sim\!$2 points over the NVFP4 baseline. Our CUDA-core small-$M$ kernel further improves latency-critical decoding, delivering up to $2.5\!\times$ kernel-level speedup over NVFP4 vLLM and approximately $2\!\times$ end-to-end decoding speedup over BF16. Code is available at https://github.com/aiha-lab/ReSET.

</details>


### [153] [Decoding Insect Song: A Multitask Semisupervised Orthoptera Bioacoustic Classifier](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13236)
*Olga Isupova,Danil Kuzin,Ella Browning,Tom Mills,Steven Reece*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出PULSE框架，一种半监督多任务学习方法用于直翅目昆虫生物声学监测，结合弱监督分类、自监督学习和知识蒸馏，在分类性能和生态嵌入表示上显著超越通用模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 被动声学监测在生态推断中潜力巨大，但现有自动化工具通常训练范围狭窄且不可迁移，缺乏适用于特定类群（如直翅目昆虫）的高性能、可迁移的分析工具。

Method: 采用半监督多任务框架PULSE，包含三个组成部分：(1) 弱监督物种分类；(2) 无标记野外音频的自监督学习；(3) 通用生物声学模型的知识蒸馏。并引入主动学习策略进一步优化。

Result: 领域适配专家模型在所有指标上超越通用模型（macro F1: 0.21 vs 0.07; AUC: 0.74 vs 0.45; AP: 0.32 vs 0.19），主动学习将F1提升至0.34、AUC提升至0.84。

Conclusion: PULSE不仅实现了优越的分类性能，其学习的嵌入表示还编码了具有生态学意义的结构，可通过交互式可视化工具支持生态发现。

Abstract: Passive acoustic monitoring holds great promise for ecological inference, yet existing automated tools are typically narrowly trained and non-transferable. We address these limitations with PULSE, a semi-supervised, multi-task framework for Orthoptera bioacoustics, combining weakly-supervised species classification, self-supervised learning on unlabelled field audio, and knowledge distillation from a general-purpose bioacoustic model. Our domain-adapted specialist model outperforms a state-of-the-art general model across all metrics (macro F1: 0.21 vs. 0.07; AUC: 0.74 vs. 0.45; AP: 0.32 vs. 0.19), with active learning further raising F1 to 0.34 and AUC to 0.84. Beyond classification, the learned embeddings encode ecologically meaningful structure, exposed through an interactive visualisation tool for ecological discovery.

</details>


### [154] [Towards More General Control of Diffusion Models Using Jeffrey Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13240)
*Raphaël Razafindralambo,Rémy Sun,Frédéric Precioso,Jes Frellsen,Pierre-Alexandre Mattei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Jeffrey guidance框架，通过Jeffrey条件化规则扩展扩散模型的引导能力，能够处理标准引导无法表达的复杂目标分布，在CIFAR-10和FFHQ上显著降低FID，并在CelebA-HQ上实现属性间的公平性控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型虽具有灵活的输出控制能力（如条件采样），但许多情况下目标分布仅通过采样规则或启发式能量函数隐式定义，缺乏统一的理论框架来处理超出简单条件采样的复杂引导需求。

Method: 提出Jeffrey guidance框架，利用Jeffrey条件化规则更新边际分布以逼近预设目标，在保持条件结构的同时最小化对联合分布的扰动。

Result: 以Inception嵌入为目标的实验在CIFAR-10和FFHQ上显著降低FID；在CelebA-HQ上成功将无条件扩散模型更新为强制属性间独立性。

Conclusion: Jeffrey guidance为扩散模型提供了一种原则性的控制框架，可处理标准引导无法表达的复杂目标分布，在生成质量和公平性方面均有效。

Abstract: A key strength of diffusion models lies in their flexibility, since their outputs can be controlled at sampling time through guidance. However, beyond simple cases such as conditional sampling, the target distribution is often left implicit, defined only through a sampling rule or a heuristic energy function. To address this, we propose Jeffrey guidance, a principled framework that extends diffusion-model control to applications beyond what standard guidance can express. It leverages Jeffrey's rule of conditioning to update marginal distributions towards a prescribed target, preserving the conditional structure and minimally perturbing the joint distribution. We first demonstrate Jeffrey guidance by targeting a prescribed embedding distribution. With Inception embeddings as the target, this leads to substantial reductions in FID on both CIFAR-10 and FFHQ. We further apply Jeffrey guidance to fairness on CelebA-HQ, updating an unconditional diffusion model to enforce independence between attributes.

</details>


### [155] [To GAN or Not To GAN: Segmentation Analysis on Mars DEM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13252)
*Douglas Dziedzorm Agbeve,Aditya V. Handrale,Salim Fares,Seif E. Idani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文利用基于神经网络的语义分割方法（监督模型和生成对抗方法）自动检测火星表面的土丘，并将结果与人工标注进行对比，发现添加人工生成数据并未提升检测效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 火星表面的土丘检测有助于火星车导航，并可能提供寻找水或生命迹象的线索。传统方法依赖人工在数字高程模型上手动标注，效率低下，因此需要自动化检测方法。

Method: 采用监督式语义分割模型和生成对抗网络（GAN）两种方法进行火星土丘的自动检测与预测，并对两种方法进行比较。

Result: 添加额外人工生成的数据并未提高检测结果。

Conclusion: 基于神经网络的语义分割方法能够实现火星土丘的自动检测，但数据增强（生成对抗方法）在本任务中未能带来性能提升。

Abstract: To better understand Martian Surface, which is needed to enable Rovers navigate Mars with ease, it is necessary to be able to determine the location of mounds. Detecting and studying these morphologies can also help us find evidence of extraterrestrial life, in this case, more specifically, water or signs of life conducive environments. Detection of mounds was done by manually mapping morphological parameters onto Digital Elevation Models. This paper solves the problem by automatically detecting and or predicting mounds on Mars using Neural Network based Semantic Segmentation methodologies. This is done by using supervised semantic segmentation model and generative adversarial approach. A comparison of the approaches shows that adding extra artificially generated data did not improve the result.

</details>


### [156] [Extracting Governing Equations from Latent Dynamics via Multi-View Contrastive Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13260)
*Paolo Muratore,Mackenzie Weygandt Mathis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DYSCO是一种多视图时间对比学习算法，能够从带噪声的高维观测中联合恢复潜在轨迹和动力学方程，并在仿射规范下实现符号级方程恢复，理论上保证强可辨识性，实验上在混沌、振荡、亚稳态等动力学系统中表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从带噪声的高维观测数据中识别潜在动力系统是表示学习、系统辨识和科学发现的交叉核心问题，现有方法在噪声非线性观测下的可辨识性缺乏理论保证。

Method: 提出DYSCO算法，利用同一底层过程的多组独立带噪声观测视图，通过多视图时间对比学习联合恢复潜在轨迹和动力学；将动力学参数化在结构化函数基中，实现仿射规范下的符号级方程恢复。

Result: 理论上将强可辨识性结果扩展到噪声非线性观测场景；实验上在混沌、振荡、亚稳态等多种动力学系统中，高斯和泊松观测噪声下均准确恢复了潜在轨迹和流场。

Conclusion: DYSCO在理论和实验上均证明了从噪声高维观测中恢复潜在动力系统的有效性，尤其适用于神经记录等泊松噪声场景。

Abstract: Identifying latent dynamical systems from noisy, high-dimensional measurements is a central problem at the intersection of representation learning, system identification, and scientific discovery. We present DYSCO, a multi-view temporal contrastive learning algorithm that jointly recovers latent trajectories and the governing dynamics from such observations, by leveraging multiple independent noisy views of the same underlying process to disentangle signal from noise. By parameterizing the dynamics in a structured functional basis, our framework further enables symbolic recovery of the governing equations within an affine gauge. We offer theoretical guarantees for strong identification up to an affine indeterminacy, extending prior identifiability results to the realistic setting of noisy nonlinear observations. Empirically, we demonstrate accurate recovery of both latent trajectories and flow fields across a diverse set of dynamical regimes (e.g., chaotic, oscillatory, and metastable) under both Gaussian and Poisson observation noise, the latter being particularly relevant for neural recordings.

</details>


### [157] [Different Layers, Different Manifolds: Module-Wise Weight-Space Geometry in Transformer Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13276)
*Kirato Yoshihara*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究Transformer中不同模块（注意力层和MLP层）对黎曼流形几何约束的偏好差异，发现对注意力层施加Stiefel约束、对MLP层施加DGram约束效果最佳，而反向分配会导致训练不稳定，原因是DGram约束下注意力权重的奇异值增长会放大注意力logits并导致softmax饱和。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究Transformer中不同模块是否偏好不同的流形几何结构，质疑统一施加流形约束的常规做法。

Method: 在GPT-2预训练中采用Manifold Muon优化器，对比Stiefel和DGram两种流形约束在注意力层和MLP层的不同层间分配方案。

Result: 注意力层用Stiefel+MLP层用DGram的组合性能最佳；反向分配和全DGram配置在统一超参数下训练不稳定；DGram约束下注意力权重的奇异值增长会放大注意力logits并导致softmax饱和。

Conclusion: Transformer的对称感知和几何感知优化应当基于模块特异性而非统一策略。

Abstract: Weight-space geometry plays a central role in neural network optimization, yet manifold constraints are often applied uniformly across all weight matrices. In this work, we ask whether different transformer modules prefer different manifold geometries. We study Manifold Muon for GPT-2 pretraining and compare layer-wise assignments of Stiefel and DGram constraints across attention and MLP blocks. Our results show a clear asymmetry: constraining attention layers with Stiefel geometry while assigning DGram geometry to MLP layers gives the best performance among the tested configurations, whereas the inverted assignment and all-DGram configuration become unstable under the shared hyperparameter setting. We trace this failure to singular value growth in DGram-constrained attention weights, which can amplify attention logits and induce softmax saturation. These findings suggest that symmetry-aware and geometry-aware optimization for transformers should be module-specific rather than uniform.

</details>


### [158] [Once-for-All: Scalable Simultaneous Forecasting via Equilibrium State Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13285)
*Beinan Xu,Andy Song,Jiti Gao,Feng Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出均衡状态估计（ESE）范式，通过估计多系统间的均衡状态并基于当前状态与均衡状态的差异进行单次联合预测，在保持与SOTA方法相当精度的同时实现10-70倍加速，且具有线性时间复杂度、强泛化性和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有预测方法每次只能预测一个系统，无法高效处理多个交互系统（如经济学、医疗保健建模）同时需要协调预测的现实场景。

Method: ESE首先估计多个交互系统间的均衡状态，然后基于当前状态与估计均衡状态之间的差异，一次性生成所有系统的整体预测。

Result: 在合成数据集以及汇率、COVID-19扩散等真实数据集上，ESE精度至少与SOTA方法相当，同时速度显著更快，可实现10-70倍加速，并且具有线性时间复杂度，随系统数量增加扩展性远优于SOTA方法，在多种扰动下仍保持准确。

Conclusion: ESE是一种快速、可泛化、鲁棒且可扩展的多系统联合预测方法，能与传统预测器无缝集成，兼具其精度与自身的高效率。

Abstract: We introduce Equilibrium State Estimation (ESE), a novel paradigm for simultaneous prediction, where multiple interacting systems require separate yet coordinated forecasts. Such scenarios often arise in real-world settings such as economics and healthcare modeling. Unlike existing approaches that predict one system at a time, ESE forecasts all systems in a single pass. It first estimates the equilibrium state across systems, then generates holistic forecasts based on the difference between the current state and the estimated equilibrium. Extensive experiments on synthetic and real-world datasets, including currency exchange and COVID-19 spread modeling, demonstrate that ESE is at least as accurate as state-of-the-art (SOTA) methods while being significantly faster. In addition, ESE integrates seamlessly with conventional predictors, combining their accuracy with its exceptional efficiency and delivering a 10-70x speedup. With linear-time complexity, ESE scales far better than SOTA methods as the number of systems increases. Moreover, it remains accurate under diverse perturbations, establishing ESE as a fast, generalizable, robust, and scalable multi-prediction method.

</details>


### [159] [Clipping Makes Distributed and Federated Asynchronous SGD Robust to Stragglers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13287)
*Samuel Erickson,Mikael Johansson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从理论上证明，梯度裁剪（gradient clipping）在异步随机梯度下降（ASGD）中可以消除最大延迟对 oracle 复杂度的影响，并提出基于 sub-Weibull 梯度噪声模型的收敛性保证，包括期望收敛和首个异步优化中的高概率收敛结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异步训练中，慢速工作节点导致的较大更新延迟会负面影响 ASGD 的收敛性。同时，深度学习实践中发现梯度裁剪能"稳定"异步训练，但缺乏理论解释。本文旨在为这一现象提供理论依据。

Method: 采用 sub-Weibull 模型刻画梯度噪声（推广了 sub-Gaussian 和 sub-exponential 分布，适用于深度学习中更重尾的分布），在 ASGD 中加入梯度裁剪，从理论上分析裁剪如何去除最大延迟对 oracle 复杂度的影响，并证明期望收敛和高概率收敛。

Result: 梯度裁剪消除了 ASGD 收敛中最大延迟的依赖；给出了期望收敛保证，并首次在异步优化中证明了高概率收敛结果。

Conclusion: 梯度裁剪的理论合理性得到验证：它通过控制重尾梯度噪声的影响，使得 ASGD 对慢速节点导致的延迟不敏感，从而保障了异步训练的稳定性与收敛性。

Abstract: In modern machine learning, parallelization of training is an important strategy for increasing scale. Asynchronous stochastic gradient descent (ASGD), which maximizes the utilization of available hardware by avoiding waiting for slow workers. However, with constant step sizes, the convergence of ASGD is nonetheless affected negatively by slow workers due to large delays in updates. At the same time, it has been empirically observed in asynchronous training of deep learning models that gradient clipping "stabilizes" training. In this work, we provide a theoretical justification for this behavior, as we show that clipping removes the dependence of the maximum delay in the oracle complexity. We employ a sub-Weibull model of gradient noise which generalizes sub-Gaussian and sub-exponential distributions to more heavy-tailed distributions, motivated by empirical observations in deep learning. We show convergence in expectation, and the first time in asynchronous optimization, convergence with high probability.

</details>


### [160] [Quantizing Time-Series Models As Dynamical Systems: Trajectory-Based Quantization Sensitivity Score](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13300)
*Mariya Pavlova,Harrison Bo Hua Zhu,Elizsveta Semenova,Yingzhen Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于轨迹的量化敏感度评分（TQS），将后训练量化问题重构为离散时间动力系统稳定性分析，实现与量化过程解耦的先验敏感度估计，并构建无需校准数据的混合精度量化框架TQS-PTQ。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有后训练量化方法中，敏感度分析通常与量化过程耦合，导致无法在量化器选择和位宽分配之前预先评估各层的量化敏感度，限制了在黑盒网络或编译后网络上的量化预算规划能力。

Method: 将网络的前向传播建模为离散时间动力系统，定义轨迹量化敏感度评分（TQS）来刻画量化误差在滚动推理过程中的传播与放大特性。TQS与具体的量化器选择和位宽分配解耦，可实现先验敏感度估计。在此基础上构建TQS-PTQ混合精度量化框架，无需校准数据或二阶近似。

Result: 实验表明，从动力系统视角出发的量化方法在资源受限场景下能够实现鲁棒且高性能的低精度部署。

Conclusion: 动力系统稳定性理论为后训练量化提供了新的理论视角，TQS评分实现了敏感度分析与量化过程的解耦，TQS-PTQ框架在无需校准数据的条件下取得了良好的混合精度量化效果。

Abstract: We introduce the Trajectory-based Quantization Sensitivity Score (TQS), a metric that reframes post-training quantization (PTQ) through the lens of dynamical-systems stability. By modeling the network's rollout as a discrete-time dynamical system, TQS characterizes how quantization-induced errors propagate and amplify over the rollout horizon. Unlike conventional PTQ methods, where sensitivity analysis is often coupled to the quantization procedure, TQS enables a priori sensitivity estimation decoupled from quantizer selection and bit-width assignment. This separation allows for quantization budget planning even for black-box or compiled networks with fused operators. Building on this, we present TQS-PTQ, a flexible mixed-precision framework that requires no calibration data or costly second-order approximations. Our experiments show that a dynamical-systems perspective provides a robust, high-performing pathway for low-precision deployment in resource-constrained settings.

</details>


### [161] [Rarity-Gated Context Conditioning for Offline Imitation Learning-Based Maritime Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13311)
*Yongmin Kim,ByeongHoon Jeon,Sungil Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出RGFiLM模块，通过基于数据驱动稀有度评分的门控机制调节上下文调制强度，在不平衡上下文分布下减少异常检测的误报，在海上航迹异常检测中取得最佳F1-FPR权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 上下文异常检测在实际部署中常面临高度不平衡的上下文分布，稀有上下文区域可能导致条件模型产生不稳定决策和过多误报，现有方法未显式处理上下文稀有度问题。

Method: 提出RGFiLM模块，结合特征线性调制（上下文条件缩放和平移隐藏特征）与数据驱动的稀有度评分门控机制；稀有度评分从上下文变量的经验分布估计，调节上下文对中间表示的调制强度——稀有上下文下门控更果断，频繁上下文下更保守。

Result: 在AIS运动序列与ERA5环境背景的海上轨迹异常检测场景中，RGFiLM在顺序异常评分流水线中实现了最佳平均F1-误报率权衡，优于上下文无关和上下文条件方法。

Conclusion: 显式考虑上下文稀有度是减少上下文敏感异常检测中误报的有效方法。

Abstract: Contextual anomaly detection aims to identify abnormal behavior conditional on context variables, but practical deployments often face highly imbalanced context distributions where rare regimes can be critical information. Under such frequency bias, context-conditioned models can produce unstable decisions and excessive false alarms in rare contexts. We propose Rarity-Gated Feature-wise Linear Modulation (RGFiLM), a rarity-aware conditioning module that combines feature-wise modulation (i.e., context-conditioned scaling and shifting of hidden features) with a gate controlled by a data-driven rarity score. The rarity score is estimated from the empirical distribution of context variables and regulates how strongly context modulates intermediate representations: the gate becomes more decisive under rare contexts while remaining conservative under frequent contexts. We evaluate RGFiLM on maritime trajectory anomaly detection using AIS motion sequences with ERA5 environmental context in an environment-sensitive detour scenario. When instantiated in a sequential anomaly scoring pipeline, RGFiLM achieves the best mean F1--False Positive Rate (FPR) trade-off among the compared context-agnostic and context-conditioned methods. These results suggest that explicitly accounting for context rarity is an effective approach for reducing false alarms in context-sensitive anomaly detection.

</details>


### [162] [Navigating the Safety-Fidelity Trade-off: Massive-Variate Time Series Forecasting for Power Systems via Probabilistic Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13338)
*Kaijie Xu,Anqi Wang,Xilin Dai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PowerPhase基准（含6个电网、最高36,964通道，远超现有基准），以及PowerForge概率预测模型，揭示预测精度与约束满足之间的安全-保真度权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有概率预测基准无法同时评估大规模多变量系统（通道数受限）和物理约束满足（缺乏时序结构或概率评估），亟需一个能覆盖大规模电网、带物理约束感知度量的基准。

Method: 构建PowerPhase基准：基于6个输电网（2,000-36,964通道），每个目标轨迹由交流潮流求解产生；引入约束感知指标（Safety_mBrier, NECV, CVaR-α）补充CRPS和Distortion。提出PowerForge模型：基于场景的分位数预测器，含类型特定的解码头和变量组间的因果桥接。

Result: 在8个基线和3个种子下，分布精度与约束满足产生不同的模型排序（安全-保真度权衡）；PowerForge在所有网格上取得最佳平均排名。

Conclusion: 大规模电网概率预测中精度与约束安全不可兼得（需权衡），PowerForge通过类型感知解码和因果桥接有效平衡两者。

Abstract: Probabilistic forecasting models are increasingly deployed on multivariate systems with distinct channel physics and operational constraints, but existing benchmarks evaluate neither property at scale. Public canonical multivariate benchmarks cap out at 2,000 channels, while power-system benchmarks either lack temporal structure or probabilistic evaluation. We introduce PowerPhase, a probabilistic forecasting benchmark built on six transmission grids ranging from 2,000 to 36,964 jointly forecasted channels, more than an order of magnitude beyond popular canonical multivariate benchmarks. Each target trajectory is the output of an AC power-flow solve, and PowerPhase ships with constraint-aware metrics, including Safety_mBrier, NECV, and CVaR-alpha, that complement CRPS and Distortion. Across eight baselines and three seeds, distributional accuracy and constraint satisfaction rank models differently, a trade-off we term safety-fidelity. We further propose PowerForge, a scenario-based quantile forecaster with type-specific decoding heads and a causal bridge between variable groups, which achieves the best average rank on every grid.

</details>


### [163] [Enhanced Low-Density Region Exploration in Classifier-Guided Diffusion Models Through Modified Reverse Diffusion Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13347)
*Jagriti Singh,Shekhar Verma,Muneendra Ojha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种无需额外训练的采样时密度感知引导方法，通过修改分类器梯度引导扩散模型向低置信区域探索，并同时引导向预测真实图像靠近，从而改善长尾样本覆盖，在ImageNet上提升ADM模型召回率同时保持FID相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准分类器引导会集中概率质量在高密度类别均值附近，导致对类条件分布尾部稀有样本的覆盖不足。现有方法需额外训练低密度分类器或判别器，成本高。

Method: 基于预训练的条件扩散模型和分类器，在反向扩散过程中：(1) 通过修改的分类器梯度引导轨迹向低置信区域探索 (低密度引导)；(2) 同时引导采样过程向预测真实图像靠近 (真实图像引导)。两种引导均在噪声图像上应用梯度，而非预测噪声。

Result: 在64x64分辨率下，ADM模型召回率一致提升，FID保持可比；在256x256分辨率下，展示了不同引导组合的视觉结果，标准分类器引导结合预测真实图像引导可生成高感知质量样本。

Conclusion: 纯采样时的密度感知引导方法可有效改善长尾覆盖，无需额外训练成本，且能保持生成质量。

Abstract: Diffusion models have emerged as state-of-the-art generative models for high-fidelity image synthesis, particularly in their classifier-free guided and classifier-guided forms. However, standard classifier guidance concentrates probability mass around high-density class mean, leading to poor coverage of rare samples in the tails of the class-conditional distributions. Recent work on diffusion-based tail sampling mitigates this by training an additional low-density-seeking classifier with a synthetic-vs-real discriminator, at the cost of additional networks and training. In parallel, a number of samplers and distillation techniques accelerate or refine diffusion sampling, but do not explicitly address long-tail coverage. We propose a purely sampling-time, density-aware extension of classifier-guided conditional diffusion model that targets low-density regions without any additional training. We have applied guidance at noisy images not on predicted noise like most diffusion models. Starting from a pretrained conditional diffusion model and classifier on ImageNet, we modify the guided reverse dynamics by steering trajectories toward low-confidence regions via the modified classifier gradient, and at each time step, we also guide the sampling process toward the predicted real image. 1st guidance helps explore low-probability samples, and 2nd guidance helps to generate samples to be close to the real data manifold. The proposed sampler consistently improves ADM model recall at 64x64 resolution while maintaining a comparable FID, and with a 256x256 ADM model, we showed the results visually with different combinations of both guidance. We also showed that standard ADM classifier guidance, combined with predicted real image guidance, helps generate high perceptual quality samples with a 256x256 ADM model on ImageNet.

</details>


### [164] [VideoMDM: Towards 3D Human Motion Generation From 2D Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13364)
*Amir Mann,Gal Michael Harari,Merav Keidar,Or Litany*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: VideoMDM是一种无需3D真值、仅从单目视频2D姿态训练3D人体运动先验的扩散框架，通过深度加权2D重投影损失替代3D监督，在HumanML3D上逼近全监督MDM性能（FID 0.88 vs 0.54），在真实视频数据集上生成运动优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D人体运动生成模型依赖大量3D标注数据，获取成本高；而单目视频数据丰富但缺乏3D真值。作者希望仅利用单目视频中的2D姿态信息，学习高质量的3D运动先验，降低对3D标注的依赖。

Method: 1）使用预训练2D-to-3D提升器从2D姿态生成近似3D序列作为带噪教师；2）对这些3D序列进行扩散加噪，模型在3D空间去噪后重投影回2D，与准确2D关键点计算深度加权重投影损失；3）理论上证明在温和假设下，该损失期望等价于直接3D监督；4）将速度一致性和过参数化表示对齐等3D正则项适配到2D设置。

Result: HumanML3D上FID为0.88（全监督MDM为0.54），接近全监督性能；在Fit3D和NBA真实视频数据集上生成的运动获得人类偏好，定量结果强。

Conclusion: VideoMDM证明了仅从单目视频2D姿态即可有效学习3D运动先验，通过精心设计的2D监督损失与正则化策略，弥合了2D训练与3D生成之间的差距，为缺乏3D标注的真实场景提供了可行方案。

Abstract: We introduce VideoMDM, a diffusion-based framework that trains 3D human motion priors directly from accurate 2D poses extracted from monocular videos, without any 3D ground truth. A pretrained 2D-to-3D lifter provides approximate 3D pose sequences that serve as a noisy teacher: these are diffused, denoised by the model in 3D, and supervised in 2D by reprojecting the prediction and comparing against accurate keypoints. We show that, under mild assumptions, a depth-weighted 2D reprojection loss is equivalent in expectation to direct 3D supervision, and we adapt standard 3D motion regularizers - velocity consistency and over-parameterized representation alignment - to this 2D setting. Unlike methods that lift 2D to 3D only at inference, VideoMDM learns a coherent 3D motion manifold during training. On HumanML3D it nearly closes the gap to fully 3D-supervised MDM (FID 0.88 vs 0.54); On real video datasets Fit3D and NBA the method learns to generate motions consistently preferred by humans, with strong quantitative results.

</details>


### [165] [Hölder++: Improving the Quality-Coherence Trade-off in Multimodal VAEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13381)
*Huyen Vo,María Martínez-García,Isabel Valera*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Hölder++，一种新的多模态变分自编码器，通过精确的Hölder池化、分离共享与私有表征的分层架构，有效改善了生成质量与模态间语义一致性的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MMVAE在生成质量与模态间语义一致性之间存在权衡，难以同时生成真实多样且跨模态语义一致的样本。近期使用近似Hölder池化的方法虽提升了一致性但牺牲了样本多样性，亟需更好的解决方案。

Method: 提出Hölder++，包含三方面贡献：(i) 首次在多模态VAE中实现无近似的精确Hölder池化；(ii) 扩展架构建模分离的共享表征与私有（模态特有）表征（Hölder+）；(iii) 引入分层推理进一步增强共享与私有表征的解耦（Hölder++）。

Result: 实验表明，Hölder++持续改善生成质量与一致性的权衡，产生更结构化的潜在空间，且学到的共享表征对下游任务具有信息性。

Conclusion: Hölder++通过精确Hölder池化与共享-私有表征解耦，有效克服了多模态VAE中生成质量与一致性的矛盾，在多个维度上优于现有方法。

Abstract: Existing approaches for multimodal variational autoencoders (VAEs) face a trade-off between generative quality and coherence-i.e., they struggle to generate realistic and diverse samples that, at the same time, are semantically consistent across modalities. A recent work shows that using a simple approximation to Hölder pooling as an aggregation method improves coherence over the SOTA MMVAE+, despite assuming a single shared representation across all modalities. Yet, it slightly compromises sample diversity. Inspired by this insight, we propose Hölder++, a novel multimodal VAE that improves the generative quality-coherence trade-off through: (i) the first implementation of Hölder pooling without any approximation for multimodal VAEs; (ii) an extended architecture that models distinct shared and private (i.e., modality-specific) representations (Hölder+); and (iii) hierarchical inference that further enhances the disentanglement between the shared and private representations (Hölder++). Our experiments corroborate that Hölder++ consistently improves the generative quality-coherence trade-off, yields more structured latent spaces, and learns shared representations that are informative for downstream tasks.

</details>


### [166] [Accelerating Speculative Diffusions via Block Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13426)
*Alexander Soen,Hisham Husain,Valentin De Bortoli,Arnaud Doucet*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种适用于扩散模型的原创投机采样机制，能够高效实现连续空间中的残差分布采样，并引入块验证方法（源自LLM）以提升草稿接受率。同时形式化了无需训练的Free Drafter启发式自投机方法，在无额外训练和极小开销下实现高达6.3%的加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将投机解码（speculative decoding）从离散的LLM领域扩展到连续的扩散模型面临核心挑战：连续空间中残差分布的高效采样难以实现，现有方法要么计算效率低，要么依赖替代方案。

Method: 1) 提出一种新的高效实现连续空间投机采样的机制；2) 将LLM中的块验证（block verification）适配到扩散模型，提升接受率；3) 形式化并分析无需训练的Free Drafter自投机框架。

Result: 在无额外训练且开销可忽略的前提下，Free Drafter相比现有投机方法获得高达6.3%的加速。

Conclusion: 所提方案成功将原始投机采样机制适配到扩散模型，并借助块验证显著提升效率；Free Drafter在无训练场景下具有实用价值。

Abstract: Speculative decoding speeds up LLM inference by using a draft model to generate tokens, with an acceptance-rejection scheme that ensures that the output matches the target distribution. Adapting this to continuous diffusions is difficult because speculative sampling requires drawing from a residual distribution. While straightforward in discrete spaces, efficiently sampling this residual in continuous space is non-trivial. Consequently, existing diffusion adaptations either use computationally inefficient sampling techniques or rely on an alternative scheme. In this work, we introduce a novel scheme that efficiently implements the original speculative sampling mechanism for diffusion models. Our approach offers a critical advantage over current methods: it enables us to adapt block verification from LLMs to diffusions -- which provably improves the acceptance rate of drafts. Furthermore, we formalize and analyze the Free Drafter, a heuristic self-speculative drafter for diffusions that requires no training. By enabling block verification, our Free Drafter yields up to a 6.3% speedup over existing speculative methods with no additional training and negligible overhead beyond the existing parallel verification pass.

</details>


### [167] [How Much Memory Do We Need? Adaptive Memory Gate for Neural Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13443)
*Jihyeon Hur,Yongseok Kwon,Min-Gi Jo,Jeongwhan Choi,Noseong Park*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出AMGFNO，通过可学习门控机制动态调整记忆权重，解决现有记忆增强神经算子在不同观测条件（分辨率、物理参数）下记忆权重固定、适应性不足的问题。在Kuramoto-Sivashinsky和Burgers方程上，低分辨率下nRMSE降低55-79%，门控值随分辨率升高从约0.7自动降至接近0。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有记忆增强神经算子使用固定的记忆权重，无法在不同观测条件（如分辨率、物理参数）下自适应调整，导致性能受限。实验表明最优记忆权重随分辨率和粘度变化，固定权重无法同时优化多种设置下的表现。

Method: 提出AMGFNO（Adaptive Memory Gated FNO），通过可学习门控机制动态调节记忆权重，使模型能够根据输入条件自适应地决定对历史状态的信赖程度。

Result: 在Kuramoto-Sivashinsky和Burgers方程上，低分辨率下nRMSE降低55-79%；门控值从低分辨率时约0.7自动下降至高分辨率时接近0，说明模型自适应地选择了较短的记忆依赖。

Conclusion: 动态门控记忆权重能有效提升神经算子在多分辨率、多物理参数条件下的泛化能力和预测精度，验证了记忆权重自适应调节的必要性和有效性。

Abstract: Neural operators have emerged as a powerful data-driven approach for solving time-dependent PDEs. Among recent advances, memory-augmented neural operators explicitly incorporate past states and have achieved remarkable performance under low-resolution observation settings. However, existing approaches apply a fixed memory weight regardless of observation conditions, such as resolution or physical parameters, limiting their adaptability. Our preliminary experiments reveal that optimal memory weight varies with resolution and viscosity, implying that a fixed memory weight cannot simultaneously optimize performance across diverse settings. We propose AMGFNO, which dynamically modulates memory weight through a learnable gate. On the Kuramoto-Sivashinsky and Burgers' equations, AMGFNO achieves 55-79% nRMSE reduction over at low resolution, with the learned gate value automatically decreasing from $\bar{g} \approx 0.7$ to near-zero as resolution increases.

</details>


### [168] [Clustering Node Attributed Networks with Graph Neural Networks and Self Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13444)
*Rodrigo de Sapienza Luna,Daniel Ratton Figueiredo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于图神经网络（GNN）的无监督图聚类框架，通过多轮自学习方式交替进行节点表示学习和聚类，并利用每轮构建的上下文图来增强表示，在合成数据和真实数据集上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的图聚类仅利用网络边信息，而真实数据中节点属性也包含丰富信息。现有方法未能充分协同利用网络结构和节点属性两种异质信息，亟需一种能够同时整合两者的无监督聚类方法。

Method: 提出多轮自学习框架：每轮使用GNN生成节点表示用于聚类，聚类结果影响下一轮用于生成表示的图结构；同时每轮利用原始图构建上下文图来辅助节点表示生成。整个过程完全无监督。

Result: 在合成数据上，该方法在网络和属性各自信息量不足时优于仅用网络或仅用属性的算法；多轮学习始终优于单轮长训练；在真实数据上，当簇大小平衡时达到与最先进方法竞争的性能。

Conclusion: 该框架有效融合了图结构和节点属性信息，通过多轮自学习机制提升了聚类性能，但在簇不平衡的真实场景中仍有改进空间。

Abstract: Graph clustering - partitioning the node set of a graph into disjoint subsets that reflect some latent information - is a fundamental problem as it finds applications in a myriad of different scenarios. While this classic problem has been tackled for decades by different communities, a recent variation of the problem driven by real data considers the scenario where nodes have attributes that are also informative. This has triggered novel methods that simultaneously leverage network information (edges) and node information (attributed) in the design of novel clustering algorithms. This work proposes a novel framework that builds on prior works that have applied graph neural networks (GNN) to graph clustering. The proposed framework operates in rounds of self learning in a fully unsupervised setting. In each round, a GNN generates representations for nodes that are used to cluster the nodes. This clustering influences the graph used to generate the node representation in the next round. Moreover, a context graph built in each round using the original graph is used to generate the node representations. Empirical results show that the proposed methodology extracts information from both network edges and node attributes in synthetic data, outperforming algorithms focused solely on the network or attributes when neither are very informative. Multiple rounds of learning also improve the performance and always outperforms a long single round of training (i.e., classic GNN graph clustering). When considering real datasets, empirical results indicate that the proposed methodology is competitive to state-of-the-art methods when cluster sizes are balanced.

</details>


### [169] [Uncertainty Estimation for Molecular Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13451)
*Paul Seij,Christian A. Naesseth,Stephan Mandt,Metod Jazbec*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于Laplace近似的后验不确定性估计方法，用于预训练分子扩散模型的样本质量评估，并通过测试时过滤提升生成性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型在3D分子生成中广泛使用，但缺乏判断生成分子质量高低的原则性信号，无法有效识别低质量样本。

Method: 在预训练的分子扩散模型上，对去噪网络进行Laplace近似，沿生成轨迹测量噪声预测的变异性，从而估计每个样本的不确定性分数。

Result: 不确定性分数与样本质量呈负相关，能够有效指示样本质量；通过过滤低质量样本可在测试时提升模型整体性能。

Conclusion: 所提出的后验不确定性估计方法为分子扩散模型提供了实用的质量筛选机制，实现了无需重新训练即可改善生成效果的测试时缩放策略。

Abstract: Diffusion models have seen wide adoption for 3D molecular generation, yet they offer no principled signal of when a generated molecule is likely to be of low quality. We propose a post-hoc method for estimating per-sample uncertainty in pretrained molecular diffusion models. Building on a Laplace approximation of the denoising network, we measure the variability of the noise prediction across the generation trajectory. Empirically, we show that the resulting uncertainty score is informative of sample quality, exhibiting a negative correlation with established sample-level quality metrics. We further study how the proposed uncertainty score can be used to filter generated samples, improving model performance via test-time scaling.

</details>


### [170] [Reinforcement Learning for Neural Model Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13461)
*Shaivi Malik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将神经网络模型编辑形式化为强化学习问题的探索性框架，通过两种环境（MaskWorld和ShiftWorld）让智能体利用奖励反馈学习编辑策略，并在偏见缓解和机器遗忘任务上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的神经网络编辑需要针对特定目标设计专门的算法，耗时且需要大量人工努力。本文旨在提供一个通用框架，避免为每个任务手动设计编辑算法。

Method: 将模型编辑建模为强化学习问题，构建两个环境：MaskWorld（乘法缩放权重）和ShiftWorld（加法更新权重）。奖励函数结合了效用保持目标和任务特定编辑目标，使智能体在保持整体性能的同时学习针对性修改。

Result: 在机器遗忘任务中，遗忘集准确率降至接近0%，保留集准确率保持90%以上；在偏见缓解任务中，偏见相关性能提升超过5%，同时保持分类效用。

Conclusion: 神经网络模型编辑可以被建模为强化学习问题，使编辑策略能够从奖励反馈中学习，而非为每个任务手动设计。

Abstract: Editing pretrained neural networks requires specialized algorithms tailored to specific objectives. Designing such algorithms is often time-consuming and demands significant effort. We present an exploratory framework that formulates neural model editing as a reinforcement learning problem, where agents modify models using reward feedback. We introduce two environments: MaskWorld, where agents scale weights multiplicatively, and ShiftWorld, where agents apply additive weight updates. The reward function combines a utility-preservation objective with a task-specific editing objective, enabling agents to learn targeted modifications while maintaining overall model performance. We evaluate the framework on bias mitigation in text classification and machine unlearning in image classification, both of which traditionally rely on specialized algorithms. Our results show that the learned policies reduce forget set accuracy to nearly 0% while preserving over 90% retain set accuracy on the unlearning task. In the bias mitigation setting, the learned policies improve bias-related performance by more than 5% while maintaining general classification utility. Our findings show that neural model editing can be cast as a reinforcement learning problem, allowing editing policies to be learned from reward feedback rather than manually engineered for each task.

</details>


### [171] [MaxProof: Scaling Mathematical Proof with Generative-Verifier RL and Population-Level Test-Time Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13473)
*Jiacheng Chen,Xinyu Zhang,Shunkai Zhang,Yanmohan Wang,Lin Li,Tiancheng Qin,Qin Wang,Zhengmao Zhu,Tianle Li,Jingyang Li,Zehan Li,Binyang Jiang,Jin Zhu,Han Ding,Fei Yu,Chenyu Du,Zijian Song,Jiayuan Song,Zhi Zhang,Yunan Huang,Weiyu Cheng,Pengyu Zhao,Yu Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MaxProof 是一种面向竞赛级数学证明的群体级测试时扩展框架，通过将 M3 模型作为生成器、验证器、精炼器和排序器，对候选证明群体进行搜索并经由锦标赛选择输出最终证明。在 IMO 2025 和 USAMO 2026 上分别达到 35/42 和 36/42，超过人类金牌线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对竞赛级数学证明任务，现有模型在单次生成时难以保证证明的正确性和完整性，本文旨在通过测试时扩展（test-time scaling）提升证明质量，突破单人单次生成的性能瓶颈。

Method: 1) 训练三种面向证明的能力：证明生成、证明验证、基于批评的证明修复，采用低假阳率的纵深防御生成式验证器；2) 将三种能力合并到单一 M3 模型中；3) 测试时将 M3 模型同时作为生成器、验证器、精炼器和排序器，在候选证明群体上进行搜索，通过锦标赛选择输出最终证明。

Result: M3 模型配合 MaxProof 测试时扩展，在 IMO 2025 上取得 35/42，在 USAMO 2026 上取得 36/42，两项均超过人类金牌线。

Conclusion: MaxProof 通过群体级搜索和锦标赛选择有效提升了证明的正确率，验证了测试时扩展在数学证明领域的巨大潜力，实现了超人类金牌水平的证明能力。

Abstract: We present MaxProof, a population-level test-time scaling framework for competition-level mathematical proof in the MiniMax-M3 series. M3 first trains three proof-oriented capabilities -- proof generation, proof verification, and critique-conditioned proof repair -- using a defense-in-depth generative verifier engineered for low false-positive rate. These capabilities are merged into a single released M3 model. At test time, MaxProof treats the model as a generator, verifier, refiner, and ranker, searches over a population of candidate proofs, and returns one final proof through tournament selection. With MaxProof test-time scaling, the M3 model reaches 35/42 on IMO 2025 and 36/42 on USAMO 2026, exceeding the human gold-medal threshold on both.

</details>


### [172] [SupraBench: A Benchmark for Supramolecular Chemistry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13477)
*Tianyi Ma,Yijun Ma,Zehong Wang,Weixiang Sun,Ziming Li,Connor R. Schmidt,Chuxu Zhang,Matthew J. Webber,Yanfang Ye*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个超分子化学基准测试SupraBench，包含四个核心任务（结合亲和力预测、最佳结合物选择、溶剂识别、主客体描述）和一个辅助视觉任务，并发布了16M token的超分子化学语料库SupraPMC。实验表明，当前LLMs在所有任务上表现不佳，领域适应预训练对分布内回归有效但与严格格式输出存在权衡，不同任务族展现了不同的失败模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 设计主客体系统耗时耗力，每个候选对需要数天湿实验验证。虽然LLMs在分子结合任务上表现良好，但缺乏系统评估LLMs在超分子化学推理能力的基准。

Method: 与领域专家合作发布SupraBench基准，设计四个核心任务（结合亲和力预测、最佳结合物选择、溶剂识别、主客体描述）和一个视觉辅助任务。同时从Europe PMC构建16M token的SupraPMC语料库用于领域适应预训练。

Result: LLMs在所有任务上存在显著提升空间；领域适应预训练在分布内回归任务上表现良好，但与严格格式输出存在权衡；不同任务族的难度分布差异显著，揭示了当前超分子化学推理中的特定缺陷。

Conclusion: SupraBench为超分子化学LLM评估建立了标准基准，当前模型仍有较大改进空间，不同任务族的失败模式为未来研究方向提供了指引。

Abstract: Supramolecular chemistry, which includes the study of non-covalent host-guest assemblies, has advanced various applications. However, designing host-guest systems remains time-consuming, requiring days of dry-lab verification per candidate pair. Although LLMs have emerged as a fast alternative with strong performance on molecular binding tasks, no benchmark currently systematically evaluates LLMs for host-guest reasoning across fundamental supramolecular chemistry tasks, e.g., binding affinity prediction. To this end, we collaborate with domain experts to release the first Supramolecular Benchmark, called SupraBench, to evaluate LLMs in chemistry reasoning. Specifically, we design four fundamental tasks, i.e., binding affinity prediction, top-binder selection, solvent identification, and host-guest description, plus an auxiliary vision-based task for molecular identification. We also release SupraPMC, a curated 16M-token corpus of Supramolecular chemistry articles distilled from Europe PMC, to support the adaptation to the supramolecular domain. We benchmark a broad range of open and proprietary LLMs and find that LLMs leave substantial headroom across all tasks. Domain adaptation pretraining over SupraPMC transfers cleanly to in-distribution regression but trades off against strict letter-format output. Moreover, the difficulty profile differs sharply across task families, revealing distinct failure modes that indicate specific gaps in current supramolecular chemistry reasoning. Our source codes and benchmark datasets are available at https://github.com/Tianyi-Billy-Ma/SupraBench.

</details>


### [173] [CRAFTIIF: Cross-Resolution Analytic Four-Type Interpretable Isolation Forest for Multivariate Time Series Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13486)
*William Smits*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CRAFTIIF是一个完全无监督的多元时间序列异常检测框架，通过生成500个跨四个小波族（Morlet、DOG、Haar、Coiflet）的随机解析小波特征，并构建五个结构化孤立森林（每种异常类型一个加一个元IF），同时检测点异常、分布异常、时序异常和集体异常四种类型。在mTSBench基准的19个数据集上，VUS-PR达0.463，较此前最优提升40.7%，并提供了内建的可解释性归因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无监督方法大多仅针对一两种异常类型，无法同时处理点异常、分布异常、时序异常和集体异常这四种结构不同的异常类型，且缺乏可解释性。

Method: CRAFTIIF生成K=500个跨四个小波族（Morlet、DOG、Haar、Coiflet）的随机解析小波特征，每个特征针对特定异常类型；构建五个结构化孤立森林（每种异常类型一个元IF）；使用自适应Otsu/MAD阈值自动校准检测；通过分支激活直接提供异常类型归因。

Result: 在mTSBench全部19个数据集上，平均F1=0.228（全部），F1=0.322（13个可检测数据集）；VUS-PR达0.463，较此前最优0.329提升40.7%。消融实验验证了自适应阈值（+38% F1）、四分支结构（+20%）和元IF（+23%）各自不可或缺。

Conclusion: CRAFTIIF是首个能同时处理四种异常类型的无监督框架，在基准测试中达到SOTA性能，并提供内建可解释性。诊断框架识别出19个数据集中有6个对任何无监督方法都基本不可检测。

Abstract: Anomaly detection in multivariate time series is challenged by four structurally distinct anomaly types -- point (isolated spikes), distributional (level shifts), temporal (rhythm changes), and collective (inter-sensor correlation breakdowns) -- each requiring different feature representations. Most unsupervised methods target only one or two types and provide limited interpretability. We present CRAFTIIF (Cross-Resolution Analytic Four-Type Interpretable Isolation Forest), a fully unsupervised framework targeting all four types without dataset-specific tuning. CRAFTIIF generates K=500 random analytic wavelet feature draws across four families (Morlet, DOG, Haar, Coiflet), each targeting a specific anomaly type, feeding five structured Isolation Forests -- one per type plus a meta-IF for compound anomalies. An adaptive Otsu/MAD threshold calibrates detection automatically across anomaly rates from 0.1% to 69.2%. Because each IF is trained exclusively on type-specific features, branch firing provides direct anomaly-type attribution by construction, without post-hoc explanation. Evaluated on all 19 datasets of the mTSBench benchmark (Zhou et al., TMLR 2026), CRAFTIIF achieves mean F1=0.228 (all 19 datasets) and F1=0.322 (13 detectable datasets), ranking first among all 25 evaluated methods on VUS-PR (0.463 vs. previous best 0.329, +40.7%). A diagnostic framework -- oracle F1, detectability limits, and branch separation ratios -- identifies 6 of 19 datasets as fundamentally undetectable by any unsupervised method. Ablation over 11 conditions confirms adaptive thresholding (+38% F1), four-branch structure (+20%), and meta-IF (+23%) are each essential. Code: https://github.com/smitswil/craftiif

</details>


### [174] [A2D2: Fine-Tuning Any-Length Discrete Diffusion for Adaptive Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13565)
*Sophia Tang,Yuchen Zhu,Molei Tao,Pranam Chatterjee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出A2D2框架，通过联合优化插入和去掩码策略以及基于质量的推理调度，实现了对任意长度离散扩散模型的奖励引导微调，并理论保证了收敛到奖励倾斜序列分布，实验表明该方法在奖励优化、生成灵活性和准确性上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散扩散模型在序列生成中表现出色，已通过token插入扩展到任意长度设置，但针对任意长度离散扩散模型的原则性奖励引导微调方法仍未被探索。

Method: 提出A2D2框架，联合优化插入策略和去掩码策略，结合基于质量的推理调度；推导了联合插入-去掩码路径测度的Radon-Nikodym导数，理论保证收敛到奖励倾斜分布；建立去掩码质量和插入质量的损失函数，提出自适应联合解码（AJD）损失以最小化解码误差。

Result: A2D2在奖励优化方面取得改进，同时增强了生成灵活性和准确性，优于先前的固定长度微调和推理时引导方法。

Conclusion: A2D2为任意长度离散扩散模型提供了一种统一的奖励引导微调框架，兼具理论保证和实证优势。

Abstract: Discrete diffusion models offer a simple and stable likelihood-based framework for sequence generation, recently extended to any-length settings via token insertion. Principled reward-guided fine-tuning for any-length discrete diffusion, however, remains largely unexplored. We introduce Fine-Tuning Any-Length Discrete Diffusion for Adaptive Decoding (A2D2), a unified framework for reward-guided fine-tuning of any-length discrete diffusion models via joint optimization of the insertion and unmasking policies together with a quality-based inference schedule. We derive the Radon-Nikodym derivative for the joint insertion-unmasking path measures, enabling theoretically guaranteed convergence to the intractable reward-tilted sequence distribution without requiring target samples. Building on this, we establish unmasking and insertion quality as tractable approaches for minimizing decoding error and introduce the Adaptive Joint Decoding (AJD) loss, which provably yields the optimal path measure that generates the reward-tilted distribution. Empirically, A2D2 improves reward optimization while enhancing generation flexibility and accuracy over prior fixed-length fine-tuning and inference-time guidance methods.

</details>


### [175] [Adjusted Cup-Product Neural Layer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13568)
*Snigdha Chandan Khilar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种调整杯积神经层（adjusted cup product neural layer），该层通过硬编码杯积与高阶规范理论中的调整项，设计出天生具备规范不变性的读出机制。理论证明在闭链上输出完全依赖于调整系数，且该系数是唯一规范不变信号的来源，该可观测量为非零二次型，在单次和二次规范变换下严格不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理和几何中许多重要可观测量的计算涉及上同调环的杯积运算，传统神经网络层缺乏对这种拓扑结构的显式编码，且难以保证规范不变性。本文旨在设计一种能硬编码杯积结构并内置规范不变性的神经层。

Method: 将杯积运算与高阶规范理论中的调整项结合，构建调整杯积神经层作为神经基元，使读出机制天然具备规范不变性。

Result: 理论证明在闭链上输出完全由调整系数决定，调整系数为零时输出恒为零；该可观测量是非零二次型，且在单次和二次规范变换下严格不变。

Conclusion: 调整项是规范不变信号的唯一来源，该层设计为实现规范不变神经网络的拓扑结构提供了新路径。

Abstract: Many important observables in physics and geometry are cup products of cochains. The adjusted cup product neural layer has been introduced in this paper. It is a neural primitive that hard wires the cup product with an adjustment term from higher gauge theory. This creates a readout that is gauge invariant by design. Their main theoretical result shows that on a closed cycle the output relies entirely on the adjustment coefficient. Setting this coefficient to zero removes the output completely regardless of other parameters. Thus the adjustment is the only source of gauge invariant signal. They prove this observable is a nonzero quadratic form and is exactly invariant under one and two gauge transformations.

</details>


### [176] [Existence Precedes Value: Joint Modeling of Observational Existence and Evolving States in Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13571)
*Yifan Hu,Hongzhou Chen,Peiyuan Liu,Yiding Liu,Zewei Dong,Jiang-Ming Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Timeflies提出统一框架，将时序预测重构为未来可观测性推断与值估计的联合问题，通过双流架构（观测流和值流）及三个专用模块显式建模观测动态与状态演化的交互，并在新基准Shadow上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法（如Neural ODEs、连续时间图网络）仍依赖推理时未来有效观测时间戳已知的隐式假设，这在许多实际系统中不成立——核心问题不仅是未来值是多少，还包括未来是否会有有效观测。需要联合建模未来可观测性和值估计。

Method: 提出Timeflies框架：1) 双流架构（观测流+值流）；2) 三个耦合模块：可靠性感知嵌入、观测引导的依赖建模、联合预测；3) 构建Shadow基准（公共数据集自然缺失+工业数据）；4) 提出OVJE指标综合评估耦合可预测性。

Result: 大量实验表明Timeflies持续优于现有方法，证明显式建模未来可观测性对含缺失值时序预测的重要性。

Conclusion: 未来可观测性推断与值估计的联合建模是提升含缺失值时序预测效果的关键方向。

Abstract: Real-world time series are often highly incomplete and irregular due to sensor dormancy, transmission delays, and event-driven sampling, making reliable forecasting fundamentally challenging. Existing methods have evolved from impute-then-forecast pipelines to continuous-time models such as Neural ODEs and continuous-time graph networks. While these approaches improve the modeling of historical irregularity, they still rely on an implicit oracle assumption at inference time: the timestamps of future valid observations are presumed to be known in advance. This assumption limits practical relevance, since in many real systems the more fundamental question is not only what the future value will be, but also whether a valid observation will occur at all. In this paper, we propose Timeflies, a unified framework that reformulates forecasting as a joint problem of future observability inference and value estimation. To explicitly model the interaction between observation dynamics and state evolution, Timeflies adopts an observation stream and a value stream, coupled through three dedicated modules for reliability-aware embedding, observation-guided dependency modeling, and joint prediction. We further construct Shadow, a benchmark that combines natural missingness from public datasets with real-world industrial data, and introduce the Observation-Value Joint Entropy (OVJE) metric to comprehensively evaluate this coupled predictability. Extensive experiments show that Timeflies consistently outperforms existing methods, highlighting the importance of explicitly modeling future observability in time series forecasting with missing values. Code and dataset are available in https://github.com/ant-intl/Timeflies.

</details>


### [177] [Learning with Simulators: No Regret in a Computationally Bounded World](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13576)
*Sasha Voitovych,Abhishek Shetty,Noah Golowich,Alexander Rakhlin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出“可模拟过程”框架：当学习器能访问近似数据生成过程的模拟器时（即使数据高度依赖），仍可恢复与独立数据场景相同的VC维泛化保证，并展示了条件采样的优势，最终给出一个能同时学习所有多项式时间可采样过程下任意VC类的统一算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典学习理论多数结果依赖于数据独立性假设，对强依赖过程的泛化研究极为有限。填补这一理论空白，探索在弱假设下（仅需可模拟性）能否恢复标准学习保证。

Method: 引入可模拟过程框架，假设学习器可访问一个近似真实数据分布（可任意复杂和依赖）的模拟器；利用条件采样技术，证明在此设置下可恢复VC维相关的误差界；进一步将算法与Kolmogorov复杂度相联系，提出在多项式时间有界采样过程中统一学习的算法。

Result: 在可模拟过程下，学习器可获得与独立同分布数据相同的泛化保证（误差界由VC维控制）；条件采样带来统计与计算上的严格优势；单个算法可在所有多项式时间有界可采样过程中同时学习任意VC类，遗憾界由过程的时间有界Kolmogorov复杂度控制。

Conclusion: 该工作显著拓宽了经典PAC学习模型，表明数据独立性并非泛化的必要前提，可模拟性是一种更弱的充分条件，为强依赖数据的理论研究开辟了新方向。

Abstract: Understanding the minimal assumptions necessary for generalization is the fundamental question in learning theory. Unfortunately, most results rely heavily on independence (or some proxy thereof) of the data-generating process, while results for strongly dependent data are far more limited. Towards addressing this gap, we introduce the framework of simulatable processes, where the learner has access to a simulator that approximates the distribution generating the data (which may be an arbitrarily complex and dependent process). Surprisingly, given access to such a simulator, we show that we can recover the same learning guarantees as in the classical setting with independent data, namely, error bounds that depend on the VC dimension. Further, we use this framework to study the power of conditional sampling and show strict statistical and computational advantages in this setting. As a highlight of our framework, we exhibit a single algorithm that simultaneously learns any given VC class under all processes samplable in bounded polynomial time, with regret controlled by the time-bounded Kolmogorov complexity of the process. This provides a significant conceptual broadening of the classical PAC model.

</details>


### [178] [Simplex-Constrained Sparse Bagging: Transitioning from Uniform Priors to Sparse Posteriors in Ensemble Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13589)
*Meher Sai Preetam,Meher Bhaskar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SCSB是一种基于概率单纯形的后训练压缩与概率校准框架，通过引入凹二次惩罚项解决L1-单纯形悖论，实现高达96%的集成压缩，同时提升校准性能与泛化精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准Bagging集成（如随机森林）对基学习器赋予均匀投票权重，忽略了各基学习器在不同局部区域的差异性能力，导致模型过度自信。现有方法缺乏对集成剪枝与概率校准的联合优化。

Method: 将集成剪枝与校准建模为概率单纯形上的联合优化问题，最小化袋外损失；针对L1范数在单纯形上恒为常数无法实现剪枝的"L1-单纯形悖论"，引入凹二次惩罚项诱导稀疏性。

Result: 实现高达96%的集成压缩，推理速度线性提升，期望校准误差降低，泛化精度保持或提升。

Conclusion: SCSB是一种模型无关的通用框架，能在保持泛化性能的同时显著压缩集成模型规模并改善概率校准，具有实际部署价值。

Abstract: We present Simplex-Constrained Sparse Bagging (SCSB), a mathematically rigorous framework for post-training compression and probability calibration of bootstrap-based bagging ensembles. Standard bagging ensembles (such as Random Forests, Bagged SVMs, and Bagged Neural Networks) assign uniform voting power to all constituent estimators. However, this naive uniform prior ignores the varying local competence of base estimators and contributes to model overconfidence. We formulate ensemble pruning and calibration as a joint optimization problem over the probability simplex by minimizing the Out-Of-Bag (OOB) loss. To induce sparsity, we address the theoretical "L1-simplex paradox" -- the mathematical reality that the L1 norm is constant on the simplex and fails to prune -- by introducing a concave quadratic penalty. SCSB is model-agnostic and achieves up to 96% ensemble compression, yielding linear inference speedups and superior probability calibration (lowered Expected Calibration Error) while preserving or enhancing generalization accuracy.

</details>


### [179] [Beyond the Commitment Boundary: Probing Epiphenomenal Chain-of-Thought in Large Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13603)
*Daniel Scalena,Sara Candussio,Luca Bortolussi,Elisabetta Fersini,Malvina Nissim,Gabriele Sarti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现Chain-of-Thought推理过程中存在一个"承诺边界"——从临时中间猜测到稳定高置信度答案的急剧转变点，该点通常出现在推理块的早期，之后的步骤对最终答案概率没有实质影响。利用注意力探针可以从中间步骤线性解码答案形成阶段，并通过提前退出推理块将CoT长度平均减少55%而对性能影响极小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前CoT推理是推理时扩展的主导范式，但单个推理步骤对最终答案的因果影响尚未被充分理解。作者旨在量化每个步骤的因果重要性，并探究答案在推理轨迹中是如何逐渐形成的。

Method: 通过早期退出（early exit）机制估计每一步的因果重要性；使用注意力探针（attention probes）从中间推理步骤线性解码答案形成阶段；利用承诺边界信号实现推理块的提前退出。

Result: 推理过程中存在一个"承诺边界"——通常在单步内完成从临时猜测到稳定答案的转变，且发生在推理块结束之前；边界之后的CoT步骤是"副现象性"的，不改变最终答案概率；注意力探针能以高精度和强泛化能力解码答案形成阶段；利用该信号可平均减少55%的CoT长度而不显著影响性能。

Conclusion: CoT推理中存在一个可检测的承诺边界，该边界之后的大部分推理步骤是冗余的。通过检测该边界实现提前退出，可以在保持性能的同时大幅减少推理成本，为高效推理提供了新思路。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning is the dominant paradigm for inference-time scaling in language models, yet the causal influence of individual steps on the final answer poorly understood. We estimate each step's causal importance via early exit and use this measure to study how answers form across the reasoning traces of several model families. Across diverse tasks, we find that reasoning typically crosses a \emph{commitment boundary} -- a sharp transition from transient intermediate guesses to a stable, high-confidence answer. This transition often happens in a single step, well before the model's reasoning block ends, and is followed by \emph{epiphenomenal} CoT steps that leave the final answer probability unaltered. Using attention probes, we show that answer-formation stages can be linearly decoded from intermediate reasoning steps with high accuracy and generalize robustly to unseen reasoning tasks. We exploit this signal to early-exit reasoning blocks at the commitment boundary, reducing the length of CoTs up to 55\% on average with negligible impact on model performance.

</details>


### [180] [The Stable Recovery Manifold: Geometric Principles Governing Recoverability in Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13637)
*Ayushman Trivedi,Bhavika Melwani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过分析恢复子空间维度（k_t）等几何指标，发现灾难性遗忘并非信息破坏，而是可访问性和流形对齐问题。遗忘的知识仍可通过紧凑的恢复流形解码，支持稳定恢复流形假设，反驳了恢复扩散假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战灾难性遗忘的传统观点（信息破坏），基于可访问性崩溃框架，从几何结构角度探究持续学习中知识恢复能力的本质。

Method: 在Split CIFAR-100上用ResNet-18进行十任务顺序训练，引入恢复子空间维度（k_t）作为度量指标，分析可恢复性、表征漂移和恢复复杂性，并构建几何模型解释可恢复性方差。

Result: 恢复维度保持稳定（平均k_t=8.0），主角度漂移与可恢复性强相关（r=-0.862），几何模型解释82.2%的可恢复性方差。表征存在显著漂移但恢复流形保持紧凑。

Conclusion: 灾难性遗忘主要是可访问性和流形对齐问题，而非知识破坏。遗忘的知识仍保持紧凑可解码性，支持稳定恢复流形假设。

Abstract: Catastrophic forgetting is often viewed as the destruction of previously learned knowledge during sequential learning. Building on the Accessibility Collapse framework, we investigate the geometric structure of recoverability in continual learning. Using Split CIFAR-100 and a sequentially trained ResNet-18, we analyze recoverability, representational drift, and recovery complexity across ten tasks. We introduce Recovery Subspace Dimensionality (k_t), a measure of the minimum number of singular directions required to preserve 90 percent of full probe performance. Contrary to our Recoverability Diffusion hypothesis, recovery dimensionality remains stable throughout training (mean k_t = 8.0) despite substantial representational drift. Principal-angle drift strongly predicts recoverability (r = -0.862), and a simple geometric model explains 82.2 percent of recoverability variance. These findings support the Stable Recovery Manifold hypothesis, suggesting that forgotten knowledge remains compactly decodable despite representational reorganization. The results indicate that catastrophic forgetting is primarily an accessibility and manifold-alignment problem rather than information destruction.

</details>


### [181] [Dense Supervision, Sparse Updates: On the Sparsity and Geometry of On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13657)
*Guo Yu,Wenlin Liu,Yulan Hu,Hao-Xuan Ma,Jun-Peng Jiang,Han-Jia Ye*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统分析了On-policy Distillation (OPD)在稀疏性和几何结构上的特性：OPD更新是稀疏的、主要集中在FFN层，且数值满秩但谱集中，更新主要落在源权重接近零的坐标上，表明OPD并非简单的密集参数重写，而是保留了在策略后训练的关键几何特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OPD作为一种结合在策略学生轨迹与密集教师监督的后训练方法效果显著，但其对模型参数的具体影响机制尚不清晰，作者希望从稀疏性和几何结构两个维度揭示OPD如何改变模型参数。

Method: 在多个语言模型和视觉-语言模型对上，从稀疏性（更新幅度、坐标稀疏性、层间分布、FFN集中性）和几何结构（数值秩、谱集中性、与源权重子空间的关系）两个角度对OPD更新进行实证分析，并通过子网训练和优化器消融等实验验证发现的实用性。

Result: 1) OPD更新幅度小且坐标稀疏，分布在各层且主要集中在FFN层；仅训练发现的子网络可接近完整OPD性能；但稀疏诱导的SGD优化器性能不如AdamW，因为密集教师监督保留了异质坐标梯度尺度。2) OPD更新数值满秩但谱集中，主要位于源权重主奇异子空间之外，且不成比例地落在源权重接近零的坐标上。

Conclusion: 密集教师监督并未将OPD转变为普通的密集参数重写，OPD保留了在策略后训练的重要几何特征。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (\textsc{OPD}) has recently become a prominent post-training recipe as it combines two desirable ingredients: on-policy student trajectories and dense teacher supervision, yet how this hybrid changes a model's parameters remains unclear. Across several language and vision-language model pairs and use cases, our analysis yields two main findings. On sparsity, \textsc{OPD}-style updates are small and coordinate-sparse. They are distributed across layers and are usually FFN-heavy. This sparse structure is operationally useful: training only the discovered subnetwork recovers nearly the same performance as full \textsc{OPD}. However, the sparsity-inducing SGD optimizer underperforms AdamW in our optimizer ablation, likely because dense teacher supervision preserves heterogeneous coordinate-wise gradient scales where AdamW's adaptive scaling remains useful. On geometry, the updates are numerically full-rank but spectrally concentrated; they lie mostly away from the principal singular subspaces of the source weights and fall disproportionately on coordinates where the source weights are close to zero. These findings suggest that dense teacher supervision does not turn \textsc{OPD} into ordinary dense parameter rewriting; instead, \textsc{OPD} retains important geometric signatures of on-policy post-training.

</details>


### [182] [Understanding Truncated Positional Encodings for Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13671)
*James Flora,Mitchell Black,Weng-Keen Wong,Amir Nayyeri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了图神经网络中常用的两类位置编码（谱编码和游走编码）在截断情况下的表达能力差异。理论证明截断后这些编码不再等价，截断谱编码不再强于1-WL测试，实验表明混合使用多种截断编码效果更好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实践中常用的截断位置编码（如前k个特征空间或邻接矩阵的幂）的理论性质尚不清楚，需要系统研究截断对表达能力的影响。

Method: 理论分析截断谱编码和游走编码的表达能力差异，引入k-调和距离这一新的谱编码族进行对比，并在真实数据集上进行实验验证。

Result: 截断后不同位置编码家族的表达能力存在根本性差异；截断谱编码不再强于1-WL测试；不同截断编码的表达能力各不相同；混合使用多种截断编码在实际任务中表现更优。

Conclusion: 截断打破了谱编码和游走编码的理论等价性，实践中应混合使用多种截断位置编码以获得更好的效果。

Abstract: Positional encodings (PEs) enhance the power of graph neural networks (GNNs), both theoretically and empirically. Two of the most popular families of PEs - spectral (e.g., Laplacian eigenspaces, effective resistance) and walk-based (polynomials of the adjacency matrix) - are theoretically equivalent in expressive power, with expressivity between the 1-WL and 3-WL tests. However, this equivalence assumes the GNN uses the "complete" version of these PEs, which requires $O(n^3)$ time and space complexity. Instead, practitioners commonly use truncated variants of these encodings, such as the first $k$ eigenspaces or powers of the adjacency matrix. However, the theoretical properties of these truncated PEs are unknown. In this work, we initiate the study of these truncated PEs. Theoretically, we show that, under truncation, several families of PEs are fundamentally different in expressive power. As a corollary, we show that truncated spectral PEs are no longer stronger than the 1-WL test. We also study a family of spectral PEs, the $k$-harmonic distances, to highlight the differences in expressive power of even closely related truncated PEs. Finally, we experimentally show that a mix of truncated PEs is preferable to any single family on real-world datasets.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [183] [Characterizing Tests in IoT Software: Practices, Challenges and Opportunities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12592)
*Rufeng Chen,Hengcheng Zhu,Wuqi Zhang,Zixu Zhou,Lili Wei*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 这是首个关于开源物联网软件测试用例的实证研究，发现尽管物联网软件包含大量测试，但其有效性有限，主要挑战在于管理复杂的外部依赖交互，而模拟对象的使用与这些挑战高度契合，有望提升测试覆盖率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物联网软件中的缺陷可能导致严重后果（如智能锁被非法入侵），但物联网软件的测试实践尚不明确，因此需要系统性地研究其测试现状、有效性和挑战。

Method: 对开源物联网软件的测试用例进行首次实证研究，评估测试用例的有效性，探索测试挑战，并分析模拟对象的使用模式。

Result: 物联网软件虽包含大量测试，但有效性有限；主要测试挑战是管理复杂的外部依赖交互（网络组件、文件系统、操作系统、数据库）；模拟对象的使用与这些挑战高度一致。

Conclusion: 模拟对象是应对物联网软件测试复杂性的有前景的解决方案，能够提升测试覆盖率。

Abstract: The Internet of Things (IoT) is experiencing rapid growth. Smart devices are emerging in smart homes and industrial applications, performing mission-critical tasks. Bugs in IoT software can lead to severe consequences. For example, a buggy smart lock can allow unauthorized access to a private property. Testing is a primary practice to expose software bugs and ensure software quality. However, little is known about how IoT software is tested. To bridge this gap, we conducted the first empirical study on test cases in open-source IoT software. Specifically, we evaluated the effectiveness of test cases in IoT software, explored the challenges inherent in testing IoT software, and analyzed the usage of mock objects. Our results indicate that while IoT software often contains a considerable number of tests, their effectiveness remains limited. We identified the primary challenges in testing IoT software as managing complex interactions with various external dependencies, such as other network-reliant IoT components, file systems, operating systems, and databases. We also observed that the use of mock objects in IoT software closely aligns with our identified testing challenges. This alignment demonstrates the potential of mocking as a solution to enhance test coverage and address the complexities of IoT software testing.

</details>


### [184] [HybridCodeAuthorship: A Benchmark Dataset for Line-Level Code Authorship Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12620)
*Luke Patterson,Li Wang,Adam Faulkner*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了HybridCodeAuthorship基准数据集，用于检测AI和人类混合编写的代码。该数据集基于CodeSearchNet构建，包含交错AI/人类代码行的Python文件。实验表明，现有最优算法（AIGCode Detector）在此任务上F1分数仅为0.48（块级）和0.56（行级），表明该基准具有较大挑战性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI代码检测基准主要包含纯学术风格的LeetCode问题，且假设代码片段完全由人类或完全由AI编写，无法反映工业代码库中交错使用AI代码助手的真实场景。需要构建更贴近实际应用的基准来评估检测算法性能。

Method: 利用CodeSearchNet中GitHub开源仓库链接构建数据集，生成Python文件中人类与AI代码行交错混合的代码样本。采用两种最先进的AI生成代码检测算法，在行级和块级两个粒度上进行性能基准测试。

Result: AIGCode Detector在块级检测任务上最高F1为0.48，行级检测任务上最高F1为0.56，表明HybridCodeAuthorship是一个具有挑战性的基准。

Conclusion: 现有AI代码检测算法在处理真实工业场景中人类-AI混合编写代码时性能有限，需要进一步发展针对交错混合代码的检测方法。

Abstract: Thanks to the rapid adoption of AI code assistants powered by large language models (LLMs), industry codebases are, increasingly, a hybrid of AI- and human-authored code. For risk management and productivity analysis purposes, it is crucial to enable fine-grained location detection of AI-generated code. To develop algorithms for this task, quality benchmarks are needed to assess performance. However, existing benchmarks tend to comprise academic, LeetCode-style problems and presume a code snippet is either completely human-authored or completely AI-authored, which is not reflective of the diverse intents and styles of industry codebases utilizing AI code assistants. To fill these gaps, we introduce HybridCodeAuthorship, a novel benchmark of Python code files with interleaved human- and AI-authored lines of code to simulate authentic utilization of AI code assistants. In this paper, we first present our dataset construction pipeline, which leverages CodeSearchNet, a massive collection of links to open sourced repositories on GitHub. We then benchmark the performance of two state-of-the-art AI-generated code detection algorithms at both the line- and chunk-level. Experimental results demonstrate that HybridCodeAuthorship is a challenging benchmark with a top-scoring algorithm, AIGCode Detector, obtaining a highest F1 score of 0.48 and 0.56 on chunk-level and line-level code detection tasks, respectively.

</details>


### [185] [Beyond Problem Solving: UOJ-Bench for Evaluating Code Generation, Hacking, and Repair in Competitive Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12864)
*Tingqiang Xu,Hangrui Zhou,Tianle Cai,Alex Gu,Kaifeng Lyu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了UOJ-Bench基准测试，用于评估LLM在编程学习中识别代码错误的能力。结果表明，即使最强模型在单次评估中识别错误率不足50%，测试时缩放虽可提升至90%以上但计算成本过高。前沿LLM在约30个问题上能发现超5%满分提交中的错误，可补充传统评测系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在竞争性编程中表现优异，但其在支持人类学习（尤其是识别代码错误这一关键教育活动）中的作用尚缺乏系统评估。

Method: 构建UOJ-Bench基准，包含代码生成、代码黑客（找错）和代码修复三个任务，数据来自UOJ真实提交，使用UOJ原生评测系统进行评估。

Result: 单次评估下最强模型无法识别超过50%的错误提交；测试时缩放可将成功率提升至90%以上，但计算成本高；前沿LLM可发现超过5%满分提交中的隐藏错误。

Conclusion: 当前LLM在错误识别方面仍有局限，但可作为传统评测系统的补充工具，测试时缩放有效但成本制约其大规模部署。

Abstract: Despite strong performance in competitive programming, the role of Large Language Models (LLMs) in supporting human learning in the same setting remains largely unexplored. In this work, we introduce UOJ-Bench, a benchmark designed to evaluate not only the problem-solving ability of LLMs, but also their ability to identify errors in human-written code -- a crucial educational activity traditionally supported by running test cases over online judge systems. UOJ-Bench consists of three distinct tasks: code generation, code hacking, and code repair, all constructed from real-world code submissions on the Universal Online Judge (UOJ) and evaluated through UOJ's native judging infrastructure. Our results show that under one-shot evaluation, even the strongest models fail to identify errors in more than 50% of a set of submissions that have been found to be incorrect by UOJ users. While test-time scaling improves success rates to above 90%, the substantial computational costs incurred from model inference limit its practicality for large-scale deployment. Despite these limitations, we find that the best-performing models under test-time scaling can uncover errors in over 5% of full-score submissions across roughly 30 problems, suggesting that frontier LLMs can already provide complementary signals beyond standard judging systems.

</details>


### [186] [The Rise of AI-Native Software Engineering: Implications for Practice, Education, and the Future Workforce](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12986)
*Mamdouh Alenezi*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文系统综述了48篇经同行评审的文献（2016-2026），提出GenAI、LLM和Agentic AI正深刻变革软件工程。通过四代理研究流程，围绕实践、教育和劳动力三条轨迹综合九大主题，贡献包括：AI原生软件工程概念框架（意图、协作、验证）、九维度能力模型、四阶段大学课程路线图、教员发展与劳动力转型策略，以及11项研究空白议程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GenAI和LLM对软件工程产生前所未有的颠覆性影响，但缺乏系统性综述来理解这一变革的全貌，特别是对开发实践、人才培养和劳动力市场的综合影响。

Method: 采用四代理研究工作流（文献发现、科学计量分析、课程转型、劳动力影响），从软件工程、机器学习、计算教育、人-AI协作和软件生产力等顶级场所筛选并分析48篇经核实的高影响力同行评审文献（2016-2026）。

Result: 发现2022年底后LLM-for-SE年产出增长约五倍；提出九大主题和实践、教育、劳动力三条轨迹的综合证据；发现关于生产力效应的证据内部不一致，表明收益高度依赖上下文。

Conclusion: AI原生时代的核心挑战在于培养工程师的判断力、验证能力和编排能力，而非仅关注代码生产；提出了概念框架、能力模型、课程路线图和研究议程等系统性贡献。

Abstract: Generative Artificial Intelligence (GenAI), Large Language Models (LLMs), and emerging Agentic AI constitute the most disruptive transformation in the history of software engineering (SE), reshaping development processes, required competencies, professional roles, and the educational outcomes that universities must deliver. This paper presents a systematic review of 48 verified, influential peer-reviewed publications (2016--2026) drawn from leading venues in software engineering, machine learning, computing education, human--AI collaboration, and software productivity. Studies were discovered, screened, and analyzed through a four-agent research workflow (Literature Discovery, Scientometric Analysis, Curriculum Transformation, and Workforce Impact) and were verified against primary sources. We synthesize the evidence along nine themes and three trajectories -- practice, education, and workforce -- and report a scientometric inflection in which annual LLM-for-SE output grew roughly five-fold after late 2022.
